<script type="module">
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { EffectComposer } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/EffectComposer.js';
import { RenderPass } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/RenderPass.js';
import { ShaderPass } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/ShaderPass.js';
import { UnrealBloomPass } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/UnrealBloomPass.js';
import { OutputPass } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/OutputPass.js';
import * as BufferGeometryUtils from 'three/addons/utils/BufferGeometryUtils.js';

// ============================================================ core / utils
const elGet = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
const clamp = (v, a, b) => v < a ? a : (v > b ? b : v);
const lerp = (a, b, t) => a + (b - a) * t;
const damp = (a, b, l, dt) => lerp(a, b, 1 - Math.exp(-l * dt));
const rnd = (a = 1, b) => b === undefined ? Math.random() * a : a + Math.random() * (b - a);
const rndi = (a, b) => Math.floor(rnd(a, b + 1));
const pick = (arr) => arr[(Math.random() * arr.length) | 0];
const wrapAngle = (a) => { while (a > Math.PI) a -= Math.PI * 2; while (a < -Math.PI) a += Math.PI * 2; return a; };
const angleDamp = (a, b, l, dt) => a + wrapAngle(b - a) * (1 - Math.exp(-l * dt));
const sstep = (a, b, x) => { const t = clamp((x - a) / (b - a), 0, 1); return t * t * (3 - 2 * t); };

const _v1 = new THREE.Vector3(), _v2 = new THREE.Vector3(), _v3 = new THREE.Vector3();
const _v4 = new THREE.Vector3(), _v5 = new THREE.Vector3(), _v6 = new THREE.Vector3();
const _q1 = new THREE.Quaternion(), _q2 = new THREE.Quaternion();
const _m1 = new THREE.Matrix4(), _m2 = new THREE.Matrix4();
const _c1 = new THREE.Color(), _c2 = new THREE.Color();
const UP = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0);

const CONFIG = {
  sunDir: new THREE.Vector3(-0.42, 0.20, 0.88).normalize(),  // scene -> sun (low over the SW ocean)
  sunColor: new THREE.Color(1.0, 0.62, 0.34),
  hemiSky: new THREE.Color(0.42, 0.32, 0.62),
  hemiGround: new THREE.Color(0.30, 0.18, 0.26),
  fogColor: new THREE.Color(0.30, 0.16, 0.27),
  fogDensity: 0.00085,
  waterY: -1.2,
};

const Tick = { t: 0, dt: 0, rawDt: 0, scale: 1, targetScale: 1, frame: 0 };

// ============================================================ renderer / scene
const canvas = elGet('gl');
const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas, antialias: false, powerPreference: 'high-performance', stencil: false });
const BASE_PR = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 1.5);
renderer.setPixelRatio(BASE_PR);
renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight, false);
renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.12;
renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
canvas.addEventListener('webglcontextlost', (e) => e.preventDefault(), false);

const scene = new THREE.Scene();
scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(CONFIG.fogColor.getHex(), CONFIG.fogDensity);

const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(62, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 4200);
camera.position.set(140, 14, 290);

// ============================================================ post processing
const GradeShader = {
  uniforms: {
    tDiffuse: { value: null },
    uTime: { value: 0 },
    uVig: { value: 1.05 },
    uFlash: { value: 0 },
    uDamage: { value: 0 },
  },
  vertexShader: /* glsl */`
    varying vec2 vUv;
    void main() { vUv = uv; gl_Position = projectionMatrix * modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0); }`,
  fragmentShader: /* glsl */`
    uniform sampler2D tDiffuse;
    uniform float uTime, uVig, uFlash, uDamage;
    varying vec2 vUv;
    float hash(vec2 p) { return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453); }
    void main() {
      vec4 c = texture2D(tDiffuse, vUv);
      float l = dot(c.rgb, vec3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
      c.rgb += (1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.55, l)) * vec3(-0.010, 0.006, 0.050);   // teal-purple shadows
      c.rgb *= vec3(1.05, 0.995, 0.945);                                        // warm key cast
      vec2 q = vUv - 0.5;
      c.rgb *= 1.0 - dot(q, q) * uVig;                                          // vignette
      float d = uDamage * smoothstep(0.06, 0.55, dot(q, q));
      c.rgb = mix(c.rgb, vec3(0.62, 0.04, 0.07), clamp(d, 0.0, 0.85));          // damage ring
      c.rgb = mix(c.rgb, vec3(3.2, 3.1, 3.0), clamp(uFlash, 0.0, 1.0));         // white-out
      c.rgb += (hash(vUv * 1731.7 + fract(uTime) * 91.3) - 0.5) * 0.014;        // grain
      gl_FragColor = c;
    }`,
};

const composerTarget = new THREE.WebGLRenderTarget(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight, {
  type: THREE.HalfFloatType, samples: 4,
});
const composer = new EffectComposer(renderer, composerTarget);
composer.setPixelRatio(BASE_PR);
composer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
const renderPass = new RenderPass(scene, camera);
const bloomPass = new UnrealBloomPass(new THREE.Vector2(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight), 0.55, 0.4, 0.85);
const gradePass = new ShaderPass(GradeShader);
const outputPass = new OutputPass();
composer.addPass(renderPass);
composer.addPass(bloomPass);
composer.addPass(gradePass);
composer.addPass(outputPass);

window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
  camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
  camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight, false);
  composer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
});

// ============================================================ dynamic quality scaling
const Quality = {
  scale: 1, ema: 14, busyEma: 8, cool: 0, shadowTier: 0,
  // rawMs = rAF delta (vsync-floored); busyMs = actual main-thread frame cost.
  // busyEma keeps the upscale path reachable on 60Hz displays where rawMs never drops below ~16.7.
  update(rawMs, busyMs, dt) {
    this.ema = lerp(this.ema, Math.min(rawMs, 80), 0.04);
    this.busyEma = lerp(this.busyEma, Math.min(busyMs, 80), 0.04);
    this.cool -= dt;
    if (this.cool > 0) return;
    if (this.ema > 17.6 && this.scale > 0.55) {
      this.scale = Math.max(0.55, this.scale - 0.1);
      this.apply(); this.cool = 1.6;
    } else if (this.scale < 1 && (this.ema < 12.8 || (this.ema < 17.2 && this.busyEma < 10))) {
      this.scale = Math.min(1, this.scale + 0.05);
      this.apply(); this.cool = 2.8;
    }
  },
  apply() {
    const pr = BASE_PR * this.scale;
    renderer.setPixelRatio(pr);
    composer.setPixelRatio(pr);
    const wantTier = this.scale < 0.75 ? 1 : 0;
    if (wantTier !== this.shadowTier) {
      this.shadowTier = wantTier;
      const size = wantTier ? 1024 : 2048;
      const light = scene.userData.sunLight;
      if (light) {
        light.shadow.mapSize.set(size, size);
        if (light.shadow.map) { light.shadow.map.dispose(); light.shadow.map = null; }
      }
    }
  },
};

// ============================================================ input
const Input = {
  keys: Object.create(null),
  pressed: Object.create(null),
  lookX: 0, lookY: 0, wheel: 0,
  lmb: false, rmb: false, lmbPressed: false,
  locked: false, enabled: false,
  endFrame() {
    for (const k in this.pressed) this.pressed[k] = false;
    this.lmbPressed = false;
    this.lookX = 0; this.lookY = 0; this.wheel = 0;
  },
  lock() {
    if (this.locked) return;
    const p = canvas.requestPointerLock({ unadjustedMovement: true });
    if (p && p.catch) p.catch(() => { try { canvas.requestPointerLock(); } catch (e) {} });
  },
};
window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
  if (e.code === 'Space' || e.code === 'Tab') e.preventDefault();
  if (!e.repeat) Input.pressed[e.code] = true;
  Input.keys[e.code] = true;
});
window.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => { Input.keys[e.code] = false; });
window.addEventListener('blur', () => { Input.keys = Object.create(null); Input.lmb = Input.rmb = false; });
document.addEventListener('pointerlockchange', () => { Input.locked = document.pointerLockElement === canvas; });
document.addEventListener('pointerlockerror', () => {});
window.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
  if (!Input.locked) return;
  const mx = clamp(e.movementX, -180, 180), my = clamp(e.movementY, -180, 180);
  Input.lookX += mx; Input.lookY += my;
});
window.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
  if (!Input.enabled) return;
  // a click that re-acquires pointer lock must not fire the weapon
  if (e.button === 0) { if (Input.locked) { Input.lmb = true; Input.lmbPressed = true; } Input.lock(); }
  if (e.button === 2) Input.rmb = true;
});
window.addEventListener('mouseup', (e) => {
  if (e.button === 0) Input.lmb = false;
  if (e.button === 2) Input.rmb = false;
});
window.addEventListener('wheel', (e) => { Input.wheel += Math.sign(e.deltaY); }, { passive: true });
window.addEventListener('contextmenu', (e) => e.preventDefault());

// ============================================================ camera shake
const Shake = {
  trauma: 0,
  p1: rnd(100), p2: rnd(100), p3: rnd(100), p4: rnd(100),
  add(t) { this.trauma = Math.min(1.4, this.trauma + t); },
  update(dt) { this.trauma = Math.max(0, this.trauma - dt * 1.7); },
  // writes positional + rotational offset into out vectors
  sample(outPos, outRot) {
    const s = this.trauma * this.trauma;
    const t = Tick.t;
    outPos.set(
      (Math.sin(t * 31.3 + this.p1) + Math.sin(t * 17.7 + this.p2) * 0.6) * 0.16 * s,
      (Math.sin(t * 27.9 + this.p2) + Math.sin(t * 13.1 + this.p3) * 0.6) * 0.13 * s,
      0);
    outRot.set(
      Math.sin(t * 23.7 + this.p3) * 0.012 * s,
      Math.sin(t * 29.1 + this.p4) * 0.012 * s,
      Math.sin(t * 19.3 + this.p1) * 0.020 * s);
  },
};

// ============================================================ slow motion
const SlowMo = {
  timer: 0,
  trigger(scale, dur) { Tick.targetScale = scale; this.timer = dur; },
  update(rawDt) {
    if (this.timer > 0) { this.timer -= rawDt; if (this.timer <= 0) Tick.targetScale = 1; }
    Tick.scale = damp(Tick.scale, Tick.targetScale, Tick.targetScale < Tick.scale ? 18 : 3.2, rawDt);
  },
};
// ============================================================ procedural textures
function makeCanvas(w, h) {
  const cv = document.createElement('canvas');
  cv.width = w; cv.height = h;
  return [cv, cv.getContext('2d')];
}
function canvasTex(cv, opts = {}) {
  const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(cv);
  t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  t.wrapS = t.wrapT = opts.repeat ? THREE.RepeatWrapping : THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
  t.anisotropy = opts.aniso ? Math.min(8, renderer.capabilities.getMaxAnisotropy()) : 1;
  return t;
}
function noisePatch(ctx, x, y, w, h, n, alpha) {
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const g = rndi(0, 50);
    ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${g},${g},${g + rndi(0, 14)},${alpha})`;
    ctx.fillRect(x + rnd(w), y + rnd(h), rnd(1, 3.4), rnd(1, 3.4));
  }
}

const TEX = {};
{
  // ---- building facades (tower + lowrise variants, with matching emissive maps)
  function facade(wd, ht, floors, cols, palette, store) {
    const [cv, ctx] = makeCanvas(wd, ht);
    const [ecv, ectx] = makeCanvas(wd, ht);
    ctx.fillStyle = palette.wall; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, wd, ht);
    ectx.fillStyle = '#000'; ectx.fillRect(0, 0, wd, ht);
    // subtle vertical pilasters
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.22)';
    for (let c = 0; c <= cols; c++) ctx.fillRect((c / cols) * wd - 1, 0, 2, ht);
    const top = 22, bot = store ? ht * 0.24 : 8;
    const gw = wd / cols, gh = (ht - top - bot) / floors;
    for (let r = 0; r < floors; r++) {
      for (let c = 0; c < cols; c++) {
        const x = c * gw + gw * 0.18, y = top + r * gh + gh * 0.2;
        const w = gw * 0.64, h = gh * 0.58;
        const roll = Math.random();
        if (roll < palette.lit) {
          const warm = Math.random() < 0.72;
          const col = warm ? `rgb(${rndi(235, 255)},${rndi(168, 205)},${rndi(98, 135)})` : `rgb(${rndi(130, 165)},${rndi(185, 215)},${rndi(235, 255)})`;
          ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
          ectx.fillStyle = col; ectx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
        } else {
          const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, y, 0, y + h);
          g.addColorStop(0, '#4d4366'); g.addColorStop(1, '#191b29');
          ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
        }
      }
    }
    if (store) {
      // glowing storefront band at street level
      const sy = ht - bot + 6;
      const hue = pick(['255,110,160', '90,220,235', '255,170,90', '170,120,255']);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#0d0e16'; ctx.fillRect(0, ht - bot, wd, bot);
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${hue},0.92)`; ctx.fillRect(4, sy, wd - 8, 7);
      ectx.fillStyle = `rgba(${hue},0.92)`; ectx.fillRect(4, sy, wd - 8, 7);
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,236,210,0.5)';
      for (let c = 0; c < cols; c++) { ctx.fillRect(c * gw + 5, sy + 14, gw - 10, bot - 26); }
      ectx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,214,170,0.4)';
      for (let c = 0; c < cols; c++) { ectx.fillRect(c * gw + 5, sy + 14, gw - 10, bot - 26); }
    }
    noisePatch(ctx, 0, 0, wd, ht, 700, 0.05);
    // reserved dark roof patch (top-left 18px) for box-top UVs
    ctx.fillStyle = '#23222b'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 18, 18);
    noisePatch(ctx, 0, 0, 18, 18, 30, 0.2);
    ectx.fillStyle = '#000'; ectx.fillRect(0, 0, 18, 18);
    return [canvasTex(cv), canvasTex(ecv)];
  }
  TEX.tower = [
    facade(256, 512, 17, 7, { wall: '#272b3d', lit: 0.46 }, false),
    facade(256, 512, 15, 6, { wall: '#332d44', lit: 0.40 }, false),
    facade(256, 512, 19, 9, { wall: '#1f2433', lit: 0.52 }, false),
  ];
  TEX.lowrise = [
    facade(256, 256, 3, 5, { wall: '#473a4d', lit: 0.38 }, true),
    facade(256, 256, 3, 6, { wall: '#3a4152', lit: 0.34 }, true),
    facade(256, 256, 2, 4, { wall: '#52404a', lit: 0.42 }, true),
  ];

  // ---- road straight (runs along V) : 13m wide profile
  {
    const [cv, ctx] = makeCanvas(256, 256);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#14151c'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 256, 256);
    noisePatch(ctx, 0, 0, 256, 256, 2400, 0.08);
    // darker wheel tracks
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.18)';
    for (const cx of [52, 96, 160, 204]) ctx.fillRect(cx - 9, 0, 18, 256);
    // double yellow center

    ctx.fillRect(124, 0, 3, 256); ctx.fillRect(130, 0, 3, 256);
    // white lane dashes
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(205,205,210,0.75)';
    for (let y = 0; y < 256; y += 64) { ctx.fillRect(73, y, 4, 30); ctx.fillRect(180, y, 4, 30); }
    // edge lines
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(200,200,205,0.6)';
    ctx.fillRect(8, 0, 4, 256); ctx.fillRect(244, 0, 4, 256);
    TEX.road = canvasTex(cv, { repeat: true, aniso: true });
  }
  // ---- intersection
  {
    const [cv, ctx] = makeCanvas(256, 256);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#14151c'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 256, 256);
    noisePatch(ctx, 0, 0, 256, 256, 2400, 0.08);
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(205,205,210,0.62)';
    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
      const o = 26 + i * 30;
      ctx.fillRect(o, 6, 18, 26); ctx.fillRect(o, 224, 18, 26);     // crosswalks N/S
      ctx.fillRect(6, o, 26, 18); ctx.fillRect(224, o, 26, 18);     // crosswalks E/W

    TEX.intersection = canvasTex(cv, { aniso: true });
  }
  // ---- sidewalk
  {
    const [cv, ctx] = makeCanvas(128, 128);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#3d3a45'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    noisePatch(ctx, 0, 0, 128, 128, 900, 0.07);
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    for (let i = 0; i <= 128; i += 32) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(i, 0); ctx.lineTo(i, 128); ctx.stroke(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, i); ctx.lineTo(128, i); ctx.stroke(); }
    TEX.sidewalk = canvasTex(cv, { repeat: true, aniso: true });
  }
  // ---- sand
  {
    const [cv, ctx] = makeCanvas(128, 128);

    for (let i = 0; i < 1600; i++) {
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${rndi(120, 215)},${rndi(95, 165)},${rndi(60, 110)},0.16)`;

  }
  // ---- wood planks (pier)
  {
    const [cv, ctx] = makeCanvas(128, 128);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#56402e'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    for (let x = 0; x < 128; x += 16) {
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${rndi(70, 96)},${rndi(50, 68)},${rndi(34, 48)})`;

    }
    for (let i = 0; i < 300; i++) { ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.12)'; ctx.fillRect(rnd(128), rnd(128), rnd(1, 8), 1); }
    TEX.planks = canvasTex(cv, { repeat: true });
  }
  // ---- shipping container (grayscale; tinted per-instance)
  {
    const [cv, ctx] = makeCanvas(128, 128);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#9a9a9a'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    for (let x = 0; x < 128; x += 8) {
      ctx.fillStyle = (x / 8) % 2 ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.13)' : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.22)';
      ctx.fillRect(x, 0, 4, 128);
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.4)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 6, 128, 3); ctx.fillRect(0, 119, 128, 3);
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)'; ctx.fillRect(8, 52, 42, 16);
    noisePatch(ctx, 0, 0, 128, 128, 600, 0.1);
    TEX.container = canvasTex(cv);
  }
  // ---- warehouse wall
  {
    const [cv, ctx] = makeCanvas(128, 128);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#4a4550'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    for (let y = 0; y < 128; y += 10) { ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.25)'; ctx.fillRect(0, y, 128, 2); }
    ctx.fillStyle = '#2c2933'; ctx.fillRect(26, 58, 34, 70); ctx.fillRect(78, 58, 34, 70);
    noisePatch(ctx, 0, 0, 128, 128, 500, 0.1);
    TEX.warehouse = canvasTex(cv, { repeat: true });
  }
  // ---- soft round particle
  {
    const [cv, ctx] = makeCanvas(64, 64);
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(32, 32, 0, 32, 32, 32);
    g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,1)'); g.addColorStop(0.35, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.55)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 64, 64);
    TEX.dot = canvasTex(cv);
  }
  // ---- smoke puff (lumpy blob)
  {
    const [cv, ctx] = makeCanvas(128, 128);
    for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) {
      const x = 64 + rnd(-26, 26), y = 64 + rnd(-26, 26), r = rnd(14, 34);
      const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(x, y, 0, x, y, r);
      g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.16)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
      ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, 7); ctx.fill();
    }
    TEX.smoke = canvasTex(cv);
  }
  // ---- light streak (wet-road reflection: bright at top, fading down)
  {
    const [cv, ctx] = makeCanvas(64, 256);
    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 256);
    g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.95)'); g.addColorStop(0.25, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.4)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 64, 256);
    const m = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 64, 0);
    m.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,1)'); m.addColorStop(0.3, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)'); m.addColorStop(0.7, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)'); m.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,1)');
    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
    ctx.fillStyle = m; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 64, 256);
    TEX.streak = canvasTex(cv);
  }
  // ---- palm frond (alpha)
  {
    const [cv, ctx] = makeCanvas(64, 256);
    ctx.translate(32, 0);
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#274a22'; ctx.lineWidth = 5;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, 0); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(6, 128, 2, 250); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.lineWidth = 2.4;
    for (let i = 0; i < 22; i++) {
      const t = 0.08 + (i / 22) * 0.88, y = t * 250, s = (i % 2 ? 1 : -1);
      const len = 26 * (1 - Math.abs(t - 0.45));
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgb(${rndi(38, 64)},${rndi(86, 120)},${rndi(40, 60)})`;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(t * 6, y);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(s * len * 0.7, y + 8, s * len, y + 22);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    TEX.frond = canvasTex(cv);
  }
  // ---- pedestrian impostor atlas (4 silhouettes)
  {
    const [cv, ctx] = makeCanvas(128, 64);
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      const x0 = i * 32 + 16;
      const hue = pick(['#3c3550', '#50343c', '#34474e', '#4a4434']);
      const skin = pick(['#c79468', '#8a6244', '#e0b48c', '#6b4a32']);
      ctx.fillStyle = hue;
      ctx.fillRect(x0 - 5, 22, 10, 22);                       // torso
      ctx.fillStyle = '#22202c';
      ctx.fillRect(x0 - 4, 44, 3.4, 18); ctx.fillRect(x0 + 0.6, 44, 3.4, 18); // legs
      ctx.fillStyle = skin;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x0, 14, 5.4, 0, 7); ctx.fill();  // head
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,200,150,0.25)';
      ctx.fillRect(x0 + 3, 22, 2, 22);                          // sun rim
    }
    TEX.peopleAtlas = canvasTex(cv);
  }
  // ---- scorch decal
  {
    const [cv, ctx] = makeCanvas(64, 64);
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(32, 32, 2, 32, 32, 32);
    g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(8,6,6,0.92)'); g.addColorStop(0.55, 'rgba(10,8,8,0.6)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 64, 64);
    TEX.scorch = canvasTex(cv);
  }
  // ---- neon signs
  function neonSign(text, color, sub) {
    const wd = 512, ht = 128;
    const [cv, ctx] = makeCanvas(wd, ht);
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,8,14,0.92)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, wd, ht);
    ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.lineWidth = 5; ctx.shadowColor = color; ctx.shadowBlur = 18;
    ctx.strokeRect(10, 10, wd - 20, ht - 20);
    ctx.font = `800 ${sub ? 56 : 68}px Arial, sans-serif`;
    ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    ctx.shadowBlur = 26; ctx.shadowColor = color;
    ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.fillText(text, wd / 2, ht / 2 - (sub ? 10 : 0));
    ctx.shadowBlur = 10; ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.85; ctx.fillText(text, wd / 2, ht / 2 - (sub ? 10 : 0)); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    if (sub) {
      ctx.font = '600 26px Arial, sans-serif';
      ctx.shadowBlur = 14; ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.fillText(sub, wd / 2, ht - 30);
    }
    return canvasTex(cv);
  }
  TEX.signs = [
    { t: neonSign('MIRAVELLE', '#ff5fa2', 'COCKTAILS'), c: 0xff5fa2 },
    { t: neonSign('CLUB LUNA', '#5fd7ff'), c: 0x5fd7ff },
    { t: neonSign('LAGUNA', '#ffb45f', 'MOTEL'), c: 0xffb45f },
    { t: neonSign('NOCTURNE', '#b86bff'), c: 0xb86bff },
    { t: neonSign('ASTRA', '#7dff9e', 'ARCADE'), c: 0x7dff9e },
    { t: neonSign('VESPERA', '#ff7a5f', 'LOUNGE'), c: 0xff7a5f },
    { t: neonSign('PALMA', '#5fffd7'), c: 0x5fffd7 },
    { t: neonSign('NEON TIDE', '#ff5f7a', 'HOTEL'), c: 0xff5f7a },
  ];
  TEX.hospitalSign = (() => {
    const [cv, ctx] = makeCanvas(256, 128);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#101418'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 256, 128);
    ctx.shadowColor = '#ff4d5e'; ctx.shadowBlur = 22; ctx.fillStyle = '#ff4d5e';
    ctx.fillRect(20, 26, 76, 76);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff'; ctx.shadowColor = '#ffffff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 12;
    ctx.fillRect(48, 40, 20, 48); ctx.fillRect(34, 54, 48, 20);
    ctx.font = '800 38px Arial, sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    ctx.shadowColor = '#9fd9ff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 16; ctx.fillStyle = '#cfeaff';
    ctx.fillText('HOSPITAL', 112, 64);
    return canvasTex(cv);
  })();
}
// ============================================================ sky, sun, environment, ocean, ambient air traffic
const SKY_SHADER = {
  vertex: /* glsl */`
    varying vec3 vDir;
    void main() {
      vDir = position;
      vec4 mv = modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
      gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mv;
    }`,
  fragment: /* glsl */`
    uniform vec3 uSunDir;
    uniform float uTime;
    varying vec3 vDir;
    float hash21(vec2 p) { return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453); }
    float vnoise(vec2 p) {
      vec2 i = floor(p), f = fract(p);
      f = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);
      return mix(mix(hash21(i), hash21(i + vec2(1, 0)), f.x), mix(hash21(i + vec2(0, 1)), hash21(i + vec2(1, 1)), f.x), f.y);
    }
    void main() {
      vec3 d = normalize(vDir);
      float h = clamp(d.y, -0.05, 1.0);
      float sunAmt = max(dot(d, uSunDir), 0.0);
      vec3 zen = vec3(0.085, 0.05, 0.20);
      vec3 mid = vec3(0.46, 0.13, 0.36);
      vec3 hor = vec3(1.02, 0.40, 0.16);
      vec3 col = mix(hor, mid, smoothstep(0.0, 0.16, h));
      col = mix(col, zen, smoothstep(0.10, 0.52, h));
      col += vec3(0.6, 0.26, 0.05) * pow(sunAmt, 3.0) * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.5, h));
      col += vec3(1.0, 0.55, 0.25) * pow(sunAmt, 22.0) * 0.6;
      float disc = smoothstep(0.99935, 0.99965, sunAmt);
      col += vec3(1.7, 0.95, 0.5) * disc * 8.0;
      // thin streaky sunset clouds
      vec2 cp = vec2(atan(d.x, d.z) * 2.2, d.y * 9.0);
      float cl = vnoise(cp * 3.0 + vec2(uTime * 0.004, 0.0)) * 0.6 + vnoise(cp * 7.0) * 0.4;
      float band = smoothstep(0.54, 0.8, cl) * smoothstep(0.5, 0.22, h) * smoothstep(0.015, 0.08, h);
      vec3 cloudCol = mix(vec3(0.34, 0.16, 0.34), vec3(1.25, 0.62, 0.32), pow(sunAmt, 2.4));
      col = mix(col, cloudCol, band * 0.5);
      // faint early stars near zenith
      float st = step(0.9992, hash21(floor(d.xz / max(d.y, 0.3) * 240.0)));
      col += vec3(0.8, 0.85, 1.0) * st * smoothstep(0.38, 0.7, h) * (0.35 + 0.3 * sin(uTime * 2.0 + d.x * 40.0));
      gl_FragColor = vec4(col, 1.0);
    }`,
};

const Sky = {
  mat: null, sun: null, birds: null, flocks: [], plane: null, planeT: 0,
  init() {
    this.mat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      uniforms: { uSunDir: { value: CONFIG.sunDir }, uTime: { value: 0 } },
      vertexShader: SKY_SHADER.vertex, fragmentShader: SKY_SHADER.fragment,
      side: THREE.BackSide, depthWrite: false, fog: false,
    });
    const dome = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(3600, 36, 18), this.mat);
    dome.frustumCulled = false;
    scene.add(dome);

    // environment map (PMREM of the sky) for PBR reflections — wet roads, car paint
    const envScene = new THREE.Scene();
    const envDome = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(40, 24, 12), this.mat);
    envScene.add(envDome);
    const pmrem = new THREE.PMREMGenerator(renderer);
    const envRT = pmrem.fromScene(envScene, 0.04, 1, 120);
    scene.environment = envRT.texture;
    pmrem.dispose();

    // sun light
    const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(CONFIG.sunColor, 2.7);
    sun.castShadow = true;
    sun.shadow.mapSize.set(2048, 2048);
    sun.shadow.camera.left = -100; sun.shadow.camera.right = 100;
    sun.shadow.camera.top = 100; sun.shadow.camera.bottom = -100;
    sun.shadow.camera.near = 10; sun.shadow.camera.far = 620;
    sun.shadow.bias = -0.0004; sun.shadow.normalBias = 0.6;
    scene.add(sun); scene.add(sun.target);
    this.sun = sun;
    scene.userData.sunLight = sun;
    const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight(CONFIG.hemiSky, CONFIG.hemiGround, 0.55);
    scene.add(hemi);

    // birds — two lazy flocks
    const bGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.7, 0.5);
    const bMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x140f1c, side: THREE.DoubleSide, fog: true });
    this.birds = new THREE.InstancedMesh(bGeo, bMat, 26);
    this.birds.frustumCulled = false;
    scene.add(this.birds);
    this.flocks = [
      { cx: 120, cz: 380, r: 70, h: 55, n: 14, off: 0, sp: 0.10 },
      { cx: -260, cz: 90, r: 55, h: 78, n: 12, off: 14, sp: -0.08 },
    ];

    // distant aircraft — blinking light crossing high sky
    const pGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.6, 6, 4);
    this.plane = new THREE.Group();
    const body = new THREE.Mesh(pGeo, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffd9b0, fog: false }));
    const strobe = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.2, 6, 4), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xff3344, fog: false }));
    strobe.position.x = 4;
    this.plane.add(body); this.plane.add(strobe);
    this.plane.userData.strobe = strobe;
    scene.add(this.plane);
    this.planeT = 0.2;
  },
  updateSun(fx, fz) {
    const s = this.sun;
    const gx = Math.round(fx / 4) * 4, gz = Math.round(fz / 4) * 4;
    s.position.set(gx + CONFIG.sunDir.x * 320, CONFIG.sunDir.y * 320, gz + CONFIG.sunDir.z * 320);
    s.target.position.set(gx, 0, gz);
  },
  update(dt) {
    this.mat.uniforms.uTime.value = Tick.t;
    // birds
    let bi = 0;
    for (const f of this.flocks) {
      for (let i = 0; i < f.n; i++) {
        const a = Tick.t * f.sp + f.off + i * 0.55;
        const wob = Math.sin(Tick.t * 2.2 + i * 1.7);
        _v1.set(f.cx + Math.cos(a) * (f.r + i * 2.6), f.h + Math.sin(a * 2.0 + i) * 4 + wob * 1.2, f.cz + Math.sin(a) * (f.r + i * 2.0));
        const flap = 0.45 + Math.abs(Math.sin(Tick.t * 9 + i * 2.3)) * 0.8;
        _q1.setFromEuler(new THREE.Euler(0, -a, 0));
        _m1.compose(_v1, _q1, _v2.set(1, 1, flap));
        this.birds.setMatrixAt(bi++, _m1);
      }
    }
    this.birds.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    // aircraft
    this.planeT += dt * 0.0045;
    if (this.planeT > 1.15) this.planeT = -0.15;
    const px = lerp(-1600, 1600, this.planeT);
    this.plane.position.set(px, 420, -900 + px * 0.22);
    this.plane.userData.strobe.material.color.setHex((Tick.t % 1.1) < 0.08 ? 0xff8899 : 0x550a11);
  },
};
// avoid per-frame Euler alloc in bird loop
{
  const _e = new THREE.Euler();
  const origUpdate = Sky.update.bind(Sky);
  Sky.update = function (dt) {
    this.mat.uniforms.uTime.value = Tick.t;
    let bi = 0;
    for (const f of this.flocks) {
      for (let i = 0; i < f.n; i++) {
        const a = Tick.t * f.sp + f.off + i * 0.55;
        const wob = Math.sin(Tick.t * 2.2 + i * 1.7);
        _v1.set(f.cx + Math.cos(a) * (f.r + i * 2.6), f.h + Math.sin(a * 2.0 + i) * 4 + wob * 1.2, f.cz + Math.sin(a) * (f.r + i * 2.0));
        const flap = 0.45 + Math.abs(Math.sin(Tick.t * 9 + i * 2.3)) * 0.8;
        _e.set(0, -a, 0);
        _q1.setFromEuler(_e);
        _m1.compose(_v1, _q1, _v2.set(1, 1, flap));
        this.birds.setMatrixAt(bi++, _m1);
      }
    }
    this.birds.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    this.planeT += dt * 0.0045;
    if (this.planeT > 1.15) this.planeT = -0.15;
    const px = lerp(-1600, 1600, this.planeT);
    this.plane.position.set(px, 420, -900 + px * 0.22);
    this.plane.userData.strobe.material.color.setHex((Tick.t % 1.1) < 0.08 ? 0xff8899 : 0x550a11);
  };
  void origUpdate;
}

// ============================================================ ocean
const Ocean = {
  mat: null,
  init() {
    this.mat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      uniforms: {
        uTime: { value: 0 },
        uSunDir: { value: CONFIG.sunDir },
        uFogColor: { value: CONFIG.fogColor },
        uFogDensity: { value: CONFIG.fogDensity },
      },
      vertexShader: /* glsl */`
        uniform float uTime;
        varying vec3 vWPos;
        varying vec3 vNormalW;
        varying float vFogDepth;
        void main() {
          vec3 p = (modelMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0)).xyz;
          float t = uTime;
          float h = 0.0;
          vec2 dx = vec2(0.0);
          // 4 directional sine waves; accumulate analytic slope
          vec2 ws[4]; float wl[4]; float am[4];
          ws[0] = vec2(0.9, 0.42); wl[0] = 46.0; am[0] = 0.34;
          ws[1] = vec2(-0.6, 0.8); wl[1] = 27.0; am[1] = 0.22;
          ws[2] = vec2(0.2, -0.97); wl[2] = 14.0; am[2] = 0.11;
          ws[3] = vec2(0.7, -0.7); wl[3] = 7.0; am[3] = 0.06;
          for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            float k = 6.28318 / wl[i];
            float ph = dot(ws[i], p.xz) * k + t * (1.1 + float(i) * 0.35);
            h += sin(ph) * am[i];
            dx += ws[i] * (cos(ph) * am[i] * k);
          }
          p.y += h;
          vWPos = p;
          vNormalW = normalize(vec3(-dx.x, 1.0, -dx.y));
          vec4 mv = viewMatrix * vec4(p, 1.0);
          vFogDepth = -mv.z;
          gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mv;
        }`,
      fragmentShader: /* glsl */`
        uniform vec3 uSunDir;
        uniform vec3 uFogColor;
        uniform float uFogDensity;
        uniform float uTime;
        varying vec3 vWPos;
        varying vec3 vNormalW;
        varying float vFogDepth;
        float hash21(vec2 p) { return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453); }
        void main() {
          vec3 N = normalize(vNormalW);
          vec3 V = normalize(cameraPosition - vWPos);
          // sparkle jitter on the normal for glitter
          float j = hash21(floor(vWPos.xz * 5.0) + floor(uTime * 7.0));
          N.xz += (j - 0.5) * 0.10;
          N = normalize(N);
          vec3 deep = vec3(0.035, 0.075, 0.13);
          vec3 horiz = vec3(0.55, 0.20, 0.28);
          float fres = pow(1.0 - max(dot(N, V), 0.0), 3.0);
          vec3 skyRef = mix(vec3(0.16, 0.09, 0.26), horiz, pow(max(dot(normalize(reflect(-V, N)), uSunDir) * 0.5 + 0.5, 0.0), 3.0));
          vec3 col = mix(deep, skyRef, 0.25 + fres * 0.65);
          // sun glint path
          vec3 R = reflect(-V, N);
          float sp = pow(max(dot(R, uSunDir), 0.0), 160.0);
          col += vec3(1.5, 0.78, 0.38) * sp * 2.6;
          float fog = 1.0 - exp(-uFogDensity * uFogDensity * vFogDepth * vFogDepth);
          col = mix(col, uFogColor, clamp(fog, 0.0, 1.0));
          gl_FragColor = vec4(col, 1.0);
        }`,
      fog: false,
    });
    const geo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(3400, 3400, 120, 120);
    geo.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);
    const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, this.mat);
    mesh.position.y = CONFIG.waterY;
    mesh.renderOrder = 1;
    mesh.frustumCulled = false;
    scene.add(mesh);
  },
  update() { this.mat.uniforms.uTime.value = Tick.t; },
};
// ============================================================ city data: roads, graph, colliders, elevation
// Main island: x[-136..376], z[-256..~250] then beach/ocean to the south (+z).
// Channel water x[-264..-136]; port island x[-470..-264]. Bridge crosses the channel at z=0.
const ROADS = [
  { x1: -120, z1: -240, x2: -120, z2: 240 },
  { x1: 40,   z1: -240, x2: 40,   z2: 240 },
  { x1: 200,  z1: -240, x2: 200,  z2: 240 },
  { x1: 360,  z1: -240, x2: 360,  z2: 240 },
  { x1: -120, z1: -240, x2: 360,  z2: -240 },
  { x1: -120, z1: -80,  x2: 360,  z2: -80 },
  { x1: -120, z1: 80,   x2: 360,  z2: 80 },
  { x1: -120, z1: 240,  x2: 360,  z2: 240 },     // beachfront strip
  { x1: -440, z1: 0,    x2: -120, z2: 0 },       // bridge route into the port
  { x1: -300, z1: -140, x2: -300, z2: 140 },
  { x1: -440, z1: -140, x2: -440, z2: 140 },
  { x1: -440, z1: -140, x2: -300, z2: -140 },
  { x1: -440, z1: 140,  x2: -300, z2: 140 },
];
const ROAD_W = 13, LANE_OFF = 2.6, LANE_OUT = 4.9;
const SIGNAL_NODES = ['40,-80', '40,80', '200,-80', '200,80'];

// ---------- graph ----------
const Graph = { nodes: new Map(), edges: [] };
{
  const nodeAt = (x, z) => {
    const k = x + ',' + z;
    let n = Graph.nodes.get(k);
    if (!n) { n = { x, z, k, edges: [], signal: SIGNAL_NODES.includes(k) }; Graph.nodes.set(k, n); }
    return n;
  };
  for (const r of ROADS) {
    const vert = r.x1 === r.x2;
    const cuts = [];
    if (vert) {
      cuts.push(r.z1, r.z2);
      for (const o of ROADS) {
        if (o === r || o.z1 !== o.z2) continue;
        if (o.z1 >= Math.min(r.z1, r.z2) && o.z1 <= Math.max(r.z1, r.z2) && r.x1 >= Math.min(o.x1, o.x2) && r.x1 <= Math.max(o.x1, o.x2)) cuts.push(o.z1);
      }
      cuts.sort((a, b) => a - b);
      for (let i = 0; i < cuts.length - 1; i++) {
        if (cuts[i] === cuts[i + 1]) continue;
        const a = nodeAt(r.x1, cuts[i]), b = nodeAt(r.x1, cuts[i + 1]);
        const e = { a, b, len: Math.abs(cuts[i + 1] - cuts[i]), fx: 0, fz: 1, vert: true, bridge: false };
        Graph.edges.push(e); a.edges.push(e); b.edges.push(e);
      }
    } else {
      cuts.push(r.x1, r.x2);
      for (const o of ROADS) {
        if (o === r || o.x1 !== o.x2) continue;
        if (o.x1 >= Math.min(r.x1, r.x2) && o.x1 <= Math.max(r.x1, r.x2) && r.z1 >= Math.min(o.z1, o.z2) && r.z1 <= Math.max(o.z1, o.z2)) cuts.push(o.x1);
      }
      cuts.sort((a, b) => a - b);
      for (let i = 0; i < cuts.length - 1; i++) {
        if (cuts[i] === cuts[i + 1]) continue;
        const a = nodeAt(cuts[i], r.z1), b = nodeAt(cuts[i + 1], r.z1);
        const mid = (cuts[i] + cuts[i + 1]) / 2;
        const e = { a, b, len: cuts[i + 1] - cuts[i], fx: 1, fz: 0, vert: false, bridge: r.z1 === 0 && mid > -276 && mid < -120 };
        Graph.edges.push(e); a.edges.push(e); b.edges.push(e);
      }
    }
  }
}
// position along edge: dir=+1 means a->b. Returns into out {x,z,fx,fz}
function edgePoint(e, t, dir, lane, out) {
  const fx = e.fx * dir, fz = e.fz * dir;
  const s = dir > 0 ? t : e.len - t;
  out.x = e.a.x + e.fx * s + (-fz) * lane;
  out.z = e.a.z + e.fz * s + (fx) * lane;
  out.fx = fx; out.fz = fz;
  return out;
}
function randomEdge(excludeBridge) {
  for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
    const e = pick(Graph.edges);
    if (!excludeBridge || !e.bridge) return e;
  }
  return Graph.edges[0];
}
function nearestNode(x, z) {
  let best = null, bd = 1e9;
  for (const n of Graph.nodes.values()) {
    const d = (n.x - x) * (n.x - x) + (n.z - z) * (n.z - z);
    if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = n; }
  }
  return best;
}

// ---------- traffic signals ----------
const Signals = {
  CYCLE: 26, GREEN: 10, YELLOW: 2.6,
  // returns 0 green / 1 yellow / 2 red for an approach axis at a signal node
  state(forNS) {
    const half = this.CYCLE / 2;
    let t = Tick.t % this.CYCLE;
    if (!forNS) t = (t + half) % this.CYCLE;
    if (t < this.GREEN) return 0;
    if (t < this.GREEN + this.YELLOW) return 1;
    return 2;
  },
};

// ---------- elevation ----------
function bridgeT(x) { return clamp((x + 140) / -130, 0, 1); }
function groundHeightAt(x, z) {
  // bridge deck (z=0 route over the channel)
  if (x < -130 && x > -280 && Math.abs(z) < 9) {
    return Math.sin(Math.PI * bridgeT(x)) * 9;
  }
  // parking structure deck + ramp (downtown)
  if (x >= 64 && x <= 148 && z >= -64 && z <= 8) return 6;
  if (x >= 47 && x <= 64 && z >= -40 && z <= -24) return 6 * (x - 47) / 17;
  // pier deck + ramp
  if (x >= 136 && x <= 164) {
    if (z >= 256 && z <= 348) return 2.5;
    if (z > 248 && z < 256) return 0.12 + (2.5 - 0.12) * (z - 248) / 8;
  }
  // beach sand slope down to the water
  if (z > 249.5 && x > -136 && x < 376) {
    if (z <= 292) return -2.2 * (z - 249.5) / 42.5;
    return -2.2 - (z - 292) * 0.12;
  }
  // off-island = seabed
  const onMain = x >= -136 && x <= 376 && z >= -256 && z <= 249.5;
  const onPort = x >= -470 && x <= -264 && z >= -190 && z <= 190;
  if (!onMain && !onPort) return -4.5;
  return 0;
}
function isWaterAt(x, z) {
  return groundHeightAt(x, z) < CONFIG.waterY - 0.15;
}

// ---------- static collision world (AABBs on a uniform grid) ----------
const Colliders = {
  boxes: [], grid: new Map(), CELL: 26, _stamp: 0, _seen: null,
  addBox(minX, minZ, maxX, maxZ, y0, y1) {
    const idx = this.boxes.length;
    this.boxes.push({ minX, minZ, maxX, maxZ, y0, y1 });
    const c = this.CELL;
    for (let ix = Math.floor(minX / c); ix <= Math.floor(maxX / c); ix++) {
      for (let iz = Math.floor(minZ / c); iz <= Math.floor(maxZ / c); iz++) {
        const k = (ix + 1024) * 4096 + (iz + 1024);
        let arr = this.grid.get(k);
        if (!arr) { arr = []; this.grid.set(k, arr); }
        arr.push(idx);
      }
    }
    return idx;
  },
  finalize() { this._seen = new Int32Array(this.boxes.length); },
  // push a circle (px,pz,r) out of all boxes that overlap [y0,y1]; writes result + hit flag
  _res: { x: 0, z: 0, hit: false, nx: 0, nz: 0 },
  resolveCircle(px, pz, r, y0, y1) {
    const res = this._res;
    res.x = px; res.z = pz; res.hit = false; res.nx = 0; res.nz = 0;
    const c = this.CELL;
    for (let ix = Math.floor((px - r) / c); ix <= Math.floor((px + r) / c); ix++) {
      for (let iz = Math.floor((pz - r) / c); iz <= Math.floor((pz + r) / c); iz++) {
        const arr = this.grid.get((ix + 1024) * 4096 + (iz + 1024));
        if (!arr) continue;
        for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
          const b = this.boxes[arr[i]];
          if (y1 < b.y0 || y0 > b.y1) continue;
          const cx = clamp(res.x, b.minX, b.maxX), cz = clamp(res.z, b.minZ, b.maxZ);
          let dx = res.x - cx, dz = res.z - cz;
          let d2 = dx * dx + dz * dz;
          if (d2 >= r * r) continue;
          if (d2 < 1e-9) {
            // center inside the box: push out along smallest penetration axis
            const pxl = res.x - b.minX, pxr = b.maxX - res.x;
            const pzl = res.z - b.minZ, pzr = b.maxZ - res.z;
            const m = Math.min(pxl, pxr, pzl, pzr);
            if (m === pxl) { res.x = b.minX - r; dx = -1; dz = 0; }
            else if (m === pxr) { res.x = b.maxX + r; dx = 1; dz = 0; }
            else if (m === pzl) { res.z = b.minZ - r; dx = 0; dz = -1; }
            else { res.z = b.maxZ + r; dx = 0; dz = 1; }
            res.hit = true; res.nx += dx; res.nz += dz;
          } else {
            const d = Math.sqrt(d2);
            const push = (r - d) / d;
            res.x += dx * push; res.z += dz * push;
            res.hit = true; res.nx += dx / d; res.nz += dz / d;
          }
        }
      }
    }
    if (res.hit) {
      const n = Math.hypot(res.nx, res.nz) || 1;
      res.nx /= n; res.nz /= n;
    }
    return res;
  },
  // 3D ray vs static boxes; returns hit distance or -1. Writes normal into _rayN.
  _rayN: { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 },
  raycast(ox, oy, oz, dx, dy, dz, maxD) {
    this._stamp++;
    const stamp = this._stamp, seen = this._seen;
    const c = this.CELL;
    let bestD = -1;
    const steps = Math.ceil(maxD / (c * 0.5)) + 1;
    for (let s = 0; s <= steps; s++) {
      const t = Math.min(s * c * 0.5, maxD);
      const wx = ox + dx * t, wz = oz + dz * t;
      const ix = Math.floor(wx / c), iz = Math.floor(wz / c);
      for (let jx = ix - 1; jx <= ix + 1; jx++) {
        for (let jz = iz - 1; jz <= iz + 1; jz++) {
          const arr = this.grid.get((jx + 1024) * 4096 + (jz + 1024));
          if (!arr) continue;
          for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
            const bi = arr[i];
            if (seen[bi] === stamp) continue;
            seen[bi] = stamp;
            const b = this.boxes[bi];
            // slab test
            let tmin = 0, tmax = maxD, nAxis = -1, nSign = 0;
            // x
            if (Math.abs(dx) < 1e-9) { if (ox < b.minX || ox > b.maxX) continue; }
            else {
              let t1 = (b.minX - ox) / dx, t2 = (b.maxX - ox) / dx, sgn = -1;
              if (t1 > t2) { const tt = t1; t1 = t2; t2 = tt; sgn = 1; }
              if (t1 > tmin) { tmin = t1; nAxis = 0; nSign = sgn; }
              tmax = Math.min(tmax, t2);
              if (tmin > tmax) continue;
            }
            // y
            if (Math.abs(dy) < 1e-9) { if (oy < b.y0 || oy > b.y1) continue; }
            else {
              let t1 = (b.y0 - oy) / dy, t2 = (b.y1 - oy) / dy, sgn = -1;
              if (t1 > t2) { const tt = t1; t1 = t2; t2 = tt; sgn = 1; }
              if (t1 > tmin) { tmin = t1; nAxis = 1; nSign = sgn; }
              tmax = Math.min(tmax, t2);
              if (tmin > tmax) continue;
            }
            // z
            if (Math.abs(dz) < 1e-9) { if (oz < b.minZ || oz > b.maxZ) continue; }
            else {
              let t1 = (b.minZ - oz) / dz, t2 = (b.maxZ - oz) / dz, sgn = -1;
              if (t1 > t2) { const tt = t1; t1 = t2; t2 = tt; sgn = 1; }
              if (t1 > tmin) { tmin = t1; nAxis = 2; nSign = sgn; }
              tmax = Math.min(tmax, t2);
              if (tmin > tmax) continue;
            }
            if (tmin <= 0 || tmin > maxD) continue;
            if (bestD < 0 || tmin < bestD) {
              bestD = tmin;
              const N = this._rayN;
              N.x = nAxis === 0 ? nSign : 0; N.y = nAxis === 1 ? nSign : 0; N.z = nAxis === 2 ? nSign : 0;
            }
          }
        }
      }
      if (bestD >= 0 && bestD < t) break;
    }
    return bestD;
  },
  losBlocked(ax, ay, az, bx, by, bz) {
    const dx = bx - ax, dy = by - ay, dz = bz - az;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy, dz);
    if (d < 0.001) return false;
    const hit = this.raycast(ax, ay, az, dx / d, dy / d, dz / d, d);
    return hit >= 0 && hit < d - 0.5;
  },
};

// ---------- derived placement data ----------
const CityData = { parkSpots: [], sidewalks: [], lightSpots: [], blocks: [] };
{
  // sidewalk walking segments + streetlight positions from graph edges
  for (const e of Graph.edges) {
    if (e.bridge) continue;
    if (e.len < 30) continue;
    for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
      const ox = (-e.fz) * side * 8.1, oz = (e.fx) * side * 8.1;
      const inset = 11;
      CityData.sidewalks.push({
        ax: e.a.x + e.fx * inset + ox, az: e.a.z + e.fz * inset + oz,
        bx: e.b.x - e.fx * inset + ox, bz: e.b.z - e.fz * inset + oz,
      });
    }
    // streetlights every ~32m, alternating sides
    const count = Math.floor((e.len - 24) / 32);
    for (let i = 0; i <= count; i++) {
      const t = 16 + i * 32;
      const side = (i % 2) ? 1 : -1;
      const ox = (-e.fz) * side * 7.4, oz = (e.fx) * side * 7.4;
      CityData.lightSpots.push({
        x: e.a.x + e.fx * t + ox, z: e.a.z + e.fz * t + oz,
        sx: -(-e.fz) * side, sz: -(e.fx) * side,    // direction from pole into the street
      });
    }
  }
  // parked cars along curbs (lane offset 5.7), skipping intersections
  let spotCount = 0;
  for (const e of Graph.edges) {
    if (e.bridge || e.len < 60) continue;
    if (e.a.z === 0 && e.b.z === 0 && e.a.x < -130) continue;  // not on bridge route
    for (let t = 20; t < e.len - 20 && spotCount < 52; t += 26) {
      if (Math.random() < 0.45) continue;
      const side = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1;
      const ox = (-e.fz) * side * 5.7, oz = (e.fx) * side * 5.7;
      const x = e.a.x + e.fx * t + ox, z = e.a.z + e.fz * t + oz;
      // keep the hero-car curb area near the pier clear
      if (Math.abs(z - 240) < 12 && x > 100 && x < 180) continue;
      const heading = Math.atan2(e.fx * side, e.fz * side);
      const roll = Math.random();
      const model = roll < 0.55 ? 'sedan' : roll < 0.72 ? 'muscle' : roll < 0.84 ? 'sports' : roll < 0.92 ? 'taxi' : 'police';
      CityData.parkSpots.push({ x, z, heading, model });
      spotCount++;
    }
  }
}
// ============================================================ city construction (all static visuals + colliders)
const CityFX = {
  signMats: [], beaconMat: null, lampMesh: null, lamps: [], lastNS: -1, lastEW: -1,
  fountainMat: null,
  update() {
    // neon flicker on a couple of signs
    for (let i = 0; i < this.signMats.length; i++) {
      const m = this.signMats[i];
      if (!m.userData.flicker) continue;
      const f = Math.sin(Tick.t * 13 + i * 7.3) + Math.sin(Tick.t * 31 + i * 3.1);
      const on = f > -1.55 ? 1 : 0.25;
      m.color.setScalar(2.1 * on);
    }
    if (this.beaconMat) {
      const p = (Math.sin(Tick.t * 2.4) * 0.5 + 0.5);
      this.beaconMat.color.setRGB(2.8 * p + 0.3, 0.25 * p, 0.28 * p);
    }
    // traffic signal lamps (only touch buffers when phase changes)
    const ns = Signals.state(true), ew = Signals.state(false);
    if (ns !== this.lastNS || ew !== this.lastEW) {
      this.lastNS = ns; this.lastEW = ew;
      for (const l of this.lamps) {
        const st = l.isNS ? ns : ew;
        const lit = (l.kind === 0 && st === 0) || (l.kind === 1 && st === 1) || (l.kind === 2 && st === 2);
        if (l.kind === 0) _c1.setRGB(lit ? 0.4 : 0.02, lit ? 3.2 : 0.07, lit ? 0.9 : 0.03);
        else if (l.kind === 1) _c1.setRGB(lit ? 3.2 : 0.08, lit ? 2.2 : 0.05, lit ? 0.3 : 0.01);
        else _c1.setRGB(lit ? 3.4 : 0.09, lit ? 0.25 : 0.02, lit ? 0.25 : 0.02);
        this.lampMesh.setColorAt(l.idx, _c1);
      }
      this.lampMesh.instanceColor.needsUpdate = true;
    }
    if (this.fountainMat) this.fountainMat.map.offset.y = -Tick.t * 0.5;
  },
};

const ROCKET_PICKUP_POS = new THREE.Vector3(106, 6, -28);
const HOSPITAL_SPAWN = new THREE.Vector3(262, 0, -224);

function buildCity() {
  const S = new THREE.Group();
  S.matrixAutoUpdate = false;
  scene.add(S);
  const add = (m) => { m.matrixAutoUpdate = false; m.updateMatrix(); S.add(m); return m; };

  // ---------- shared materials ----------
  const roadMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: TEX.road, roughness: 0.14, metalness: 0.0, envMapIntensity: 1.5 });
  const interMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: TEX.intersection, roughness: 0.14, metalness: 0.0, envMapIntensity: 1.5 });
  const walkMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: TEX.sidewalk, roughness: 0.55, metalness: 0, envMapIntensity: 0.5 });
  const concMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x4a4452, roughness: 0.7, envMapIntensity: 0.3 });
  const darkMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x232028, roughness: 0.8, envMapIntensity: 0.2 });
  const metalMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x5a5f6e, roughness: 0.45, metalness: 0.6, envMapIntensity: 0.8 });

  // ---------- ground ----------
  {
    const g = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(512 + 8, 506), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x1c1923, roughness: 0.75, envMapIntensity: 0.35 }));
    g.geometry.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);
    g.position.set(120, -0.04, -3.2);
    g.receiveShadow = true;
    add(g);
    const p = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(206, 380), g.material);
    p.geometry.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);
    p.position.set(-367, -0.04, 0);
    p.receiveShadow = true;
    add(p);
    // beach sand (sloping into the water)
    const sg = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(512, 62, 48, 8);
    sg.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);
    const pos = sg.attributes.position;
    for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
      const wx = pos.getX(i) + 120, wz = pos.getZ(i) + 280.5;
      pos.setY(i, groundHeightAt(wx === 0 ? 0.01 : wx, Math.max(wz, 250)) + 0.03);
    }
    sg.computeVertexNormals();
    TEX.sand.repeat.set(34, 5);
    const sand = new THREE.Mesh(sg, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: TEX.sand, roughness: 0.95 }));
    sand.position.set(120, 0, 280.5);
    sand.receiveShadow = true;
    add(sand);
  }

  // ---------- roads + intersections ----------
  {
    for (const e of Graph.edges) {
      if (e.bridge) continue;
      const L = e.len - 13;
      if (L <= 0) continue;
      const geo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(ROAD_W, L);
      geo.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);
      const uv = geo.attributes.uv;
      for (let i = 0; i < uv.count; i++) uv.setY(i, uv.getY(i) * (L / 13));
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(geo, roadMat);
      m.position.set((e.a.x + e.b.x) / 2, 0.02, (e.a.z + e.b.z) / 2);
      if (!e.vert) m.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2;
      m.receiveShadow = true;
      add(m);
    }
    const iGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(ROAD_W, ROAD_W);
    iGeo.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);
    for (const n of Graph.nodes.values()) {
      if (n.x < -130 && n.z === 0 && n.x > -276) continue;
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(iGeo, interMat);
      m.position.set(n.x, 0.025, n.z);
      m.receiveShadow = true;
      add(m);
    }
  }

  // ---------- sidewalks ----------
  {
    const sGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(1, 1, 1);
    sGeo.translate(0, 0.5, 0);
    const items = [];
    for (const e of Graph.edges) {
      if (e.bridge || e.len < 26) continue;
      for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
        const ox = (-e.fz) * side * 8.1, oz = (e.fx) * side * 8.1;
        items.push({ x: (e.a.x + e.b.x) / 2 + ox, z: (e.a.z + e.b.z) / 2 + oz, w: e.vert ? 3.2 : e.len - 21, d: e.vert ? e.len - 21 : 3.2 });
      }
    }
    for (const n of Graph.nodes.values()) {
      if (n.x < -270 && n.z === 0) { /* port end ok */ }
      for (const sx of [-1, 1]) for (const sz of [-1, 1]) {
        items.push({ x: n.x + sx * 8.1, z: n.z + sz * 8.1, w: 3.2, d: 3.2 });
      }
    }
    TEX.sidewalk.repeat.set(2, 2);
    const im = new THREE.InstancedMesh(sGeo, walkMat, items.length);
    items.forEach((it, i) => {
      _m1.compose(_v1.set(it.x, 0, it.z), _q1.identity(), _v2.set(it.w, 0.14, it.d));
      im.setMatrixAt(i, _m1);
    });
    im.receiveShadow = true;
    im.frustumCulled = false;
    add(im);
  }

  // ---------- buildings ----------
  const towerMats = TEX.tower.map(([map, em]) => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
    map, emissiveMap: em, emissive: 0xffffff, emissiveIntensity: 1.25, roughness: 0.8, envMapIntensity: 0.35,
  }));
  const lowMats = TEX.lowrise.map(([map, em]) => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
    map, emissiveMap: em, emissive: 0xffffff, emissiveIntensity: 1.15, roughness: 0.8, envMapIntensity: 0.3,
  }));
  function roofBox() {
    const g = new THREE.BoxGeometry(1, 1, 1);
    g.translate(0, 0.5, 0);
    const uv = g.attributes.uv;
    for (let i = 8; i < 16; i++) uv.setXY(i, 0.01 + (i % 2) * 0.05, 0.94 + ((i >> 1) % 2) * 0.05);
    return g;
  }
  const bGeo = roofBox();
  const towerInst = [[], [], []], lowInst = [[], [], []];
  const acInst = [], antInst = [], beaconPos = [];
  const signAnchors = [];

  function placeBuilding(x, z, w, d, h, facing) {
    // keep the hospital block + respawn point clear (hospital at 262,-206; spawn at 262,-224)
    if (x + w / 2 > 238 && x - w / 2 < 286 && z + d / 2 > -232 && z - d / 2 < -192) return;
    const v = rndi(0, 2);
    _m1.compose(_v1.set(x, 0, z), _q1.identity(), _v2.set(w, h, d));
    (h > 26 ? towerInst : lowInst)[v].push(_m1.clone());
    Colliders.addBox(x - w / 2, z - d / 2, x + w / 2, z + d / 2, 0, h);
    if (h > 26 && h < 90 && Math.random() < 0.6) {
      _m2.compose(_v1.set(x + rnd(-w / 4, w / 4), h, z + rnd(-d / 4, d / 4)), _q1.identity(), _v2.set(rnd(2, 4), rnd(1.2, 2.2), rnd(2, 4)));
      acInst.push(_m2.clone());
    }
    if (h >= 90) {
      _m2.compose(_v1.set(x, h, z), _q1.identity(), _v2.set(0.4, rnd(8, 14), 0.4));
      antInst.push(_m2.clone());
      beaconPos.push(new THREE.Vector3(x, h + rnd(8, 13), z));
    }
    if (facing) signAnchors.push({ x, z, w, d, h, facing });
  }

  // march buildings along one side of a block
  function marchSide(x0, z0, x1, z1, edge, hMin, hMax, faceOut) {
    // edge: 's' = along south boundary (z1), 'n' = north (z0), 'w','e'
    const horiz = edge === 's' || edge === 'n';
    const span = horiz ? (x1 - x0) : (z1 - z0);
    let t = rnd(2, 8);
    while (t < span - 16) {
      const w = rnd(18, 32);
      if (t + w > span - 2) break;
      const depth = rnd(15, 22);
      const h = rnd(hMin, hMax);
      let bx, bz, bw, bd, facing = null;
      if (horiz) {
        bx = x0 + t + w / 2; bw = w; bd = depth;
        bz = edge === 's' ? z1 - depth / 2 : z0 + depth / 2;
        if (faceOut) facing = edge === 's' ? '+z' : '-z';
      } else {
        bz = z0 + t + w / 2; bd = w; bw = depth;
        bx = edge === 'w' ? x0 + depth / 2 : x1 - depth / 2;
        if (faceOut) facing = edge === 'w' ? '-x' : '+x';
      }
      placeBuilding(bx, bz, bw, bd, h, facing);
      t += w + rnd(3, 9);
    }
  }

  {
    const IN = 11;
    // beach row (z 80..240): lowrise hotels/clubs, neon faces toward the water
    for (const [bx0, bx1] of [[-120, 40], [40, 200], [200, 360]]) {
      const x0 = bx0 + IN, x1 = bx1 - IN, z0 = 80 + IN, z1 = 240 - IN;
      marchSide(x0, z0, x1, z1, 's', 10, 22, true);
      marchSide(x0, z0, x1, z1 - 34, 'n', 12, 26, false);
    }
    // north row (z -240..-80): the skyline
    for (const [bx0, bx1] of [[-120, 40], [40, 200], [200, 360]]) {
      const x0 = bx0 + IN, x1 = bx1 - IN, z0 = -240 + IN, z1 = -80 - IN;
      marchSide(x0, z0, x1, z1, 's', 36, 90, false);
      marchSide(x0, z0, x1, z1, 'n', 50, 130, false);
      placeBuilding((x0 + x1) / 2 + rnd(-18, 18), (z0 + z1) / 2, rnd(26, 36), rnd(24, 34), rnd(90, 150), null);
    }
    // middle row west + east blocks (x 40..200 is plaza/parking)
    for (const [bx0, bx1] of [[-120, 40], [200, 360]]) {
      const x0 = bx0 + IN, x1 = bx1 - IN, z0 = -80 + IN, z1 = 80 - IN;
      marchSide(x0, z0, x1, z1, 's', 24, 60, false);
      marchSide(x0, z0, x1, z1, 'n', 30, 80, false);
      marchSide(x0, z0 + 30, x1, z1 - 30, bx0 === -120 ? 'w' : 'e', 20, 50, false);
    }
  }

  // hospital (north road, distinct white building)
  {
    const hx = 262, hz = -206;
    const hMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xcfd4e0, roughness: 0.7, envMapIntensity: 0.4 });
    const hb = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(40, 18, 20), hMat);
    hb.position.set(hx, 9, hz); hb.castShadow = hb.receiveShadow = true;
    add(hb);
    Colliders.addBox(hx - 20, hz - 10, hx + 20, hz + 10, 0, 18);
    const sign = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(14, 7), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: TEX.hospitalSign, transparent: true }));
    sign.material.color.setScalar(1.9);
    sign.position.set(hx, 12, hz - 10.1); sign.rotation.y = Math.PI;
    add(sign);
  }

  // commit building instances
  function commitInst(lists, mats, geo, shadows) {
    lists.forEach((arr, v) => {
      if (!arr.length) return;
      const im = new THREE.InstancedMesh(geo, mats[v], arr.length);
      arr.forEach((m, i) => im.setMatrixAt(i, m));
      im.castShadow = shadows; im.receiveShadow = shadows;
      im.frustumCulled = false;
      add(im);
    });
  }
  commitInst(towerInst, towerMats, bGeo, true);
  commitInst(lowInst, lowMats, bGeo, true);
  if (acInst.length) {
    const im = new THREE.InstancedMesh(bGeo, darkMat, acInst.length);
    acInst.forEach((m, i) => im.setMatrixAt(i, m));
    im.frustumCulled = false;
    add(im);
  }
  if (antInst.length) {
    const im = new THREE.InstancedMesh(bGeo, darkMat, antInst.length);
    antInst.forEach((m, i) => im.setMatrixAt(i, m));
    im.frustumCulled = false;
    add(im);
  }
  // rooftop beacons
  if (beaconPos.length) {
    CityFX.beaconMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xff2233, fog: false });
    const im = new THREE.InstancedMesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.7, 6, 5), CityFX.beaconMat, beaconPos.length);
    beaconPos.forEach((p, i) => {
      _m1.compose(p, _q1.identity(), _v2.set(1, 1, 1));
      im.setMatrixAt(i, _m1);
    });
    im.frustumCulled = false;
    add(im);
  }

  // ---------- distant skyline impostors ----------
  {
    const mats = TEX.tower.map(([map, em]) => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
      map, emissiveMap: em, emissive: 0xffffff, emissiveIntensity: 2.0, color: 0x8a86b0, roughness: 0.9,
    }));
    const lists = [[], [], []];
    for (let i = 0; i < 66; i++) {
      let x, z;
      if (i < 40) { x = rnd(-380, 980); z = rnd(-720, -330); }
      else { x = rnd(460, 1000); z = rnd(-320, 240); }
      const w = rnd(22, 48), h = rnd(50, 200);
      _m1.compose(_v1.set(x, 0, z), _q1.identity(), _v2.set(w, h, rnd(22, 48)));
      lists[rndi(0, 2)].push(_m1.clone());
    }
    lists.forEach((arr, v) => {
      if (!arr.length) return;
      const im = new THREE.InstancedMesh(bGeo, mats[v], arr.length);
      arr.forEach((m, i) => im.setMatrixAt(i, m));
      im.frustumCulled = false;
      add(im);
    });
  }

  // ---------- neon signs (beach strip + a few downtown) ----------
  const streakItems = [];   // {x,z,dx,dz,w,len,r,g,b}
  {
    let si = 0;
    for (const a of signAnchors) {
      if (a.facing !== '+z' || Math.random() < 0.25) continue;
      const def = TEX.signs[si++ % TEX.signs.length];
      const w = Math.min(a.w * 0.8, 13), h = w / 4;
      const sy = clamp(a.h * 0.62, 4.5, a.h - h / 2 - 1);
      const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: def.t, transparent: true, fog: false });
      mat.color.setScalar(2.1);
      mat.userData.flicker = Math.random() < 0.3;
      CityFX.signMats.push(mat);
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w, h), mat);
      const fz = a.z + a.d / 2 + 0.15;
      m.position.set(a.x, sy, fz);
      add(m);
      _c1.setHex(def.c);
      streakItems.push({ x: a.x, z: fz + 0.2, dx: 0, dz: 1, w: w * 0.8, len: rnd(10, 16), r: _c1.r * 1.7, g: _c1.g * 1.7, b: _c1.b * 1.7 });
    }
    // big rooftop sign downtown
    const def = TEX.signs[7];
    const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: def.t, transparent: true, fog: false });
    mat.color.setScalar(2.3);
    CityFX.signMats.push(mat);
    const big = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(26, 6.5), mat);
    big.position.set(280, 86, -76); big.rotation.y = 0;
    add(big);
  }

  // ---------- streetlights ----------
  {
    const poleGeo = BufferGeometryUtils.mergeGeometries([
      new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.09, 0.13, 5.6, 6).translate(0, 2.8, 0),
      new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.14, 0.12, 2.2).translate(0, 5.55, 1.05),
    ]);
    const headGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.42, 0.16, 0.8);
    const spots = CityData.lightSpots;
    const poles = new THREE.InstancedMesh(poleGeo, darkMat, spots.length);
    const headMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffd9a0, fog: false });
    headMat.color.multiplyScalar(2.4);
    const heads = new THREE.InstancedMesh(headGeo, headMat, spots.length);
    spots.forEach((s, i) => {
      const rotY = Math.atan2(s.sx, s.sz);
      _q1.setFromAxisAngle(UP, rotY);
      _m1.compose(_v1.set(s.x, groundHeightAt(s.x, s.z), s.z), _q1, _v2.set(1, 1, 1));
      poles.setMatrixAt(i, _m1);
      _m1.compose(_v1.set(s.x + s.sx * 2.0, groundHeightAt(s.x, s.z) + 5.52, s.z + s.sz * 2.0), _q1, _v2.set(1, 1, 1));
      heads.setMatrixAt(i, _m1);
      const py0 = groundHeightAt(s.x, s.z);
      Colliders.addBox(s.x - 0.16, s.z - 0.16, s.x + 0.16, s.z + 0.16, py0, py0 + 5.6);
      streakItems.push({ x: s.x + s.sx * 2.0, z: s.z + s.sz * 2.0, dx: s.sx, dz: s.sz, w: 1.6, len: 11, r: 1.9, g: 1.45, b: 0.95 });
    });
    poles.castShadow = true;
    poles.frustumCulled = false; heads.frustumCulled = false;
    add(poles); add(heads);
  }

  // ---------- static wet-road streaks ----------
  {
    const g = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1, 1);
    g.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);
    g.translate(0, 0, 0.5);
    const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: TEX.streak, transparent: true, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, fog: true });
    const im = new THREE.InstancedMesh(g, mat, streakItems.length);
    streakItems.forEach((s, i) => {
      _q1.setFromAxisAngle(UP, Math.atan2(s.dx, s.dz));
      _m1.compose(_v1.set(s.x, groundHeightAt(s.x, s.z) + 0.07, s.z), _q1, _v2.set(s.w, 1, s.len));
      im.setMatrixAt(i, _m1);
      im.setColorAt(i, _c1.setRGB(s.r * 0.55, s.g * 0.55, s.b * 0.55));
    });
    im.frustumCulled = false;
    im.renderOrder = 4;
    add(im);
  }

  // ---------- traffic lights ----------
  {
    const nodes = SIGNAL_NODES.map(k => Graph.nodes.get(k)).filter(Boolean);
    const poleGeo = BufferGeometryUtils.mergeGeometries([
      new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.1, 0.12, 5.0, 6).translate(0, 2.5, 0),
      new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.5, 1.25, 0.36).translate(0, 4.4, 0),       // NS head
      new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.36, 1.25, 0.5).translate(0, 3.0, 0),       // EW head
    ]);
    const poles = new THREE.InstancedMesh(poleGeo, darkMat, nodes.length * 2);
    const lampGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.15, 6, 5);
    const lampCount = nodes.length * 2 * 6;
    const lampMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffffff, fog: false });
    const lamps = new THREE.InstancedMesh(lampGeo, lampMat, lampCount);
    let pi = 0, li = 0;
    for (const n of nodes) {
      for (const corner of [[8.4, 8.4], [-8.4, -8.4]]) {
        const px = n.x + corner[0], pz = n.z + corner[1];
        _m1.compose(_v1.set(px, 0, pz), _q1.identity(), _v2.set(1, 1, 1));
        poles.setMatrixAt(pi++, _m1);
        // NS head lamps (face along z), kinds: 0 green(bottom) 1 yellow 2 red(top)
        for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
          _m1.compose(_v1.set(px, 4.0 + k * 0.38, pz + (corner[1] > 0 ? -0.26 : 0.26)), _q1.identity(), _v2.set(1, 1, 1));
          lamps.setMatrixAt(li, _m1);
          lamps.setColorAt(li, _c1.setRGB(0.05, 0.05, 0.05));
          CityFX.lamps.push({ idx: li++, isNS: true, kind: k === 0 ? 0 : k === 1 ? 1 : 2 });
        }
        // EW head lamps
        for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
          _m1.compose(_v1.set(px + (corner[0] > 0 ? -0.26 : 0.26), 2.6 + k * 0.38, pz), _q1.identity(), _v2.set(1, 1, 1));
          lamps.setMatrixAt(li, _m1);
          lamps.setColorAt(li, _c1.setRGB(0.05, 0.05, 0.05));
          CityFX.lamps.push({ idx: li++, isNS: false, kind: k === 0 ? 0 : k === 1 ? 1 : 2 });
        }
        Colliders.addBox(px - 0.25, pz - 0.25, px + 0.25, pz + 0.25, 0, 5);
      }
    }
    poles.frustumCulled = false; lamps.frustumCulled = false;
    poles.castShadow = true;
    add(poles); add(lamps);
    CityFX.lampMesh = lamps;
  }

  // ---------- palms ----------
  {
    const palmPts = [];
    for (let x = -110; x <= 370; x += 14) palmPts.push({ x: x + rnd(-3, 3), z: 249.5 + rnd(-0.8, 0.8), s: rnd(0.85, 1.25) });
    for (let x = -100; x <= 360; x += 38) palmPts.push({ x: x + rnd(-4, 4), z: 231.5 + rnd(-1, 1), s: rnd(0.8, 1.1) });
    for (const p of [[168, -34], [182, 6], [168, 38], [-100, 60], [-100, -60]]) palmPts.push({ x: p[0], z: p[1], s: 1 });
    const trunkGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.14, 0.24, 7.2, 6);
    trunkGeo.translate(0, 3.6, 0);
    const trunkMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x6b513a, roughness: 0.9 });
    const trunks = new THREE.InstancedMesh(trunkGeo, trunkMat, palmPts.length);
    const frondGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.1, 3.6, 1, 3);
    frondGeo.translate(0, -1.5, 0);
    frondGeo.rotateX(-1.25);
    const frondMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: TEX.frond, transparent: true, alphaTest: 0.4, side: THREE.DoubleSide, roughness: 0.85 });
    frondMat.onBeforeCompile = (shader) => {
      shader.uniforms.uTime = { value: 0 };
      shader.vertexShader = 'uniform float uTime;\n' + shader.vertexShader.replace(
        '#include <begin_vertex>',
        `#include <begin_vertex>
        #ifdef USE_INSTANCING
          vec4 iw = instanceMatrix * vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
          float ph = iw.x * 0.21 + iw.z * 0.17;
          float wgt = clamp((1.5 + position.y) / 3.6, 0.0, 1.0);
          float sway = sin(uTime * 1.5 + ph) * 0.7 + sin(uTime * 2.7 + ph * 1.9) * 0.3;
          transformed.x += sway * 0.22 * wgt * wgt;
          transformed.z += cos(uTime * 1.1 + ph) * 0.12 * wgt * wgt;
        #endif`);
      frondMat.userData.shader = shader;
    };
    frondMat.customProgramCacheKey = () => 'palmfrond';
    const fronds = new THREE.InstancedMesh(frondGeo, frondMat, palmPts.length * 6);
    let fi = 0;
    palmPts.forEach((p, i) => {
      const y = groundHeightAt(p.x, p.z);
      Colliders.addBox(p.x - 0.2, p.z - 0.2, p.x + 0.2, p.z + 0.2, y, y + 6.5 * p.s);
      const lean = rnd(-0.09, 0.09), leanZ = rnd(-0.09, 0.09);
      _q1.setFromEuler(new THREE.Euler(leanZ, rnd(Math.PI * 2), lean));
      _m1.compose(_v1.set(p.x, y, p.z), _q1, _v2.set(p.s, p.s, p.s));
      trunks.setMatrixAt(i, _m1);
      const topX = p.x + lean * 7 * p.s, topY = y + 7.1 * p.s, topZ = p.z + leanZ * 7 * p.s;
      for (let f = 0; f < 6; f++) {
        _q1.setFromEuler(new THREE.Euler(rnd(-0.12, 0.12), (f / 6) * Math.PI * 2 + rnd(0.4), 0));
        _m1.compose(_v1.set(topX, topY, topZ), _q1, _v2.set(p.s, p.s, p.s));
        fronds.setMatrixAt(fi++, _m1);
      }
    });
    trunks.castShadow = true; fronds.castShadow = true;
    trunks.frustumCulled = false; fronds.frustumCulled = false;
    add(trunks); add(fronds);
    CityFX.frondMat = frondMat;
  }

  // ---------- pier ----------
  {
    TEX.planks.repeat.set(5, 18);
    const deckMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: TEX.planks, roughness: 0.8 });
    const deck = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(28, 0.5, 92), deckMat);
    deck.position.set(150, 2.25, 302);
    deck.castShadow = deck.receiveShadow = true;
    add(deck);
    // ramp from the sidewalk
    const ramp = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(20, 0.4, 9.5), deckMat);
    ramp.position.set(150, 1.25, 252);
    ramp.rotation.x = -Math.atan2(2.38, 8);
    add(ramp);
    // legs
    const legGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.32, 0.32, 7, 6);
    const legs = new THREE.InstancedMesh(legGeo, darkMat, 24);
    let li = 0;
    for (let z = 262; z <= 344; z += 16) for (const x of [138.5, 150, 161.5]) {
      _m1.compose(_v1.set(x, -1, z), _q1.identity(), _v2.set(1, 1, 1));
      legs.setMatrixAt(li++, _m1);
    }
    legs.count = li;
    add(legs);
    // rails (visual + colliders)
    const railMat = darkMat;
    for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
      const r = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.3, 1.1, 92), railMat);
      r.position.set(150 + side * 13.8, 3.35, 302);
      add(r);
      Colliders.addBox(150 + side * 13.8 - 0.3, 256, 150 + side * 13.8 + 0.3, 348, 2.5, 4.2);
    }
    const rEnd = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(28, 1.1, 0.3), railMat);
    rEnd.position.set(150, 3.35, 347.8);
    add(rEnd);
    Colliders.addBox(136, 347.5, 164, 348.4, 2.5, 4.2);
    // pier kiosk with a sign
    const kiosk = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(6, 3.2, 5), lowMats[2]);
    kiosk.position.set(143, 4.15, 330);
    kiosk.castShadow = true;
    add(kiosk);
    Colliders.addBox(140, 327.5, 146, 332.5, 2.5, 7.4);
    const def = TEX.signs[4];
    const sm = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: def.t, transparent: true, fog: false });
    sm.color.setScalar(2.1);
    CityFX.signMats.push(sm);
    const sgn = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(6, 1.5), sm);
    sgn.position.set(146.2, 5.2, 330); sgn.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2;
    add(sgn);
    // pier lamps
    const lampMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffe0b0, fog: false });
    lampMat.color.multiplyScalar(2.2);
    const lampGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.2, 6, 5);
    const plamps = new THREE.InstancedMesh(lampGeo, lampMat, 10);
    const poleGeo2 = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.07, 0.09, 3.4, 5);
    poleGeo2.translate(0, 1.7, 0);
    const ppoles = new THREE.InstancedMesh(poleGeo2, darkMat, 10);
    let pli = 0;
    for (let z = 266; z <= 344; z += 19.5) for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
      _m1.compose(_v1.set(150 + side * 12.6, 2.55, z), _q1.identity(), _v2.set(1, 1, 1));
      ppoles.setMatrixAt(pli, _m1);
      _m1.compose(_v1.set(150 + side * 12.6, 6.1, z), _q1.identity(), _v2.set(1, 1, 1));
      plamps.setMatrixAt(pli++, _m1);
    }
    add(ppoles); add(plamps);
  }

  // ---------- parking structure (rocket pickup on the roof) ----------
  {
    const x0 = 64, x1 = 148, z0 = -64, z1 = 8;
    const deck = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(x1 - x0, 0.5, z1 - z0), concMat);
    deck.position.set((x0 + x1) / 2, 5.75, (z0 + z1) / 2);
    deck.castShadow = deck.receiveShadow = true;
    add(deck);
    // columns + dark underbelly walls
    const colGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.45, 0.45, 5.6, 6);
    colGeo.translate(0, 2.8, 0);
    const cols = new THREE.InstancedMesh(colGeo, concMat, 24);
    let ci = 0;
    for (let x = x0 + 6; x <= x1 - 6; x += 18) for (let z = z0 + 6; z <= z1 - 6; z += 19) {
      if (ci >= 24) break;
      _m1.compose(_v1.set(x, 0, z), _q1.identity(), _v2.set(1, 1, 1));
      cols.setMatrixAt(ci++, _m1);
    }
    cols.count = ci;
    add(cols);
    const skirt = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(x1 - x0, 2.2, z1 - z0), darkMat);
    skirt.position.set((x0 + x1) / 2, 1.1, (z0 + z1) / 2);
    add(skirt);
    Colliders.addBox(x0, z0, x1, z1, 0, 2.6);   // block driving underneath
    // perimeter wall on the roof, with a gap at the ramp mouth (west side, z -40..-24)
    const wallH = 1.05, wy0 = 6, wy1 = 6 + wallH;
    const mkWall = (wx, wz, ww, wd) => {
      const w = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(ww, wallH, wd), concMat);
      w.position.set(wx, wy0 + wallH / 2, wz);
      w.castShadow = true;
      add(w);
      Colliders.addBox(wx - ww / 2, wz - wd / 2, wx + ww / 2, wz + wd / 2, wy0, wy1);
    };
    mkWall((x0 + x1) / 2, z0 + 0.25, x1 - x0, 0.5);
    mkWall((x0 + x1) / 2, z1 - 0.25, x1 - x0, 0.5);
    mkWall(x1 - 0.25, (z0 + z1) / 2, 0.5, z1 - z0);
    mkWall(x0 + 0.25, (z0 + (-40)) / 2, 0.5, -40 - z0);          // south of ramp gap
    mkWall(x0 + 0.25, ((-24) + z1) / 2, 0.5, z1 - (-24));        // north of ramp gap
    // ramp slab + side rails
    const ramp = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(17.7, 0.4, 16), concMat);
    ramp.position.set(55.5, 2.85, -32);
    ramp.rotation.z = Math.atan2(6, 17);
    ramp.castShadow = ramp.receiveShadow = true;
    add(ramp);
    for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
      const r = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(18.5, 0.9, 0.4), concMat);
      r.position.set(55.5, 3.6, -32 + side * 8);
      r.rotation.z = Math.atan2(6, 17);
      add(r);
    }
    Colliders.addBox(46, -41, 65, -39.6, 0, 7.4);
    Colliders.addBox(46, -24.4, 65, -23, 0, 7.4);
    // deck slab (camera/bullets), excluded for things standing on it; carved at the
    // ramp mouth so ascending cars' hull circles never touch the slab's west face
    Colliders.addBox(x0, z0, x1, -40, 5.45, 5.92);
    Colliders.addBox(x0, -24, x1, z1, 5.45, 5.92);
  }

  // ---------- plaza fountain ----------
  {
    const basin = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(4.6, 5, 0.9, 16), concMat);
    basin.position.set(175, 0.45, 0);
    basin.castShadow = true;
    add(basin);
    Colliders.addBox(170.5, -4.5, 179.5, 4.5, 0, 1);
    const wTex = TEX.dot.clone();
    wTex.wrapS = wTex.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
    CityFX.fountainMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: wTex, transparent: true, opacity: 0.7, color: 0x7fd4ff, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false });
    const jet = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.5, 1.3, 3, 8, 1, true), CityFX.fountainMat);
    jet.position.set(175, 2.2, 0);
    add(jet);
  }

  // ---------- bridge ----------
  {
    const xA = -293.5, xB = -126.5, SEG = 44, W = ROAD_W;
    const geo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(xB - xA, W, SEG, 1);
    geo.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);   // local x along bridge, z across
    const pos = geo.attributes.position;
    for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
      const wx = pos.getX(i) + (xA + xB) / 2;
      pos.setY(i, Math.sin(Math.PI * bridgeT(wx)) * 9 + 0.02);
    }
    geo.computeVertexNormals();
    const uv = geo.attributes.uv;
    for (let i = 0; i < uv.count; i++) uv.setX(i, uv.getX(i) * ((xB - xA) / 13));
    const deck = new THREE.Mesh(geo, roadMat);
    deck.position.set((xA + xB) / 2, 0, 0);
    deck.rotation.y = 0;
    deck.receiveShadow = true; deck.castShadow = true;
    // NOTE: plane local x must map to world x: geometry built with x along width param 1
    add(deck);
    // girder under deck + rails + colliders
    for (let x = xA + 6; x < xB; x += 12) {
      const h = Math.sin(Math.PI * bridgeT(x)) * 9;
      const g = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(12.4, 1.1, W + 1), metalMat);
      g.position.set(x + 3, h - 0.62, 0);
      add(g);
      for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
        const r = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(12.4, 1.0, 0.35), metalMat);
        r.position.set(x + 3, h + 0.85, side * (W / 2 + 0.35));
        add(r);
        Colliders.addBox(x - 3, side * (W / 2 + 0.2) - 0.4, x + 9, side * (W / 2 + 0.2) + 0.4, h - 0.6, h + 1.6);
      }
      // deck underside (camera/bullets) — must stay below the lowest deck point over its span
      const hMin = Math.min(Math.sin(Math.PI * bridgeT(x - 3)) * 9, Math.sin(Math.PI * bridgeT(x + 9)) * 9);
      if (hMin > 1.9) Colliders.addBox(x - 3, -6.5, x + 9, 6.5, hMin - 1.5, hMin - 0.45);
    }
    // pylons + cables
    for (const px of [-178, -232]) {
      const h = Math.sin(Math.PI * bridgeT(px)) * 9;
      for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
        const tower = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.6, 34, 1.6), metalMat);
        tower.position.set(px, h + 17 - 6, side * (W / 2 + 1.6));
        tower.castShadow = true;
        add(tower);
        Colliders.addBox(px - 0.9, side * (W / 2 + 1.6) - 0.9, px + 0.9, side * (W / 2 + 1.6) + 0.9, 0, h + 28);
        for (let c = 1; c <= 4; c++) {
          const cx = px + c * 13 * (px < -205 ? -1 : 1);
          if (cx < xA || cx > xB) continue;
          const cy = Math.sin(Math.PI * bridgeT(cx)) * 9 + 1.2;
          const ty = h + 28 - 6;
          const len = Math.hypot(cx - px, ty - cy);
          const cable = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.12, len, 0.12), darkMat);
          cable.position.set((px + cx) / 2, (ty + cy) / 2, side * (W / 2 + 1.6));
          cable.rotation.z = Math.atan2(cx - px, ty - cy);
          add(cable);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // ---------- industrial port ----------
  {
    // container stacks
    const cGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(6.1, 2.6, 2.5);
    cGeo.translate(0, 1.3, 0);
    const cMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: TEX.container, roughness: 0.7, envMapIntensity: 0.4 });
    const colors = [0xb5482a, 0x2a6db5, 0x3da05a, 0xc9852f, 0x35a3a3, 0x8a3d9e, 0x9e9e9e];
    const mats = [];
    const rows = [
      { x0: -420, z0: 22, nx: 8, nz: 4, ry: 0 },
      { x0: -420, z0: 70, nx: 9, nz: 3, ry: 0 },
      { x0: -350, z0: 40, nx: 6, nz: 4, ry: 0 },
      { x0: -420, z0: -60, nx: 7, nz: 3, ry: 0 },
      { x0: -345, z0: -75, nx: 5, nz: 3, ry: 0 },
    ];
    for (const r of rows) {
      for (let i = 0; i < r.nx; i++) for (let j = 0; j < r.nz; j++) {
        const stack = rndi(1, 3);
        for (let s = 0; s < stack; s++) {
          mats.push({ x: r.x0 + i * 6.6, z: r.z0 + j * 3.1, y: s * 2.62, c: pick(colors) });
        }
        Colliders.addBox(r.x0 + i * 6.6 - 3.05, r.z0 + j * 3.1 - 1.25, r.x0 + i * 6.6 + 3.05, r.z0 + j * 3.1 + 1.25, 0, stack * 2.62);
      }
    }
    const im = new THREE.InstancedMesh(cGeo, cMat, mats.length);
    mats.forEach((m, i) => {
      _m1.compose(_v1.set(m.x, m.y, m.z), _q1.identity(), _v2.set(1, 1, 1));
      im.setMatrixAt(i, _m1);
      im.setColorAt(i, _c1.setHex(m.c).multiplyScalar(rnd(0.7, 1.1)));
    });
    im.castShadow = im.receiveShadow = true;
    im.frustumCulled = false;
    add(im);

    // warehouses
    TEX.warehouse.repeat.set(6, 1);
    const wMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: TEX.warehouse, roughness: 0.75 });
    for (const [wx, wz, ww, wd] of [[-370, 110, 56, 16], [-370, -110, 56, 16], [-458, -20, 14, 50]]) {
      const wh = 11;
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(ww, wh, wd), wMat);
      m.position.set(wx, wh / 2, wz);
      m.castShadow = m.receiveShadow = true;
      add(m);
      const roof = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(wd / 2, wd / 2, ww, 3, 1, false, Math.PI, Math.PI), darkMat);
      roof.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
      roof.scale.y = 1; roof.scale.x = 0.4;
      roof.position.set(wx, wh, wz);
      add(roof);
      Colliders.addBox(wx - ww / 2, wz - wd / 2, wx + ww / 2, wz + wd / 2, 0, wh + 4);
    }

    // gantry cranes
    for (const [cx, cz] of [[-300, -55], [-292, 80]]) {
      const crane = new THREE.Group();
      for (const lx of [-7, 7]) for (const lz of [-5, 5]) {
        const leg = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.1, 19, 1.1), metalMat);
        leg.position.set(lx, 9.5, lz);
        leg.castShadow = true;
        crane.add(leg);
        Colliders.addBox(cx + lx - 0.7, cz + lz - 0.7, cx + lx + 0.7, cz + lz + 0.7, 0, 19);
      }
      const beamTop = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(30, 1.6, 2.2), metalMat);
      beamTop.position.set(4, 19.6, 0);
      beamTop.castShadow = true;
      crane.add(beamTop);
      const beamX = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(16, 1.4, 11.6), metalMat);
      beamX.position.set(0, 18.2, 0);
      crane.add(beamX);
      const cab = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.6, 2.2, 2.6), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xc9852f, roughness: 0.6 }));
      cab.position.set(10, 17.5, 0);
      crane.add(cab);
      const cable = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.12, 9, 0.12), darkMat);
      cable.position.set(12, 14.5, 0);
      crane.add(cable);
      const spreader = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(6.1, 0.7, 2.5), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xb5482a, roughness: 0.7 }));
      spreader.position.set(12, 9.6, 0);
      crane.add(spreader);
      crane.position.set(cx, 0, cz);
      crane.traverse(o => { o.matrixAutoUpdate = false; o.updateMatrix(); });
      S.add(crane);
    }

    // docked cargo ship silhouette (unreachable, west of port)
    const ship = new THREE.Group();
    const hull = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(110, 9, 16), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x2c3038, roughness: 0.8 }));
    hull.position.y = 2.5;
    ship.add(hull);
    const castle = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(10, 14, 12), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x8a8d96, roughness: 0.7 }));
    castle.position.set(-44, 13, 0);
    ship.add(castle);
    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
      const c = new THREE.Mesh(cGeo, cMat);
      c.position.set(-26 + i * 9, 7, rnd(-4, 4));
      ship.add(c);
    }
    const shipLight = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.5, 6, 5), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffe0b0, fog: false }));
