    shipLight.material.color.multiplyScalar(2);
    shipLight.position.set(-44, 21, 0);
    ship.add(shipLight);
    ship.position.set(-520, CONFIG.waterY, -30);
    ship.traverse(o => { o.matrixAutoUpdate = false; o.updateMatrix(); });
    S.add(ship);
  }

  // ---------- seawalls + boundaries ----------
  {
    const walls = [
      // main island west edge (gap at bridge z -8..8)
      [-137.5, -256, -135, -9, 1.4], [-137.5, 9, -135, 249.5, 1.4],
      // main island north + east
      [-137.5, -257.5, 377.5, -255, 1.4], [375, -256, 377.5, 249.5, 1.4],
      // port island perimeter (gap at bridge on east edge)
      [-265.5, -190, -263, -9, 1.4], [-265.5, 9, -263, 190, 1.4],
      [-471.5, -190, -469, 190, 1.4], [-471, -191.5, -263, -189, 1.4], [-471, 189, -263, 191.5, 1.4],
    ];
    const wallMat = concMat;
    for (const [ax, az, bx, bz, h] of walls) {
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(bx - ax, h, bz - az), wallMat);
      m.position.set((ax + bx) / 2, h / 2 - 0.1, (az + bz) / 2);
      add(m);
      Colliders.addBox(ax, az, bx, bz, -1, h + 1);
    }
    // invisible surf barrier along the beach (cars + player stay knee-deep), pier passes over it
    Colliders.addBox(-136, 274, 134, 277, -3, 2.5);
    Colliders.addBox(166, 274, 376, 277, -3, 2.5);
    Colliders.addBox(134, 256, 136, 348, -3, 2.5);
    Colliders.addBox(164, 256, 166, 348, -3, 2.5);
    // east/west ends of the beach strip
    Colliders.addBox(-137.5, 249.5, -135, 276, -3, 2.5);
    Colliders.addBox(375, 249.5, 377.5, 276, -3, 2.5);
  }

  Colliders.finalize();
}
// ============================================================ characters: skinned rigs, animation, ragdoll, pedestrians
const BONE = { HIPS: 0, TORSO: 1, HEAD: 2, ARML: 3, ARMR: 4, LEGL: 5, LEGR: 6, TAIL: 7 };
const charMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.82, envMapIntensity: 0.25 });

function buildCharGeometry(outfit) {
  // outfit: {skin, hair, top, bottom, shoes, cap, helmet, vest, pony}
  const parts = [];
  function part(w, h, d, x, y, z, bone, hex) {
    const g = new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d);
    g.translate(x, y, z);
    const n = g.attributes.position.count;
    const col = new Float32Array(n * 3);
    _c1.setHex(hex);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { col[i * 3] = _c1.r; col[i * 3 + 1] = _c1.g; col[i * 3 + 2] = _c1.b; }
    g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(col, 3));
    const si = new Uint16Array(n * 4), sw = new Float32Array(n * 4);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { si[i * 4] = bone; sw[i * 4] = 1; }
    g.setAttribute('skinIndex', new THREE.BufferAttribute(si, 4));
    g.setAttribute('skinWeight', new THREE.BufferAttribute(sw, 4));
    parts.push(g);
  }
  part(0.34, 0.2, 0.21, 0, 0.86, 0, BONE.HIPS, outfit.bottom);
  part(0.38, 0.5, 0.24, 0, 1.22, 0, BONE.TORSO, outfit.top);
  if (outfit.vest) part(0.4, 0.3, 0.27, 0, 1.26, 0, BONE.TORSO, outfit.vest);
  part(0.21, 0.23, 0.21, 0, 1.575, 0, BONE.HEAD, outfit.skin);
  part(0.23, 0.1, 0.23, 0, 1.71, -0.015, BONE.HEAD, outfit.hair);             // hair top
  part(0.23, 0.16, 0.06, 0, 1.6, -0.115, BONE.HEAD, outfit.hair);             // hair back
  if (outfit.cap) part(0.24, 0.06, 0.26, 0, 1.72, 0.02, BONE.HEAD, outfit.cap);
  if (outfit.helmet) { part(0.26, 0.16, 0.26, 0, 1.68, 0, BONE.HEAD, outfit.helmet); }
  if (outfit.pony) part(0.07, 0.34, 0.07, 0, 1.52, -0.18, BONE.TAIL, outfit.hair);
  // arms hang from shoulders at (±0.245, 1.41)
  part(0.11, 0.52, 0.13, -0.245, 1.16, 0, BONE.ARML, outfit.top);
  part(0.11, 0.52, 0.13, 0.245, 1.16, 0, BONE.ARMR, outfit.top);
  part(0.09, 0.1, 0.11, -0.245, 0.86, 0, BONE.ARML, outfit.skin);             // hands
  part(0.09, 0.1, 0.11, 0.245, 0.86, 0, BONE.ARMR, outfit.skin);
  // legs pivot at (±0.105, 0.86)
  part(0.135, 0.62, 0.16, -0.105, 0.55, 0, BONE.LEGL, outfit.bottom);
  part(0.135, 0.62, 0.16, 0.105, 0.55, 0, BONE.LEGR, outfit.bottom);
  part(0.14, 0.1, 0.24, -0.105, 0.05, 0.03, BONE.LEGL, outfit.shoes);
  part(0.14, 0.1, 0.24, 0.105, 0.05, 0.03, BONE.LEGR, outfit.shoes);
  return BufferGeometryUtils.mergeGeometries(parts);
}

const CHAR_GEOS = {};
{
  const skins = [0xc7946a, 0x8a6244, 0xe0b48c, 0x6b4a32, 0xd9a276];
  const tops = [0x7a4a5e, 0x3c5a72, 0x5e6e3c, 0x8a8a92, 0x4a3c72, 0xb56a3c, 0x2e6e62, 0x99435a];
  const bottoms = [0x2a2733, 0x3a3445, 0x4a4038, 0x33424a];
  CHAR_GEOS.civ = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
    CHAR_GEOS.civ.push(buildCharGeometry({
      skin: pick(skins), hair: pick([0x241b16, 0x4a3320, 0x6e5538, 0x16161c, 0x8a8078]),
      top: tops[i % tops.length], bottom: pick(bottoms), shoes: pick([0x1c1c22, 0x655d52, 0x8c8478]),
    }));
  }
  CHAR_GEOS.cop = buildCharGeometry({
    skin: pick(skins), hair: 0x1c1814, top: 0x222e4e, bottom: 0x181d30, shoes: 0x14141a, cap: 0x1a2340,
  });
  CHAR_GEOS.swat = buildCharGeometry({
    skin: 0xc7946a, hair: 0x111114, top: 0x24272e, bottom: 0x1d2026, shoes: 0x101014, helmet: 0x171a20, vest: 0x15171c,
  });
  CHAR_GEOS.player = buildCharGeometry({
    skin: 0xdca87e, hair: 0x2a1c16, top: 0xe9ded6, bottom: 0x2c2836, shoes: 0xd9d4cc, pony: true,
  });
}

// ---- weapon prototypes ----
function buildGunMeshes() {
  const dark = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x1d1f24, roughness: 0.5, metalness: 0.55 });
  const olive = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x4a4a36, roughness: 0.65 });
  const pistol = new THREE.Group();
  {
    const slide = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.05, 0.07, 0.26), dark);
    slide.position.set(0, 0.03, 0.1);
    const grip = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.05, 0.14, 0.07), dark);
    grip.position.set(0, -0.06, 0.0);
    pistol.add(slide, grip);
  }
  const smg = new THREE.Group();
  {
    const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.07, 0.1, 0.42), dark);
    body.position.set(0, 0.02, 0.16);
    const mag = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.05, 0.18, 0.07), dark);
    mag.position.set(0, -0.1, 0.12);
    const barrel = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.018, 0.018, 0.16, 6), dark);
    barrel.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    barrel.position.set(0, 0.04, 0.43);
    smg.add(body, mag, barrel);
  }
  const rocket = new THREE.Group();
  {
    const tube = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.075, 0.075, 0.95, 8), olive);
    tube.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    tube.position.set(0, 0.02, 0.22);
    const muzzleRing = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.095, 0.095, 0.1, 8), dark);
    muzzleRing.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    muzzleRing.position.set(0, 0.02, 0.66);
    const grip = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.05, 0.12, 0.06), dark);
    grip.position.set(0, -0.07, 0.1);
    rocket.add(tube, muzzleRing, grip);
  }
  return { pistol, smg, rocket };
}
const GUN_PROTO = buildGunMeshes();

// ---- character factory ----
function makeCharacter(kind, variant = 0) {
  const geo = kind === 'civ' ? CHAR_GEOS.civ[variant % CHAR_GEOS.civ.length] : CHAR_GEOS[kind];
  const mesh = new THREE.SkinnedMesh(geo, charMat);
  mesh.castShadow = true;
  const bones = [];
  const hips = new THREE.Bone(); hips.position.set(0, 0.86, 0);
  const torso = new THREE.Bone(); torso.position.set(0, 0.18, 0);
  const head = new THREE.Bone(); head.position.set(0, 0.50, 0);
  const armL = new THREE.Bone(); armL.position.set(-0.245, 0.37, 0);
  const armR = new THREE.Bone(); armR.position.set(0.245, 0.37, 0);
  const legL = new THREE.Bone(); legL.position.set(-0.105, 0, 0);
  const legR = new THREE.Bone(); legR.position.set(0.105, 0, 0);
  const tail = new THREE.Bone(); tail.position.set(0, 0.09, -0.16);
  hips.add(torso); torso.add(head); torso.add(armL); torso.add(armR);
  hips.add(legL); hips.add(legR); head.add(tail);
  bones.push(hips, torso, head, armL, armR, legL, legR, tail);
  // geometry parts are modeled in world-rest space; compensate bone offsets
  // (bind happens with bones at rest pose below)
  mesh.add(hips);
  mesh.bind(new THREE.Skeleton(bones));
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  group.add(mesh);
  // weapon mount on the right hand
  const mount = new THREE.Group();
  mount.position.set(0, -0.6, 0.03);
  mount.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
  armR.add(mount);
  const guns = { pistol: GUN_PROTO.pistol.clone(), smg: GUN_PROTO.smg.clone(), rocket: GUN_PROTO.rocket.clone() };
  for (const k in guns) { guns[k].visible = false; mount.add(guns[k]); }
  let glow = null;
  if (kind === 'player') {
    glow = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.39, 0.03, 0.25), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xff4f9e }));
    glow.material.color.multiplyScalar(1.8);
    glow.position.set(0, 0.12, 0.0);
    torso.add(glow);
  }
  return {
    kind, group, mesh, mount, guns,
    b: { hips, torso, head, armL, armR, legL, legR, tail },
    anim: { mode: 'idle', phase: rnd(6), speed: 0, aimPitch: 0, blend: 0 },
    rag: { on: false, t: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0, wx: 0, wy: 0, wz: 0, amp: 0, ph: [rnd(6), rnd(6), rnd(6), rnd(6)], om: [rnd(6, 10), rnd(6, 10), rnd(6, 10), rnd(6, 10)], settled: false },
    health: 30, active: false, gunKind: null,
  };
}
function charShowGun(ch, kindName) {
  for (const k in ch.guns) ch.guns[k].visible = (k === kindName);
  ch.gunKind = kindName;
}

// pose the rig; mode: idle | walk | run | flee | aim | aimwalk
function poseCharacter(ch, dt, moveSpeed) {
  const a = ch.anim, b = ch.b;
  const sp = moveSpeed !== undefined ? moveSpeed : a.speed;
  a.phase += dt * clamp(sp * 2.1, 1.2, 14);
  const p = a.phase;
  const swing = Math.sin(p);
  let legAmp = 0, armAmp = 0, lean = 0, bob = 0;
  if (a.mode === 'walk') { legAmp = 0.5; armAmp = 0.38; bob = 0.025; }
  else if (a.mode === 'run') { legAmp = 0.85; armAmp = 0.6; lean = 0.16; bob = 0.05; }
  else if (a.mode === 'flee') { legAmp = 0.95; armAmp = 0.2; lean = 0.2; bob = 0.06; }
  const k = clamp(sp / 2, 0, 1);
  legAmp *= Math.max(k, a.mode === 'idle' ? 0 : 0.5);
  b.legL.rotation.x = swing * legAmp;
  b.legR.rotation.x = -swing * legAmp;
  b.legL.rotation.z = 0; b.legR.rotation.z = 0;
  b.hips.position.y = 0.86 + Math.abs(Math.cos(p)) * bob;
  b.hips.rotation.set(lean, 0, 0);
  b.torso.rotation.set(0, swing * 0.06 * legAmp, 0);
  b.head.rotation.set(-lean * 0.7 + Math.sin(Tick.t * 1.1) * 0.02, 0, 0);
  if (a.mode === 'flee') {
    b.armL.rotation.set(-2.5 + Math.sin(p * 1.7) * 0.25, 0, -0.5 - Math.sin(p * 2.3) * 0.15);
    b.armR.rotation.set(-2.5 + Math.cos(p * 1.9) * 0.25, 0, 0.5 + Math.cos(p * 2.1) * 0.15);
  } else if (a.mode === 'aim' || a.mode === 'aimwalk') {
    const pitch = a.aimPitch;
    b.armR.rotation.set(-Math.PI / 2 + pitch, 0, 0);
    if (ch.gunKind === 'rocket') {
      b.armL.rotation.set(-Math.PI / 2 + pitch + 0.35, 0.5, 0.25);
      b.head.rotation.x = -0.08;
    } else if (ch.gunKind === 'smg') {
      b.armL.rotation.set(-Math.PI / 2 + pitch + 0.18, 0.55, 0.3);
    } else {
      b.armL.rotation.set(-0.4 - swing * 0.12 * armAmp, 0, 0.12);
    }
    if (a.mode === 'aimwalk') {
      b.legL.rotation.x = swing * 0.42;
      b.legR.rotation.x = -swing * 0.42;
    }
  } else {
    b.armL.rotation.set(-swing * armAmp, 0, 0.06 + Math.sin(Tick.t * 1.3) * 0.01);
    b.armR.rotation.set(swing * armAmp, 0, -0.06);
    if (a.mode === 'idle') {
      b.armL.rotation.x = Math.sin(Tick.t * 1.2) * 0.03;
      b.armR.rotation.x = -Math.sin(Tick.t * 1.2) * 0.03;
      b.hips.position.y = 0.86 + Math.sin(Tick.t * 1.2) * 0.008;
    }
  }
  // ponytail lag
  if (b.tail) {
    b.tail.rotation.x = clamp(0.25 + sp * 0.06 + Math.sin(p * 2) * 0.05 * k, 0, 0.8);
  }
}

function startRagdoll(ch, vx, vy, vz, spin) {
  const r = ch.rag;
  r.on = true; r.t = 0; r.settled = false;
  r.vx = vx; r.vy = Math.max(vy, 2.2); r.vz = vz;
  r.wx = rnd(-6, -2.5) * (Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1) * (0.6 + spin * 0.4);
  r.wy = rnd(-2, 2);
  r.wz = rnd(-3, 3) * (0.5 + spin * 0.5);
  r.amp = 1;
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { r.ph[i] = rnd(6); r.om[i] = rnd(6, 11); }
}
function updateRagdoll(ch, dt) {
  const r = ch.rag, g = ch.group, b = ch.b;
  r.t += dt;
  if (!r.settled) {
    r.vy -= 20 * dt;
    g.position.x += r.vx * dt;
    g.position.y += r.vy * dt;
    g.position.z += r.vz * dt;
    const gy = groundHeightAt(g.position.x, g.position.z);
    if (g.position.y < gy + 0.45 && r.vy <= 0) {
      g.position.y = gy + 0.45;
      if (r.vy < -3.5) { AudioFX.thud(g.position, Math.min(1, -0.05 * r.vy)); r.vy = -r.vy * 0.3; }
      else r.vy = 0;
      r.vx *= Math.exp(-5 * dt); r.vz *= Math.exp(-5 * dt);
      r.wx *= Math.exp(-7 * dt); r.wy *= Math.exp(-7 * dt); r.wz *= Math.exp(-7 * dt);
    } else {
      r.wx *= Math.exp(-1.2 * dt); r.wz *= Math.exp(-1.2 * dt);
    }
    const wMag = Math.hypot(r.wx, r.wy, r.wz);
    if (wMag > 1e-4) {
      _v1.set(r.wx / wMag, r.wy / wMag, r.wz / wMag);
      _q1.setFromAxisAngle(_v1, wMag * dt);
      g.quaternion.premultiply(_q1);
    }
    const speed2 = r.vx * r.vx + r.vz * r.vz + r.vy * r.vy;
    const w2 = r.wx * r.wx + r.wy * r.wy + r.wz * r.wz;
    if (r.t > 0.5 && speed2 < 0.5 && w2 < 0.6 && g.position.y < gy + 0.5) r.settled = true;
  } else {
    // ease into a flat lying pose
    _v1.set(1, 0, 0).applyQuaternion(g.quaternion);
    const yaw = Math.atan2(_v1.z, _v1.x);
    _q1.setFromEuler(ch.mesh.userData._e || (ch.mesh.userData._e = new THREE.Euler()));
    ch.mesh.userData._e.set(-Math.PI / 2, 0, -yaw, 'YXZ');
    _q1.setFromEuler(ch.mesh.userData._e);
    g.quaternion.slerp(_q1, 1 - Math.exp(-6 * dt));
    const gy = groundHeightAt(g.position.x, g.position.z);
    g.position.y = damp(g.position.y, gy + 0.32, 6, dt);
  }
  // flailing limbs, decaying
  const e = Math.exp(-1.6 * r.t) * r.amp;
  b.armL.rotation.set(-0.4 + Math.sin(r.om[0] * r.t + r.ph[0]) * 1.1 * e, 0, -0.3 * e);
  b.armR.rotation.set(-0.4 + Math.sin(r.om[1] * r.t + r.ph[1]) * 1.1 * e, 0, 0.3 * e);
  b.legL.rotation.x = Math.sin(r.om[2] * r.t + r.ph[2]) * 0.9 * e;
  b.legR.rotation.x = Math.sin(r.om[3] * r.t + r.ph[3]) * 0.9 * e;
  b.head.rotation.x = Math.sin(r.om[0] * r.t * 1.3 + r.ph[1]) * 0.4 * e;
  b.hips.rotation.set(0, 0, 0);
  b.hips.position.y = 0.45;
}

// ============================================================ pedestrians
const Peds = {
  list: [], imp: null, impData: [], MAXP: 26,
  init() {
    for (let i = 0; i < this.MAXP; i++) {
      const ch = makeCharacter('civ', i);
      ch.group.visible = false;
      scene.add(ch.group);
      ch.seg = null; ch.segT = 0; ch.segDir = 1; ch.speed = rnd(1.0, 1.6);
      ch.state = 'off'; ch.fleeX = 0; ch.fleeZ = 0; ch.deadT = 0;
      this.list.push(ch);
    }
    // far impostors
    const N = 60;
    const g = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.9, 1.8);
    g.translate(0, 0.9, 0);
    const mat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      uniforms: { uMap: { value: TEX.peopleAtlas }, uFogColor: { value: CONFIG.fogColor }, uFogDensity: { value: CONFIG.fogDensity } },
      vertexShader: /* glsl */`
        varying vec2 vUv;
        varying float vFogDepth;
        varying float vCol;
        void main() {
          vec3 ipos = vec3(instanceMatrix[3][0], instanceMatrix[3][1], instanceMatrix[3][2]);
          vCol = instanceMatrix[0][0];   // atlas column smuggled in matrix slot [0][0]
          float colId = floor(vCol + 0.5);
          vUv = vec2((uv.x + colId) * 0.25, uv.y);
          vec3 camRight = normalize(vec3(viewMatrix[0][0], viewMatrix[1][0], viewMatrix[2][0]));
          vec3 wp = ipos + camRight * position.x + vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0) * position.y;
          vec4 mv = viewMatrix * vec4(wp, 1.0);
          vFogDepth = -mv.z;
          gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mv;
        }`,
      fragmentShader: /* glsl */`
        uniform sampler2D uMap;
        uniform vec3 uFogColor;
        uniform float uFogDensity;
        varying vec2 vUv;
        varying float vFogDepth;
        void main() {
          vec4 c = texture2D(uMap, vUv);
          if (c.a < 0.5) discard;
          float fog = 1.0 - exp(-uFogDensity * uFogDensity * vFogDepth * vFogDepth * 4.0);
          gl_FragColor = vec4(mix(c.rgb * 0.75, uFogColor, clamp(fog, 0.0, 1.0)), 1.0);
        }`,
      transparent: false,
    });
    this.imp = new THREE.InstancedMesh(g, mat, N);
    this.imp.frustumCulled = false;
    scene.add(this.imp);
    for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
      const seg = pick(CityData.sidewalks);
      this.impData.push({ seg, t: rnd(1), dir: Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1, col: rndi(0, 3), sp: rnd(0.01, 0.03) });
    }
  },
  spawnOne(px, pz) {
    let ch = null;
    for (const c of this.list) if (c.state === 'off') { ch = c; break; }
    if (!ch) return;
    // pick a sidewalk segment 35..120m from player
    for (let tries = 0; tries < 14; tries++) {
      const seg = pick(CityData.sidewalks);
      const t = rnd(1);
      const x = lerp(seg.ax, seg.bx, t), z = lerp(seg.az, seg.bz, t);
      const d = Math.hypot(x - px, z - pz);
      if (d < 30 || d > 110) continue;
      ch.seg = seg; ch.segT = t; ch.segDir = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1;
      ch.state = 'walk'; ch.active = true; ch.health = 30;
      ch.rag.on = false; ch.deadT = 0;
      ch.group.visible = true;
      ch.group.position.set(x, groundHeightAt(x, z), z);
      ch.group.quaternion.identity();
      ch.mesh.position.set(0, 0, 0);
      ch.anim.mode = 'walk';
      return;
    }
  },
  alarm(x, z, r) {
    for (const ch of this.list) {
      if (ch.state !== 'walk') continue;
      const dx = ch.group.position.x - x, dz = ch.group.position.z - z;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
      if (d < r) {
        ch.state = 'flee';
        ch.anim.mode = 'flee';
        const inv = d > 0.5 ? 1 / d : 1;
        ch.fleeX = dx * inv; ch.fleeZ = dz * inv;
        AudioFX.scream(ch.group.position);
      }
    }
  },
  kill(ch, vx, vy, vz, spin, byPlayer) {
    if (ch.state === 'dead' || ch.state === 'off') return;
    ch.state = 'dead';
    ch.deadT = 0;
    startRagdoll(ch, vx, vy, vz, spin);
    if (byPlayer) Wanted.crime('killPed', ch.group.position);
    this.alarm(ch.group.position.x, ch.group.position.z, 40);
  },
  update(dt, px, pz) {
    // keep crowd density up near the player
    let activeCount = 0;
    for (const c of this.list) if (c.state !== 'off') activeCount++;
    if (activeCount < this.MAXP - 2 && Tick.frame % 9 === 0) { this.spawnOne(px, pz); this.spawnOne(px, pz); }
    for (const ch of this.list) {
      if (ch.state === 'off') continue;
      const g = ch.group;
      const d2p = Math.hypot(g.position.x - px, g.position.z - pz);
      if (d2p > 150) { ch.state = 'off'; ch.group.visible = false; ch.active = false; continue; }
      if (ch.state === 'dead') {
        ch.deadT += dt;
        updateRagdoll(ch, dt);
        if (ch.deadT > 9) {
          g.position.y -= dt * 0.5;
          if (ch.deadT > 10.5) { ch.state = 'off'; ch.group.visible = false; ch.active = false; }
        }
        continue;
      }
      if (ch.state === 'walk') {
        const seg = ch.seg;
        const len = Math.hypot(seg.bx - seg.ax, seg.bz - seg.az);
        ch.segT += (ch.speed * ch.segDir * dt) / Math.max(len, 1);
        if (ch.segT > 1) { ch.segT = 1; ch.segDir = -1; }
        if (ch.segT < 0) { ch.segT = 0; ch.segDir = 1; }
        const x = lerp(seg.ax, seg.bx, ch.segT), z = lerp(seg.az, seg.bz, ch.segT);
        const dirx = (seg.bx - seg.ax) / len * ch.segDir, dirz = (seg.bz - seg.az) / len * ch.segDir;
        g.position.x = x; g.position.z = z;
        g.position.y = groundHeightAt(x, z);
        g.rotation.y = Math.atan2(dirx, dirz);
        ch.anim.speed = ch.speed;
        poseCharacter(ch, dt, ch.speed);
      } else if (ch.state === 'flee') {
        const sp = 4.6;
        // weave while fleeing
        const wob = Math.sin(Tick.t * 3.1 + ch.anim.phase) * 0.5;
        let mx = ch.fleeX - ch.fleeZ * wob * 0.4, mz = ch.fleeZ + ch.fleeX * wob * 0.4;
        const ml = Math.hypot(mx, mz) || 1;
        mx /= ml; mz /= ml;
        g.position.x += mx * sp * dt;
        g.position.z += mz * sp * dt;
        const res = Colliders.resolveCircle(g.position.x, g.position.z, 0.4, g.position.y + 0.2, g.position.y + 1.6);
        g.position.x = res.x; g.position.z = res.z;
        if (res.hit) { // slide along, bias away
          ch.fleeX += res.nx * 0.8; ch.fleeZ += res.nz * 0.8;
          const l = Math.hypot(ch.fleeX, ch.fleeZ) || 1;
          ch.fleeX /= l; ch.fleeZ /= l;
        }
        g.position.y = groundHeightAt(g.position.x, g.position.z);
        g.rotation.y = Math.atan2(mx, mz);
        ch.anim.speed = sp;
        poseCharacter(ch, dt, sp);
        if (d2p > 130) { ch.state = 'off'; ch.group.visible = false; ch.active = false; }
      }
    }
    // far impostors (cheap shuffle, update every 3rd frame)
    if (Tick.frame % 3 === 0) {
      for (let i = 0; i < this.impData.length; i++) {
        const d = this.impData[i];
        const len = Math.hypot(d.seg.bx - d.seg.ax, d.seg.bz - d.seg.az) || 1;
        d.t += d.sp * d.dir * 3 * dt;
        if (d.t > 1) { d.t = 1; d.dir = -1; } else if (d.t < 0) { d.t = 0; d.dir = 1; }
        const x = lerp(d.seg.ax, d.seg.bx, d.t), z = lerp(d.seg.az, d.seg.bz, d.t);
        const dp = Math.hypot(x - px, z - pz);
        _m1.identity();
        _m1.elements[0] = d.col;                 // pack atlas column into matrix slot [0][0]
        // hide impostors close to the player (real peds live there)
        _m1.setPosition(x, dp < 85 ? -80 : groundHeightAt(x, z), z);
        this.imp.setMatrixAt(i, _m1);
      }
      this.imp.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    }
  },
};
// ============================================================ vehicles: models, arcade physics, traffic
const VEH = {
  sedan:  { top: 30, rev: 9,  acc: 8,    brk: 18, grip: 9,   hb: 2.5, steer: 2.0, drift: 0.4, len: 4.6, wid: 2.0, wb: 1.45, wr: 0.34, ws: 1,   seatH: 1.0, hp: 90 },
  taxi:   { top: 32, rev: 9,  acc: 8.5,  brk: 18, grip: 9,   hb: 2.5, steer: 2.0, drift: 0.4, len: 4.6, wid: 2.0, wb: 1.45, wr: 0.34, ws: 1,   seatH: 1.0, hp: 90 },
  sports: { top: 52, rev: 12, acc: 14,   brk: 28, grip: 8,   hb: 1.6, steer: 2.6, drift: 1.4, len: 4.4, wid: 2.0, wb: 1.42, wr: 0.33, ws: 1,   seatH: 0.85, hp: 100 },
  muscle: { top: 46, rev: 11, acc: 12.5, brk: 22, grip: 5.5, hb: 1.0, steer: 2.2, drift: 1.1, len: 4.9, wid: 2.1, wb: 1.55, wr: 0.36, ws: 1.05, seatH: 0.95, hp: 110 },
  police: { top: 47, rev: 11, acc: 11.5, brk: 24, grip: 7.5, hb: 2.0, steer: 2.4, drift: 0.8, len: 4.8, wid: 2.1, wb: 1.5,  wr: 0.34, ws: 1,   seatH: 1.0, hp: 100 },
  swat:   { top: 34, rev: 8,  acc: 6.5,  brk: 16, grip: 10,  hb: 4.0, steer: 1.6, drift: 0.2, len: 5.6, wid: 2.4, wb: 1.9,  wr: 0.4,  ws: 1.25, seatH: 1.4, hp: 220 },
};
const GLASS = 0x10131c, TRIM = 0x1a1c22, CHROME = 0x8a8f9a;

function buildCarGeo(model, paint) {
  const parts = [];
  function part(w, h, d, x, y, z, hex) {
    const g = new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d);
    g.translate(x, y, z);
    const n = g.attributes.position.count;
    const col = new Float32Array(n * 3);
    _c1.setHex(hex);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { col[i * 3] = _c1.r; col[i * 3 + 1] = _c1.g; col[i * 3 + 2] = _c1.b; }
    g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(col, 3));
    parts.push(g);
  }
  if (model === 'sports') {
    part(1.96, 0.42, 4.4, 0, 0.52, 0, paint);                    // low hull
    part(1.7, 0.36, 1.9, 0, 0.9, -0.25, GLASS);                  // canopy
    part(1.74, 0.05, 1.0, 0, 1.1, -0.45, paint);                 // roof sliver
    part(1.9, 0.1, 0.5, 0, 0.85, -2.05, paint);                  // spoiler
    part(2.0, 0.18, 0.3, 0, 0.36, 2.12, TRIM);
    part(2.0, 0.18, 0.3, 0, 0.36, -2.12, TRIM);
  } else if (model === 'muscle') {
    part(2.06, 0.55, 4.9, 0, 0.6, 0, paint);
    part(1.86, 0.46, 1.7, 0, 1.1, -0.5, GLASS);
    part(1.9, 0.06, 0.9, 0, 1.36, -0.55, paint);
    part(0.5, 0.12, 1.4, 0, 0.92, 1.4, TRIM);                    // hood scoop
    part(2.1, 0.2, 0.32, 0, 0.4, 2.55, CHROME);
    part(2.1, 0.2, 0.32, 0, 0.4, -2.55, CHROME);
  } else if (model === 'swat') {
    part(2.36, 1.1, 5.6, 0, 0.95, 0, paint);
    part(2.2, 0.85, 1.5, 0, 1.95, 0.9, paint);                   // high cab
    part(2.0, 0.5, 0.1, 0, 1.95, 1.68, GLASS);
    part(2.4, 0.35, 0.5, 0, 0.55, 2.95, TRIM);                   // bullbar
    part(2.36, 0.5, 5.0, 0, 1.78, -0.7, paint);
  } else {
    // sedan family (sedan, taxi, police)
    part(1.96, 0.5, 4.6, 0, 0.57, 0, paint);                     // hull
    part(1.78, 0.5, 2.2, 0, 1.05, -0.2, GLASS);                  // cabin glass
    part(1.82, 0.07, 1.6, 0, 1.32, -0.25, model === 'police' ? 0xf2f2f2 : paint); // roof
    part(2.0, 0.18, 0.3, 0, 0.38, 2.2, TRIM);
    part(2.0, 0.18, 0.3, 0, 0.38, -2.2, TRIM);
    if (model === 'police') {
      part(2.0, 0.3, 1.4, 0, 0.62, 0.1, 0xf2f2f2);               // white door band
    }
    if (model === 'taxi') part(0.7, 0.16, 0.36, 0, 1.44, -0.25, 0xfFD75e);
  }
  return BufferGeometryUtils.mergeGeometries(parts);
}
function buildLightGeo(model) {
  const parts = [];
  function lamp(w, h, d, x, y, z, r, g, b) {
    const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d);
    geo.translate(x, y, z);
    const n = geo.attributes.position.count;
    const col = new Float32Array(n * 3);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { col[i * 3] = r; col[i * 3 + 1] = g; col[i * 3 + 2] = b; }
    geo.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(col, 3));
    parts.push(geo);
  }
  const d = VEH[model];
  const fy = model === 'swat' ? 0.85 : 0.6, fz = d.len / 2 - 0.02;
  lamp(0.42, 0.16, 0.1, -d.wid / 2 + 0.34, fy, fz, 1.7, 1.6, 1.35);
  lamp(0.42, 0.16, 0.1, d.wid / 2 - 0.34, fy, fz, 1.7, 1.6, 1.35);
  lamp(0.42, 0.14, 0.1, -d.wid / 2 + 0.34, fy, -fz, 1.9, 0.12, 0.07);
  lamp(0.42, 0.14, 0.1, d.wid / 2 - 0.34, fy, -fz, 1.9, 0.12, 0.07);
  return BufferGeometryUtils.mergeGeometries(parts);
}

const CAR_VARIANTS = {
  sedan: [0xcfd2d8, 0x32363f, 0x8a2a32, 0x2a4a8a],
  taxi: [0xe8b818],
  sports: [0xff2e8a, 0xe8e8ee, 0x18c7d8],
  muscle: [0xd87f18, 0x23262e, 0x4a8a3a],
  police: [0x14161c],
  swat: [0x23262e],
};

const Vehicles = {
  list: [], buckets: {}, wheelMesh: null, barMesh: null, dynStreaks: null,
  MAXV: 96, _active: [], _nearParked: [], _nearParkedTick: 0,
  playerVeh: null, heroSports: null,
  skid: null, skidHead: 0, SKID_N: 1400,

  init() {
    const bodyMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.32, metalness: 0.25, envMapIntensity: 1.25 });
    const lightMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ vertexColors: true, fog: true });
    lightMat.color.setScalar(1.7);
    for (const model in VEH) {
      CAR_VARIANTS[model].forEach((paint, vi) => {
        const key = model + vi;
        const cap = model === 'sedan' ? 28 : 18;
        const body = new THREE.InstancedMesh(buildCarGeo(model, paint), bodyMat, cap);
        body.castShadow = true;
        body.frustumCulled = false;
        body.count = cap;
        const lights = new THREE.InstancedMesh(buildLightGeo(model), lightMat, cap);
        lights.frustumCulled = false;
        lights.count = cap;
        for (let i = 0; i < cap; i++) {
          _m1.makeScale(0, 0, 0);
          body.setMatrixAt(i, _m1); lights.setMatrixAt(i, _m1);
          body.setColorAt(i, _c1.setRGB(1, 1, 1));
        }
        scene.add(body); scene.add(lights);
        this.buckets[key] = { body, lights, free: [], model };
        for (let i = cap - 1; i >= 0; i--) this.buckets[key].free.push(i);
      });
    }
    // wheels (global)
    const wGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.34, 0.34, 0.25, 10);
    wGeo.rotateZ(Math.PI / 2);
    const wMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x15161a, roughness: 0.85 });
    this.wheelMesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(wGeo, wMat, this.MAXV * 4);
    this.wheelMesh.frustumCulled = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < this.MAXV * 4; i++) { _m1.makeScale(0, 0, 0); this.wheelMesh.setMatrixAt(i, _m1); }
    scene.add(this.wheelMesh);
    // police lightbars (slot index parallels vehicle pool index)
    const barGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.1, 0.13, 0.42);
    const barMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ fog: false });
    this.barMesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(barGeo, barMat, 24);
    this.barMesh.frustumCulled = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < 24; i++) { _m1.makeScale(0, 0, 0); this.barMesh.setMatrixAt(i, _m1); this.barMesh.setColorAt(i, _c1.setRGB(0, 0, 0)); }
    scene.add(this.barMesh);
    this.barFree = [];
    for (let i = 23; i >= 0; i--) this.barFree.push(i);
    // dynamic under-car light streaks
    const sGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1, 1);
    sGeo.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);
    sGeo.translate(0, 0, 0.5);
    const sMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: TEX.streak, transparent: true, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false });
    this.dynStreaks = new THREE.InstancedMesh(sGeo, sMat, 80);
    this.dynStreaks.frustumCulled = false;
    this.dynStreaks.renderOrder = 4;
    for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) { _m1.makeScale(0, 0, 0); this.dynStreaks.setMatrixAt(i, _m1); this.dynStreaks.setColorAt(i, _c1.setRGB(0, 0, 0)); }
    scene.add(this.dynStreaks);
    // skid marks ring buffer
    {
      const N = this.SKID_N;
      const pos = new Float32Array(N * 4 * 3);
      const extra = new Float32Array(N * 4 * 2); // birth, alpha
      const idx = new Uint32Array(N * 6);
      for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
        idx[i * 6] = i * 4; idx[i * 6 + 1] = i * 4 + 1; idx[i * 6 + 2] = i * 4 + 2;
        idx[i * 6 + 3] = i * 4; idx[i * 6 + 4] = i * 4 + 2; idx[i * 6 + 5] = i * 4 + 3;
      }
      const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
      g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
      g.setAttribute('aEx', new THREE.BufferAttribute(extra, 2));
      g.setIndex(new THREE.BufferAttribute(idx, 1));
      const mat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
        uniforms: { uT: { value: 0 } },
        vertexShader: `
          attribute vec2 aEx;
          uniform float uT;
          varying float vA;
          void main() {
            float age = uT - aEx.x;
            vA = aEx.y * clamp(1.0 - age / 14.0, 0.0, 1.0);
            gl_Position = projectionMatrix * modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
          }`,
        fragmentShader: `
          varying float vA;
          void main() { gl_FragColor = vec4(0.015, 0.015, 0.02, vA); }`,
        transparent: true, depthWrite: false,
      });
      this.skid = new THREE.Mesh(g, mat);
      this.skid.frustumCulled = false;
      this.skid.renderOrder = 3;
      scene.add(this.skid);
    }
    // vehicle pool
    for (let i = 0; i < this.MAXV; i++) {
      this.list.push({
        active: false, model: 'sedan', def: VEH.sedan, key: '', slot: -1, poolI: i,
        x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, heading: 0, vx: 0, vz: 0, vy: 0, airborne: false,
        steer: 0, inSteer: 0, inThrottle: 0, inBrake: 0, inHand: false,
        spin: 0, health: 100, state: 'off', burnT: 0, smokeT: 0, deadT: 0, honkT: 0,
        pitch: 0, roll: 0, sirenOn: false, barSlot: -1, isPolice: false,
        ai: { edge: null, dir: 1, t: 0, lane: LANE_OFF, cruise: 12, blockedT: 0, revT: 0, panic: 0 },
        cop: null, pitT: 0, lastSkid: 0,
      });
    }
  },

  alloc(model, variant) {
    for (const v of this.list) {
      if (!v.active) {
        const variants = CAR_VARIANTS[model];
        const vi = variant !== undefined ? variant : rndi(0, variants.length - 1);
        const key = model + vi;
        const bucket = this.buckets[key];
        if (!bucket.free.length) return null;
        v.slot = bucket.free.pop();
        v.key = key; v.model = model; v.def = VEH[model];
        v.active = true; v.health = v.def.hp; v.burnT = 0; v.deadT = 0; v.smokeT = 0;
        v.vx = v.vz = v.vy = 0; v.steer = 0; v.spin = 0; v.pitch = 0; v.roll = 0;
        v.inThrottle = 0; v.inBrake = 0; v.inHand = false; v.airborne = false;
        v.sirenOn = false; v.isPolice = model === 'police' || model === 'swat';
        v.cop = null; v.pitT = 0; v.lastPlayerHit = -999; v.wasStolen = false;
        this.buckets[key].body.setColorAt(v.slot, _c1.setRGB(1, 1, 1));
        this.buckets[key].body.instanceColor.needsUpdate = true;
        if (v.isPolice && this.barFree.length) v.barSlot = this.barFree.pop();
        else v.barSlot = -1;
        return v;
      }
    }
    return null;
  },
  release(v) {
    if (!v.active) return;
    v.active = false; v.state = 'off';
    _m1.makeScale(0, 0, 0);
    const b = this.buckets[v.key];
    b.body.setMatrixAt(v.slot, _m1);
    b.lights.setMatrixAt(v.slot, _m1);
    b.body.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    b.lights.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    b.free.push(v.slot);
    for (let w = 0; w < 4; w++) this.wheelMesh.setMatrixAt(v.poolI * 4 + w, _m1);
    this.wheelMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    if (v.barSlot >= 0) {
      this.barMesh.setMatrixAt(v.barSlot, _m1);
      this.barMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
      this.barFree.push(v.barSlot);
      v.barSlot = -1;
    }
    if (v === this.playerVeh) this.playerVeh = null;
  },
  spawn(model, x, z, heading, state, variant) {
    const v = this.alloc(model, variant);
    if (!v) return null;
    v.x = x; v.z = z; v.heading = heading;
    v.y = groundHeightAt(x, z);
    v.state = state || 'free';
    this.render(v);
    return v;
  },

  // ---------- physics ----------
  step(v, dt) {
    const d = v.def;
    const fx = Math.sin(v.heading), fz = Math.cos(v.heading);
    const rx = -fz, rz = fx;
    let vF = v.vx * fx + v.vz * fz;
    let vR = v.vx * rx + v.vz * rz;
    const throttle = clamp(v.inThrottle, -1, 1), brake = clamp(v.inBrake, 0, 1);
    if (throttle > 0) vF += throttle * d.acc * dt;
    else if (throttle < 0) vF += throttle * (vF > 0.5 ? d.brk : d.acc * 0.6) * dt;
    if (brake > 0) {
      const dec = d.brk * brake * dt;
      if (Math.abs(vF) <= dec) vF = 0; else vF -= Math.sign(vF) * dec;
    }
    vF = clamp(vF, -d.rev, d.top);
    vF -= vF * (d.acc / d.top) * dt;
    const grip = v.inHand ? d.hb : d.grip;
    vR *= Math.exp(-grip * dt);
    // steering
    v.steer = damp(v.steer, clamp(v.inSteer, -1, 1), Math.abs(v.inSteer) > 0.05 ? 9 : 13, dt);
    const aspd = Math.abs(vF);
    const steerEff = d.steer * Math.min(aspd / 6, 1) * (1 - 0.55 * clamp(aspd / d.top, 0, 1));
    v.heading += v.steer * steerEff * Math.sign(vF || 1) * dt;
    if (v.inHand) v.heading += v.steer * d.drift * clamp(aspd / 15, 0, 1) * dt;
    if (Math.abs(vR) > 4) vF *= Math.exp(-0.35 * dt);
    const nfx = Math.sin(v.heading), nfz = Math.cos(v.heading);
    v.vx = nfx * vF + (-nfz) * vR;
    v.vz = nfz * vF + (nfx) * vR;
    v.x += v.vx * dt;
    v.z += v.vz * dt;
    // vertical
    const gy = groundHeightAt(v.x, v.z);
    if (v.y > gy + 0.12) {
      v.airborne = true;
      v.vy -= 20 * dt;
      v.y += v.vy * dt;
      if (v.y <= gy) {
        v.y = gy; v.airborne = false;
        if (v.vy < -4) { this.damage(v, -v.vy * 1.2, v.x, v.z, false); Shake.add(v === this.playerVeh ? 0.18 : 0); }
        v.vy = 0;
      }
    } else {
      const rise = gy - v.y;
      if (rise > 0) { v.y = gy; v.vy = Math.max(v.vy, rise / Math.max(dt, 0.001) * 0.55); }
      else { v.y = damp(v.y, gy, 14, dt); if (rise < -0.4) { v.vy = 0; v.airborne = true; } else v.vy = 0; }
    }
    // visual pitch/roll
    const ahead = groundHeightAt(v.x + nfx * 2, v.z + nfz * 2);
    const behind = groundHeightAt(v.x - nfx * 2, v.z - nfz * 2);
    v.pitch = damp(v.pitch, Math.atan2(behind - ahead, 4) + clamp((throttle > 0 ? -1 : 1) * aspd * 0.0012 * d.acc, -0.05, 0.05), 8, dt);
    v.roll = damp(v.roll, clamp(vR * 0.014, -0.1, 0.1), 8, dt);
    // static collisions: two circles along the hull
    const halfL = d.len * 0.5 - 0.9;
    for (const off of [halfL, -halfL]) {
      const cx = v.x + nfx * off, cz = v.z + nfz * off;
      const res = Colliders.resolveCircle(cx, cz, d.wid * 0.5 + 0.12, v.y + 0.3, v.y + 1.2);
      if (res.hit) {
        v.x += res.x - cx; v.z += res.z - cz;
        const vn = v.vx * res.nx + v.vz * res.nz;
        if (vn < 0) {
          v.vx -= res.nx * vn * 1.3;
          v.vz -= res.nz * vn * 1.3;
          const impact = -vn;
          if (impact > 3) {
            this.damage(v, impact * 1.7, cx, cz, false);
            v.spin += (off > 0 ? 1 : -1) * (res.nx * nfz - res.nz * nfx) * impact * 0.05;
            AudioFX.crash(v.x, v.z, Math.min(1, impact / 16));
            Particles.impactSparks(cx, v.y + 0.5, cz, res.nx, 0, res.nz, Math.min(14, impact));
            if (v === this.playerVeh) Shake.add(Math.min(0.5, impact * 0.03));
          }
        }
      }
    }
    v.heading += v.spin * dt;
    v.spin *= Math.exp(-3 * dt);
    // skid marks + smoke + screech
    const slipping = (Math.abs(vR) > 3.4 || (v.inHand && aspd > 8)) && !v.airborne;
    if (slipping) {
      const bx = v.x - nfx * d.wb, bz = v.z - nfz * d.wb;
      const sd = Math.hypot(v.vx, v.vz) * dt * 1.3 + 0.18;
      const mvx = v.vx / (Math.hypot(v.vx, v.vz) || 1), mvz = v.vz / (Math.hypot(v.vx, v.vz) || 1);
      for (const side of [-0.85, 0.85]) {
        this.writeSkid(bx + (-nfz) * side, v.y + 0.04, bz + nfx * side, mvx, mvz, sd, clamp(Math.abs(vR) / 7, 0.5, 0.92));
      }
      if (Tick.frame % 3 === 0) {
        Particles.tireSmoke(bx, v.y + 0.25, bz);
      }
    }
    return slipping ? Math.abs(vR) : 0;
  },

  writeSkid(x, y, z, dx, dz, len, alpha) {
    const i = this.skidHead;
    this.skidHead = (this.skidHead + 1) % this.SKID_N;
    const pos = this.skid.geometry.attributes.position;
    const ex = this.skid.geometry.attributes.aEx;
    const px = -dz * 0.13, pz = dx * 0.13;
    const hx = dx * len * 0.5, hz = dz * len * 0.5;
    const arr = pos.array, o = i * 12;
    arr[o] = x - hx + px; arr[o + 1] = y; arr[o + 2] = z - hz + pz;
    arr[o + 3] = x - hx - px; arr[o + 4] = y; arr[o + 5] = z - hz - pz;
    arr[o + 6] = x + hx - px; arr[o + 7] = y; arr[o + 8] = z + hz - pz;
    arr[o + 9] = x + hx + px; arr[o + 10] = y; arr[o + 11] = z + hz + pz;
    const eo = i * 8, ea = ex.array;
    for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) { ea[eo + k * 2] = Tick.t; ea[eo + k * 2 + 1] = alpha; }
    pos.addUpdateRange(o, 12); pos.needsUpdate = true;
    ex.addUpdateRange(eo, 8); ex.needsUpdate = true;
  },

  damage(v, amount, hx, hz, byPlayer) {
    if (v.state === 'dead' || !v.active) return;
    if (byPlayer) v.lastPlayerHit = Tick.t;
    if (v === this.playerVeh) amount *= 0.4;   // the player's ride is tougher than AI cars
    v.health -= amount;
    const b = this.buckets[v.key];
    const t = clamp(v.health / v.def.hp, 0, 1);
    b.body.setColorAt(v.slot, _c1.setRGB(0.25 + 0.75 * t, 0.25 + 0.75 * t, 0.25 + 0.75 * t));
    b.body.instanceColor.needsUpdate = true;
    if (v.health <= 0) this.explode(v);
    else if (v.health < 32 && v.burnT === 0) v.burnT = 0.01;  // catch fire
  },
  damageRadius(x, y, z, radius, maxDmg, byPlayer) {
    for (const v of this.list) {
      if (!v.active || v.state === 'dead') continue;
      const d = Math.hypot(v.x - x, v.z - z, v.y - y);
      if (d > radius) continue;
      const f = 1 - d / radius;
      // launch
      const inv = d > 0.5 ? 1 / d : 1;
      v.vx += (v.x - x) * inv * f * 16;
      v.vz += (v.z - z) * inv * f * 16;
      v.vy += f * 9;
      v.airborne = true; v.y += 0.15;
      v.spin += rnd(-3, 3) * f;
      if (v.state === 'parked') v.state = 'free';
      this.damage(v, maxDmg * f, x, z, byPlayer);
    }
  },
  explode(v) {
    if (v.state === 'dead') return;
    const wasPlayer = v === this.playerVeh;
    const blame = wasPlayer || (Tick.t - v.lastPlayerHit < 6);
    v.state = 'dead';
    v.deadT = 0; v.burnT = 0;
    v.vy += rnd(5, 8); v.airborne = true; v.y += 0.1;
    v.spin += rnd(-4, 4);
    const b = this.buckets[v.key];
    b.body.setColorAt(v.slot, _c1.setRGB(0.06, 0.055, 0.05));
    b.body.instanceColor.needsUpdate = true;
    FX.explode(v.x, v.y + 0.8, v.z, 1.0);
    if (v.isPolice && blame) Wanted.crime('destroyPolice', null);
    if (v.cop) v.cop = null;
    if (wasPlayer) Game.onVehicleExploded(v);
    this.damageRadius(v.x, v.y + 0.5, v.z, 6.5, 48, blame);
    if (!wasPlayer) Game.explosionNearPlayer(v.x, v.y + 0.5, v.z, 6.5, 42);
    Peds.alarm(v.x, v.z, 55);
  },

  // ---------- traffic AI ----------
  aiDrive(v, dt) {
    const ai = v.ai;
    if (!ai.edge) { this.assignRoute(v); if (!ai.edge) return; }
    const speed = Math.hypot(v.vx, v.vz);
    // advance along edge by projection
    const e = ai.edge;
    const fx = e.fx * ai.dir, fz = e.fz * ai.dir;
    const sx = ai.dir > 0 ? e.a.x : e.b.x, sz = ai.dir > 0 ? e.a.z : e.b.z;
    ai.t = (v.x - sx) * fx + (v.z - sz) * fz;
    if (ai.t >= e.len - 7) {
      // choose next edge at the upcoming node
      const node = ai.dir > 0 ? e.b : e.a;
      let next = null;
      for (let tries = 0; tries < 6; tries++) {
        const cand = pick(node.edges);
        if (cand === e && node.edges.length > 1) continue;
        next = cand;
        if (Math.random() < 0.75 && cand !== e) break;
      }
      if (next) {
        ai.edge = next;
        ai.dir = (next.a === node) ? 1 : -1;
        ai.t = -7;
      }
    }
    // signal check
    let wantStop = false;
    const node = ai.dir > 0 ? e.b : e.a;
    if (node.signal && ai.t > e.len - 26 && ai.t < e.len - 8) {
      const st = Signals.state(e.vert);
      if (st === 2 || (st === 1 && ai.t < e.len - 16)) wantStop = true;
    }
    // car ahead check
    for (const o of this._active) {
      if (o === v || !o.active) continue;
      const dx = o.x - v.x, dz = o.z - v.z;
      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
      if (dist > 16) continue;
      const fwd = dx * Math.sin(v.heading) + dz * Math.cos(v.heading);
      const lat = Math.abs(dx * -Math.cos(v.heading) + dz * Math.sin(v.heading));
      if (fwd > 1 && fwd < 7 + speed * 0.6 && lat < 2.2) { wantStop = true; if (fwd < 6 && speed < 1 && v.honkT <= 0) { AudioFX.honk(v.x, v.z); v.honkT = rnd(3, 6); } }
    }
    v.honkT -= dt;
    // target point ahead in lane
    const look = 6 + speed * 0.6;
    const tx = sx + fx * (ai.t + look) + (-fz) * ai.lane;
    const tz = sz + fz * (ai.t + look) + (fx) * ai.lane;
    const desired = Math.atan2(tx - v.x, tz - v.z);
    const err = wrapAngle(desired - v.heading);
    v.inSteer = clamp(err * 2.2, -1, 1);
    const cruise = ai.panic > 0 ? 24 : ai.cruise;
    if (wantStop && ai.panic <= 0) {
      v.inThrottle = 0;
      v.inBrake = 1;
    } else {
      v.inBrake = 0;
      v.inThrottle = speed < cruise ? (Math.abs(err) > 1.1 ? 0.25 : 0.85) : 0;
    }
    ai.panic = Math.max(0, ai.panic - dt);
    // blocked recovery
    if (speed < 0.7 && v.inThrottle > 0.3) ai.blockedT += dt; else ai.blockedT = 0;
    if (ai.blockedT > 3) { ai.revT = 1.0; ai.blockedT = 0; }
    if (ai.revT > 0) {
      ai.revT -= dt;
      v.inThrottle = -0.7; v.inBrake = 0; v.inSteer = -v.inSteer;
    }
  },
  assignRoute(v) {
    // nearest edge (clamped projection) so repositioned cars merge onto the street they're on
    let e = null, bd = Infinity;
    for (const c of Graph.edges) {
      const t = clamp((v.x - c.a.x) * c.fx + (v.z - c.a.z) * c.fz, 0, c.len);
      const dx = c.a.x + c.fx * t - v.x, dz = c.a.z + c.fz * t - v.z;
      const d = dx * dx + dz * dz;
      if (d < bd) { bd = d; e = c; }
    }
    v.ai.edge = e || randomEdge(false);
    v.ai.dir = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1;
    v.ai.lane = LANE_OFF + (Math.random() < 0.3 ? LANE_OUT - LANE_OFF : 0);
    v.ai.cruise = rnd(9, 13.5);
  },
  trafficCount: 0,
  spawnTraffic(px, pz) {
    const e = randomEdge(true);
    const t = rnd(0.15, 0.85);
    const dir = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1;
    const p = edgePoint(e, t * e.len, dir, LANE_OFF * 1, this._ep);
    const d = Math.hypot(p.x - px, p.z - pz);
    if (d < 70 || d > 230) return;
    // avoid spawning in view if close
    _v1.set(p.x - camera.position.x, 0, p.z - camera.position.z).normalize();
    camera.getWorldDirection(_v2); _v2.y = 0; _v2.normalize();
    if (d < 130 && _v1.dot(_v2) > 0.45) return;
    const roll = Math.random();
    const model = roll < 0.5 ? 'sedan' : roll < 0.62 ? 'taxi' : roll < 0.76 ? 'muscle' : roll < 0.86 ? 'sports' : 'police';
    const v = this.spawn(model, p.x, p.z, Math.atan2(p.fx, p.fz), 'traffic');
    if (v) {
      v.ai.edge = e; v.ai.dir = dir; v.ai.lane = LANE_OFF; v.ai.cruise = rnd(9, 13.5);
      v.vx = p.fx * 8; v.vz = p.fz * 8;
    }
  },
  _ep: { x: 0, z: 0, fx: 0, fz: 0 },

  // ---------- per-frame ----------
  update(dt, px, pz) {
    const act = this._active;
    act.length = 0;
    let traffic = 0;
    for (const v of this.list) {
      if (!v.active) continue;
      const dp = Math.hypot(v.x - px, v.z - pz);
      if (v.state === 'traffic') {
        traffic++;
        if (dp > 320) { this.release(v); continue; }
      }
      if (v.state === 'free' && dp > 320) { this.release(v); continue; }
      if (v.state === 'dead') {
        v.deadT += dt;
        if (v.deadT > 1.2) {
          // burnt hulk: settle
          v.inThrottle = 0; v.inBrake = 1;
        }
        if (Tick.frame % 4 === 0 && v.deadT < 14) Particles.carFire(v.x, v.y + 0.9, v.z, 0.7);
        if ((v.deadT > 26 && dp > 110) || v.deadT > 80) { this.release(v); continue; }
      }
      if (v.burnT > 0 && v.state !== 'dead') {
        v.burnT += dt;
        if (Tick.frame % 5 === 0) Particles.carFire(v.x, v.y + 0.8, v.z, 0.45);
        // the player's own car burns longer — time to bail out
        if (v.burnT > (v === this.playerVeh ? 6.5 : 3.8)) { this.explode(v); }
      }
      if (v.state !== 'parked') act.push(v);
      else if (dp < 90) act.push(v);   // parked cars near the player participate passively in collisions
    }
    this.trafficCount = traffic;
    if (traffic < 14 && Tick.frame % 22 === 0) this.spawnTraffic(px, pz);

    // drive + integrate
    for (const v of act) {
      if (v.state === 'traffic') this.aiDrive(v, dt);
      if (v.state === 'free' || v.state === 'dead') { v.inThrottle = 0; v.inBrake = 0.45; v.inSteer = 0; v.inHand = false; }
      if (v.state !== 'parked') {
        const slip = this.step(v, dt);
        if (v === this.playerVeh && slip > 0) AudioFX.screech(clamp(slip / 9, 0, 1));
      }
    }
    // pair collisions
    for (let i = 0; i < act.length; i++) {
      const a = act[i];
      for (let j = i + 1; j < act.length; j++) {
        const b = act[j];
        const dx = b.x - a.x, dz = b.z - a.z;
        const rr = (a.def.len + b.def.len) * 0.225;
        const d2 = dx * dx + dz * dz;
        if (d2 > rr * rr || d2 < 1e-6) continue;
        if (Math.abs(a.y - b.y) > 2.2) continue;
        const d = Math.sqrt(d2);
        const nx = dx / d, nz = dz / d;
        const overlap = rr - d;
        const aMove = a.state === 'parked' ? 0 : 0.5, bMove = b.state === 'parked' ? 0 : 0.5;
        a.x -= nx * overlap * aMove; a.z -= nz * overlap * aMove;
        b.x += nx * overlap * bMove; b.z += nz * overlap * bMove;
        const rvx = b.vx - a.vx, rvz = b.vz - a.vz;
        const rel = rvx * nx + rvz * nz;
        if (rel < 0) {
          const imp = -rel * 0.62;
          a.vx -= nx * imp; a.vz -= nz * imp;
          b.vx += nx * imp; b.vz += nz * imp;
          const crashV = -rel;
          if (crashV > 3.5) {
            const mx = (a.x + b.x) / 2, mz = (a.z + b.z) / 2;
            this.damage(a, crashV * 1.05, mx, mz, b === this.playerVeh);
            this.damage(b, crashV * 1.05, mx, mz, a === this.playerVeh);
            a.spin += rnd(-1, 1) * crashV * 0.04;
            b.spin += rnd(-1, 1) * crashV * 0.04;
            AudioFX.crash(mx, mz, Math.min(1, crashV / 14));
            Particles.impactSparks(mx, a.y + 0.6, mz, nx, 0.3, nz, Math.min(16, crashV * 1.2));
            if (a === this.playerVeh || b === this.playerVeh) Shake.add(Math.min(0.55, crashV * 0.035));
            if (a.state === 'parked') a.state = 'free';
            if (b.state === 'parked') b.state = 'free';
            if (a.state === 'traffic') a.ai.panic = 4;
            if (b.state === 'traffic') b.ai.panic = 4;
          }
        }
      }
    }
    // run over pedestrians
    for (const v of act) {
      const sp = Math.hypot(v.vx, v.vz);
      if (sp < 4 || v.state === 'parked') continue;
      const fx = Math.sin(v.heading), fz = Math.cos(v.heading);
      for (const ch of Peds.list) {
        if (ch.state !== 'walk' && ch.state !== 'flee') continue;
        const dx = ch.group.position.x - v.x, dz = ch.group.position.z - v.z;
        if (Math.abs(dx) > 4 || Math.abs(dz) > 4) continue;
        const lon = dx * fx + dz * fz, lat = Math.abs(dx * -fz + dz * fx);
        if (Math.abs(lon) < v.def.len * 0.5 + 0.3 && lat < v.def.wid * 0.5 + 0.3) {
          Peds.kill(ch, v.vx * 0.9, sp * 0.32, v.vz * 0.9, 1, v === this.playerVeh);
          AudioFX.thud(ch.group.position, 0.8);
          if (v === this.playerVeh) Shake.add(0.12);
        }
      }
    }
    // render + streaks
    this.renderAll(dt);
  },

  render(v) {
    const b = this.buckets[v.key];
    _q1.setFromAxisAngle(UP, v.heading);
    _q2.set(Math.sin(v.pitch / 2), 0, 0, Math.cos(v.pitch / 2));
    _q1.multiply(_q2);
    _q2.set(0, 0, Math.sin(v.roll / 2), Math.cos(v.roll / 2));
    _q1.multiply(_q2);
    _m1.compose(_v1.set(v.x, v.y, v.z), _q1, _v2.set(1, 1, 1));
    b.body.setMatrixAt(v.slot, _m1);
    b.lights.setMatrixAt(v.slot, _m1);
    b.body.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    b.lights.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    // wheels
    const d = v.def;
    const fx = Math.sin(v.heading), fz = Math.cos(v.heading);
    v.spinWheel = (v.spinWheel || 0) + ((v.vx * fx + v.vz * fz) / d.wr) * Tick.dt;
    for (let w = 0; w < 4; w++) {
      const lon = (w < 2 ? d.wb : -d.wb);
      const lat = (w % 2 ? 0.86 : -0.86) * (d.wid / 2.0);
      const wx = v.x + fx * lon + (-fz) * lat;
      const wz = v.z + fz * lon + (fx) * lat;
      const wy = v.y + d.wr * d.ws;
      _q1.setFromAxisAngle(UP, v.heading + (w < 2 ? v.steer * 0.45 : 0));
      _q2.set(Math.sin(v.spinWheel / 2), 0, 0, Math.cos(v.spinWheel / 2));
      _q1.multiply(_q2);
      _m1.compose(_v1.set(wx, wy, wz), _q1, _v2.set(d.ws, d.ws, d.ws));
      this.wheelMesh.setMatrixAt(v.poolI * 4 + w, _m1);
    }
    // lightbar
    if (v.barSlot >= 0) {
      const by = v.model === 'swat' ? 2.45 : 1.42;
      _q1.setFromAxisAngle(UP, v.heading);
      _m1.compose(_v1.set(v.x + fx * -0.2, v.y + by, v.z + fz * -0.2), _q1, _v2.set(1, 1, 1));
      this.barMesh.setMatrixAt(v.barSlot, _m1);
      if (v.sirenOn) {
        const phase = (Tick.t * 6 + v.poolI) % 2 < 1;
        this.barMesh.setColorAt(v.barSlot, phase ? _c1.setRGB(3.2, 0.15, 0.15) : _c1.setRGB(0.15, 0.3, 3.4));
      } else {
        this.barMesh.setColorAt(v.barSlot, _c1.setRGB(0.4, 0.05, 0.07));
      }
    }
  },
  renderAll(dt) {
    for (const v of this._active) this.render(v);
    this.wheelMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    if (this.barMesh.instanceColor) this.barMesh.instanceColor.needsUpdate = true;
    this.barMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    this.skid.material.uniforms.uT.value = Tick.t;
    // dynamic streaks: headlights/taillights/police strobes on wet road
    let si = 0;
    const cap = 80;
    for (const v of this._active) {
      if (si >= cap - 3 || v.state === 'dead') continue;
      const fx = Math.sin(v.heading), fz = Math.cos(v.heading);
      const gy = groundHeightAt(v.x, v.z);
      if (v.y - gy > 1.5) continue;
      // headlight streak
      _q1.setFromAxisAngle(UP, v.heading);
      _m1.compose(_v1.set(v.x + fx * (v.def.len / 2), gy + 0.06, v.z + fz * (v.def.len / 2)), _q1, _v2.set(2.2, 1, 10));
      this.dynStreaks.setMatrixAt(si, _m1);
      this.dynStreaks.setColorAt(si, _c1.setRGB(0.7, 0.62, 0.45));
      si++;
      // tail streak (skip on the player's car — it would smear right under the chase camera)
      if (v !== this.playerVeh) {
        _q2.setFromAxisAngle(UP, v.heading + Math.PI);
        _m1.compose(_v1.set(v.x - fx * (v.def.len / 2), gy + 0.06, v.z - fz * (v.def.len / 2)), _q2, _v2.set(1.6, 1, 5));
        this.dynStreaks.setMatrixAt(si, _m1);
        this.dynStreaks.setColorAt(si, _c1.setRGB(0.55, 0.04, 0.02));
        si++;
      }
      if (v.sirenOn) {
        const phase = (Tick.t * 6 + v.poolI) % 2 < 1;
        _q1.setFromAxisAngle(UP, v.heading + Math.PI / 2);
        _m1.compose(_v1.set(v.x, gy + 0.07, v.z), _q1, _v2.set(3.4, 1, 9));
        this.dynStreaks.setMatrixAt(si, _m1);
        this.dynStreaks.setColorAt(si, phase ? _c1.setRGB(1.6, 0.06, 0.06) : _c1.setRGB(0.08, 0.15, 1.8));
        si++;
        _q1.setFromAxisAngle(UP, v.heading - Math.PI / 2);
        _m1.compose(_v1.set(v.x, gy + 0.07, v.z), _q1, _v2.set(3.4, 1, 9));
        this.dynStreaks.setMatrixAt(si, _m1);
        this.dynStreaks.setColorAt(si, phase ? _c1.setRGB(0.08, 0.15, 1.8) : _c1.setRGB(1.6, 0.06, 0.06));
        si++;
      }
    }
    for (; si < cap; si++) {
      _m1.makeScale(0, 0, 0);
      this.dynStreaks.setMatrixAt(si, _m1);
    }
    this.dynStreaks.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    if (this.dynStreaks.instanceColor) this.dynStreaks.instanceColor.needsUpdate = true;
  },

  nearestEnterable(x, z, maxD) {
    let best = null, bd = maxD * maxD;
    for (const v of this.list) {
      if (!v.active || v.state === 'dead') continue;
      const d2 = (v.x - x) * (v.x - x) + (v.z - z) * (v.z - z);
      if (d2 < bd) { bd = d2; best = v; }
    }
    return best;
  },
  spawnParked() {
    for (const s of CityData.parkSpots) {
      this.spawn(s.model, s.x, s.z, s.heading, 'parked');
    }
    // the gleaming hero sports car on the beachfront, right by the player spawn
    this.heroSports = this.spawn('sports', 138, 245.4, -Math.PI / 2, 'parked', 0);
  },
};
// ============================================================ particles, FX, weapons, projectiles, pickups
function makeParticleSystem(count, texture, additive) {
  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  const pos = new Float32Array(count * 3);
  const col = new Float32Array(count * 4);
  const siz = new Float32Array(count);
  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
  geo.setAttribute('aCol', new THREE.BufferAttribute(col, 4));
  geo.setAttribute('aSize', new THREE.BufferAttribute(siz, 1));
  const mat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
    uniforms: { uMap: { value: texture } },
    vertexShader: `
      attribute vec4 aCol;
      attribute float aSize;
      varying vec4 vCol;
      void main() {
        vCol = aCol;
        vec4 mv = modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
        gl_PointSize = clamp(aSize * (340.0 / max(1.0, -mv.z)), 0.5, 320.0);
        gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mv;
      }`,
    fragmentShader: `
      uniform sampler2D uMap;
      varying vec4 vCol;
      void main() {
        vec4 t = texture2D(uMap, gl_PointCoord);
        gl_FragColor = vec4(vCol.rgb * t.rgb, vCol.a * t.a);
        if (gl_FragColor.a < 0.003) discard;
      }`,
    transparent: true, depthWrite: false,
    blending: additive ? THREE.AdditiveBlending : THREE.NormalBlending,
  });
  const points = new THREE.Points(geo, mat);
  points.frustumCulled = false;
  points.renderOrder = additive ? 8 : 7;
  scene.add(points);
  return {
    n: 0, cap: count, geo,
    px: new Float32Array(count), py: new Float32Array(count), pz: new Float32Array(count),
    vx: new Float32Array(count), vy: new Float32Array(count), vz: new Float32Array(count),
    age: new Float32Array(count), life: new Float32Array(count),
    s0: new Float32Array(count), s1: new Float32Array(count),
    r: new Float32Array(count), g: new Float32Array(count), b: new Float32Array(count),
    a0: new Float32Array(count), grav: new Float32Array(count), drag: new Float32Array(count),
    fadeIn: new Float32Array(count),
    emit(x, y, z, vx, vy, vz, life, s0, s1, r, g, b, a, grav, drag, fadeIn) {
      let i;
      if (this.n < this.cap) i = this.n++;
      else { i = (Math.random() * this.cap) | 0; }
      this.px[i] = x; this.py[i] = y; this.pz[i] = z;
      this.vx[i] = vx; this.vy[i] = vy; this.vz[i] = vz;
      this.age[i] = 0; this.life[i] = life;
      this.s0[i] = s0; this.s1[i] = s1;
      this.r[i] = r; this.g[i] = g; this.b[i] = b; this.a0[i] = a;
      this.grav[i] = grav; this.drag[i] = drag; this.fadeIn[i] = fadeIn || 0.03;
    },
    update(dt) {
      const P = this.geo.attributes.position.array;
      const C = this.geo.attributes.aCol.array;
      const S = this.geo.attributes.aSize.array;
      let n = this.n;
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        this.age[i] += dt;
        if (this.age[i] >= this.life[i]) {
          // swap with last
          n--;
          const j = n;
          if (i !== j) {
            this.px[i] = this.px[j]; this.py[i] = this.py[j]; this.pz[i] = this.pz[j];
            this.vx[i] = this.vx[j]; this.vy[i] = this.vy[j]; this.vz[i] = this.vz[j];
            this.age[i] = this.age[j]; this.life[i] = this.life[j];
            this.s0[i] = this.s0[j]; this.s1[i] = this.s1[j];
            this.r[i] = this.r[j]; this.g[i] = this.g[j]; this.b[i] = this.b[j];
            this.a0[i] = this.a0[j]; this.grav[i] = this.grav[j]; this.drag[i] = this.drag[j];
            this.fadeIn[i] = this.fadeIn[j];
          }
          i--;
          continue;
        }
        const dr = Math.exp(-this.drag[i] * dt);
        this.vx[i] *= dr; this.vz[i] *= dr; this.vy[i] = this.vy[i] * dr - this.grav[i] * dt;
        this.px[i] += this.vx[i] * dt;
        this.py[i] += this.vy[i] * dt;
        this.pz[i] += this.vz[i] * dt;
      }
      this.n = n;
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const t = this.age[i] / this.life[i];
        P[i * 3] = this.px[i]; P[i * 3 + 1] = this.py[i]; P[i * 3 + 2] = this.pz[i];
        const a = this.a0[i] * (1 - t) * Math.min(1, this.age[i] / this.fadeIn[i]);
        C[i * 4] = this.r[i]; C[i * 4 + 1] = this.g[i]; C[i * 4 + 2] = this.b[i]; C[i * 4 + 3] = a;
        S[i] = lerp(this.s0[i], this.s1[i], t);
      }
      this.geo.setDrawRange(0, n);
      this.geo.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
      this.geo.attributes.aCol.needsUpdate = true;
      this.geo.attributes.aSize.needsUpdate = true;
    },
  };
}

const Particles = {
  add: null, smoke: null,
  init() {
    this.add = makeParticleSystem(2048, TEX.dot, true);
    this.smoke = makeParticleSystem(1280, TEX.smoke, false);
  },
  update(dt) { this.add.update(dt); this.smoke.update(dt); },
  muzzle(x, y, z, dx, dy, dz) {
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      this.add.emit(x, y, z, dx * rnd(6, 14) + rnd(-2, 2), dy * rnd(6, 14) + rnd(-2, 2), dz * rnd(6, 14) + rnd(-2, 2),
        rnd(0.04, 0.09), rnd(0.5, 0.9), 0.1, 2.4, 1.7, 0.7, 0.9, 0, 6);
    }
  },
  impactSparks(x, y, z, nx, ny, nz, count) {
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      this.add.emit(x, y, z,
        nx * rnd(2, 9) + rnd(-4, 4), Math.abs(ny) * rnd(2, 8) + rnd(0.5, 5), nz * rnd(2, 9) + rnd(-4, 4),
        rnd(0.15, 0.45), rnd(0.1, 0.2), 0.03, 2.6, 1.9, 0.9, 1, 22, 2);
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
      this.smoke.emit(x, y, z, nx * 1.5 + rnd(-0.5, 0.5), 1 + rnd(1), nz * 1.5 + rnd(-0.5, 0.5),
        rnd(0.5, 0.9), 0.5, 1.6, 0.45, 0.42, 0.4, 0.45, -0.5, 1.5);
    }
  },
  hitPuff(x, y, z) {
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      this.smoke.emit(x, y, z, rnd(-1, 1), rnd(0.5, 1.5), rnd(-1, 1),
        rnd(0.3, 0.5), 0.4, 1.1, 0.85, 0.8, 0.78, 0.5, 0, 2);
    }
  },
  tireSmoke(x, y, z) {
    this.smoke.emit(x, y, z, rnd(-1.2, 1.2), rnd(0.6, 1.6), rnd(-1.2, 1.2),
      rnd(0.6, 1.1), 0.7, 2.6, 0.66, 0.64, 0.62, 0.34, -0.6, 1.8);
  },
  exhaust(x, y, z) {
    this.smoke.emit(x, y, z, rnd(-0.3, 0.3), rnd(0.4, 0.9), rnd(-0.3, 0.3),
      rnd(0.5, 0.9), 0.25, 0.9, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.22, -0.3, 1.2);
  },
  carFire(x, y, z, s) {
    this.add.emit(x + rnd(-0.5, 0.5), y, z + rnd(-0.5, 0.5), rnd(-0.5, 0.5), rnd(1.5, 3.2) * s, rnd(-0.5, 0.5),
      rnd(0.3, 0.6), 1.4 * s, 0.3, 2.6, 1.1, 0.25, 0.85, -1, 1.5);
    this.smoke.emit(x, y + 0.6, z, rnd(-0.4, 0.4), rnd(1.4, 2.4), rnd(-0.4, 0.4),
      rnd(1.2, 2.2), 1.0, 3.0, 0.12, 0.11, 0.11, 0.5, -1.2, 0.8);
  },
  dust(x, y, z) {
    this.smoke.emit(x, y, z, rnd(-1.5, 1.5), rnd(0.5, 2), rnd(-1.5, 1.5),
      rnd(0.4, 0.8), 0.35, 1.3, 0.55, 0.48, 0.4, 0.5, 1.5, 2);
  },
  splash(x, z, big) {
    const n = big ? 16 : 6;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      this.add.emit(x + rnd(-1, 1), CONFIG.waterY + 0.1, z + rnd(-1, 1), rnd(-3, 3), rnd(3, 8) * (big ? 1.4 : 1), rnd(-3, 3),
        rnd(0.4, 0.9), 0.5, 0.15, 0.6, 0.85, 1.0, 0.8, 14, 1);
    }
  },
};

// ---------- tracers ----------
const Tracers = {
  pool: [], N: 24,
  init() {
    const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.05, 0.05, 1);
    geo.translate(0, 0, 0.5);
    const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffd9a0, transparent: true, opacity: 0.9, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false });
    mat.color.multiplyScalar(2.2);
    for (let i = 0; i < this.N; i++) {
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
      m.visible = false;
      scene.add(m);
      this.pool.push({ m, t: 0, len: 0, speed: 320, active: false, dir: new THREE.Vector3(), from: new THREE.Vector3() });
    }
  },
  fire(fx, fy, fz, tx, ty, tz) {
    for (const tr of this.pool) {
      if (tr.active) continue;
      tr.active = true; tr.m.visible = true;
      tr.from.set(fx, fy, fz);
      tr.dir.set(tx - fx, ty - fy, tz - fz);
      tr.len = tr.dir.length();
      tr.dir.normalize();
      tr.t = 0;
      tr.m.position.copy(tr.from);
      tr.m.lookAt(tx, ty, tz);
      return;
    }
  },
  update(dt) {
    for (const tr of this.pool) {
      if (!tr.active) continue;
      tr.t += tr.speed * dt;
      const head = Math.min(tr.t, tr.len);
      const tail = Math.max(0, tr.t - 7);
      if (tail >= tr.len) { tr.active = false; tr.m.visible = false; continue; }
      _v1.copy(tr.from).addScaledVector(tr.dir, tail);
      tr.m.position.copy(_v1);
      tr.m.scale.set(1, 1, Math.max(0.1, head - tail));
    }
  },
};

// ---------- shell casings ----------
const Casings = {
  N: 26, mesh: null, d: [],
  init() {
    const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.022, 0.022, 0.05);
    const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xc9a227, roughness: 0.3, metalness: 0.8 });
    this.mesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(geo, mat, this.N);
    this.mesh.frustumCulled = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < this.N; i++) {
      _m1.makeScale(0, 0, 0);
      this.mesh.setMatrixAt(i, _m1);
      this.d.push({ on: false, x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0, rx: 0, ry: 0, wx: 0, wy: 0, age: 0 });
    }
    scene.add(this.mesh);
  },
  eject(x, y, z, rx, rz) {
    for (const c of this.d) {
      if (c.on) continue;
      c.on = true; c.age = 0;
      c.x = x; c.y = y; c.z = z;
      c.vx = rx * rnd(1.5, 2.5) + rnd(-0.5, 0.5); c.vy = rnd(2, 3.2); c.vz = rz * rnd(1.5, 2.5) + rnd(-0.5, 0.5);
      c.rx = rnd(6); c.ry = rnd(6); c.wx = rnd(-12, 12); c.wy = rnd(-12, 12);
      return;
    }
  },
  update(dt) {
    let dirty = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < this.N; i++) {
      const c = this.d[i];
      if (!c.on) continue;
      dirty = true;
      c.age += dt;
      c.vy -= 14 * dt;
      c.x += c.vx * dt; c.y += c.vy * dt; c.z += c.vz * dt;
      const gy = groundHeightAt(c.x, c.z) + 0.02;
      if (c.y < gy) { c.y = gy; c.vy = -c.vy * 0.3; c.vx *= 0.6; c.vz *= 0.6; c.wx *= 0.5; c.wy *= 0.5; if (Math.abs(c.vy) > 0.5) AudioFX.casing(c.x, c.z); }
      c.rx += c.wx * dt; c.ry += c.wy * dt;
      if (c.age > 2.2) { c.on = false; _m1.makeScale(0, 0, 0); this.mesh.setMatrixAt(i, _m1); continue; }
      _q1.setFromAxisAngle(UP, c.ry);
      _q2.set(Math.sin(c.rx / 2), 0, 0, Math.cos(c.rx / 2));
      _q1.multiply(_q2);
      _m1.compose(_v1.set(c.x, c.y, c.z), _q1, _v2.set(1, 1, 1));
      this.mesh.setMatrixAt(i, _m1);
    }
    if (dirty) this.mesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
  },
};

// ---------- debris ----------
const Debris = {
  N: 40, mesh: null, d: [],
  init() {
    const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.3, 0.22, 0.3);
    const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x33302e, roughness: 0.9 });
    this.mesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(geo, mat, this.N);
    this.mesh.frustumCulled = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < this.N; i++) {
      _m1.makeScale(0, 0, 0);
      this.mesh.setMatrixAt(i, _m1);
      this.d.push({ on: false, x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0, rx: 0, ry: 0, wx: 0, wy: 0, age: 0, s: 1 });
    }
    scene.add(this.mesh);
    this.mesh.castShadow = true;
  },
  burst(x, y, z, count, power) {
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      let slot = null;
      for (const c of this.d) if (!c.on) { slot = c; break; }
      if (!slot) return;
      slot.on = true; slot.age = 0;
      slot.x = x; slot.y = y; slot.z = z;
      const a = rnd(Math.PI * 2), sp = rnd(4, 13) * power;
      slot.vx = Math.cos(a) * sp; slot.vz = Math.sin(a) * sp; slot.vy = rnd(5, 13) * power;
      slot.wx = rnd(-14, 14); slot.wy = rnd(-14, 14);
      slot.rx = rnd(6); slot.ry = rnd(6);
      slot.s = rnd(0.5, 1.6);
    }
  },
  update(dt) {
    let dirty = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < this.N; i++) {
      const c = this.d[i];
      if (!c.on) continue;
      dirty = true;
      c.age += dt;
      c.vy -= 22 * dt;
      c.x += c.vx * dt; c.y += c.vy * dt; c.z += c.vz * dt;
      const gy = groundHeightAt(c.x, c.z) + 0.12;
      if (c.y < gy) { c.y = gy; c.vy = -c.vy * 0.35; c.vx *= 0.55; c.vz *= 0.55; c.wx *= 0.5; c.wy *= 0.5; }
      c.rx += c.wx * dt; c.ry += c.wy * dt;
      const fade = c.age > 3 ? Math.max(0, 1 - (c.age - 3) / 0.5) : 1;
      if (fade <= 0) { c.on = false; _m1.makeScale(0, 0, 0); this.mesh.setMatrixAt(i, _m1); continue; }
      _q1.setFromAxisAngle(UP, c.ry);
      _q2.set(Math.sin(c.rx / 2), 0, 0, Math.cos(c.rx / 2));
      _q1.multiply(_q2);
      _m1.compose(_v1.set(c.x, c.y, c.z), _q1, _v2.set(c.s * fade, c.s * fade, c.s * fade));
      this.mesh.setMatrixAt(i, _m1);
    }
    if (dirty) this.mesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
  },
};

// ---------- explosions / FX ----------
const FX = {
  rings: [], flashes: [], scorches: [], scorchI: 0, emitters: [],
  init() {
    const ringGeo = new THREE.RingGeometry(0.8, 1, 36);
    ringGeo.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
      const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffc080, transparent: true, opacity: 0, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(ringGeo, mat);
      m.visible = false;
      m.renderOrder = 9;
      scene.add(m);
      this.rings.push({ m, t: 1e9, dur: 0.55, size: 1 });
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      const l = new THREE.PointLight(0xffa050, 0, 90, 1.8);
      scene.add(l);
      this.flashes.push({ l, t: 1e9, dur: 0.3, peak: 0 });
    }
    const scGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1, 1);
    scGeo.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: TEX.scorch, transparent: true, opacity: 0, depthWrite: false, color: 0x000000 });
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(scGeo, mat);
      m.visible = false;
      m.renderOrder = 2;
      scene.add(m);
      this.scorches.push({ m, t: 1e9 });
    }
  },
  ring(x, y, z, size) {
    let best = this.rings[0];
    for (const r of this.rings) if (r.t > best.t) best = r;
    best.t = 0; best.size = size;
    best.m.position.set(x, y, z);
    best.m.visible = true;
  },
  flash(x, y, z, peak) {
    let best = this.flashes[0];
    for (const f of this.flashes) if (f.t > best.t) best = f;
    best.t = 0; best.peak = peak;
    best.l.position.set(x, y, z);
  },
  scorchAt(x, y, z, size) {
    const s = this.scorches[this.scorchI++ % this.scorches.length];
    s.t = 0;
    s.m.visible = true;
    s.m.position.set(x, y + 0.045, z);
    s.m.scale.set(size, 1, size);
    s.m.rotation.y = rnd(Math.PI * 2);
    s.m.material.opacity = 0.85;
  },
  explode(x, y, z, power, opts) {
    // fireball
    const n = Math.floor(28 * power);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const a = rnd(Math.PI * 2), e = rnd(-0.5, 1.2), sp = rnd(2, 11) * power;
      Particles.add.emit(x, y, z, Math.cos(a) * sp, Math.abs(Math.sin(e)) * sp + rnd(2, 6), Math.sin(a) * sp,
        rnd(0.45, 1.05), rnd(2.2, 4.2) * power, 0.5, 2.7, rnd(0.7, 1.3), 0.22, 0.95, -2, 2.2, 0.01);
    }
    // white-hot core
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      Particles.add.emit(x, y, z, rnd(-3, 3), rnd(1, 6), rnd(-3, 3),
        rnd(0.18, 0.42), rnd(2.6, 4.4) * power, 0.4, 3.2, 2.9, 2.2, 1, 0, 3, 0.01);
    }
    // smoke column over ~1.4s
    this.emitters.push({ x, y, z, t: 0, dur: 1.5 * power, rate: 26, acc: 0, power });
    // sparks
    Particles.impactSparks(x, y, z, 0, 1, 0, Math.floor(16 * power));
    Debris.burst(x, y + 0.4, z, Math.floor(9 * power), power);
    this.ring(x, Math.max(groundHeightAt(x, z) + 0.3, y - 1), z, 15 * power);
    this.flash(x, y + 2, z, 320 * power);
    const gy = groundHeightAt(x, z);
    if (y - gy < 3) this.scorchAt(x, gy, z, 7 * power);
    const dCam = camera.position.distanceTo(_v1.set(x, y, z));
    Shake.add(clamp(1.1 * power * (1 - dCam / 90), 0, 0.85));
    AudioFX.explosion(x, y, z, power);
    Peds.alarm(x, z, 60 * power);
    if (isWaterAt(x, z)) Particles.splash(x, z, true);
  },
  update(dt) {
    for (const r of this.rings) {
      if (r.t > r.dur) { if (r.m.visible) r.m.visible = false; continue; }
      r.t += dt;
      const k = clamp(r.t / r.dur, 0, 1);
      const s = (0.1 + k) * r.size;
      r.m.scale.set(s, 1, s);
      r.m.material.opacity = (1 - k) * 0.7;
    }
    for (const f of this.flashes) {
      if (f.t > f.dur) { if (f.l.intensity > 0) f.l.intensity = 0; continue; }
      f.t += dt;
      const k = clamp(f.t / f.dur, 0, 1);
      f.l.intensity = f.peak * (1 - k) * (1 - k);
    }
    for (const s of this.scorches) {
      if (s.t > 30) continue;
      s.t += dt;
      if (s.t > 24) s.m.material.opacity = Math.max(0, 0.85 * (1 - (s.t - 24) / 6));
      if (s.t > 30) s.m.visible = false;
    }
    for (let i = this.emitters.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const e = this.emitters[i];
      e.t += dt;
      if (e.t > e.dur) { this.emitters.splice(i, 1); continue; }
      e.acc += e.rate * dt;
      while (e.acc >= 1) {
        e.acc -= 1;
        const k = e.t / e.dur;
        Particles.smoke.emit(e.x + rnd(-1, 1) * e.power, e.y + k * 7 * e.power, e.z + rnd(-1, 1) * e.power,
          rnd(-0.8, 0.8), rnd(2.5, 5), rnd(-0.8, 0.8),
          rnd(1.4, 2.6), 1.6 * e.power, 4.5 * e.power, 0.13, 0.12, 0.12, 0.55, -1.6, 0.7);
      }
    }
  },
};

// ---------- hitscan ----------
const Combat = {
  _hit: { d: 0, type: '', veh: null, ch: null, x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, nx: 0, ny: 1, nz: 0 },
  raySphere(ox, oy, oz, dx, dy, dz, cx, cy, cz, r) {
    const lx = cx - ox, ly = cy - oy, lz = cz - oz;
    const l2 = lx * lx + ly * ly + lz * lz;
    if (l2 <= r * r) return 0;   // origin inside sphere: immediate hit (point-blank)
    const tca = lx * dx + ly * dy + lz * dz;
    if (tca < 0) return -1;
    const d2 = l2 - tca * tca;
    if (d2 > r * r) return -1;
    return tca - Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, r * r - d2));
  },
  cast(ox, oy, oz, dx, dy, dz, maxD, fromPlayer) {
    const h = this._hit;
    h.type = 'none'; h.d = maxD; h.veh = null; h.ch = null;
    const sd = Colliders.raycast(ox, oy, oz, dx, dy, dz, maxD);
    if (sd >= 0 && sd < h.d) {
      h.d = sd; h.type = 'static';
      h.nx = Colliders._rayN.x; h.ny = Colliders._rayN.y; h.nz = Colliders._rayN.z;
    }
    // ground march
    let py = oy;
    for (let s = 1; s <= 12; s++) {
      const t = (maxD / 12) * s;
      if (t > h.d) break;
      const gx = ox + dx * t, gy = oy + dy * t, gz = oz + dz * t;
      if (gy < groundHeightAt(gx, gz)) {
        // refine between previous and this step
        const t0 = (maxD / 12) * (s - 1);
        let lo = t0, hi = t;
        for (let it = 0; it < 5; it++) {
          const mid = (lo + hi) / 2;
          if (oy + dy * mid < groundHeightAt(ox + dx * mid, oz + dz * mid)) hi = mid; else lo = mid;
        }
        if (hi < h.d) { h.d = hi; h.type = 'ground'; h.nx = 0; h.ny = 1; h.nz = 0; }
        break;
      }
      py = gy;
    }
    // vehicles
    for (const v of Vehicles.list) {
      if (!v.active) continue;
      if (Math.abs(v.x - ox) > maxD + 8 || Math.abs(v.z - oz) > maxD + 8) continue;
      const t = this.raySphere(ox, oy, oz, dx, dy, dz, v.x, v.y + 0.8, v.z, v.def.len * 0.34);
      if (t >= 0 && t < h.d) { h.d = t; h.type = 'veh'; h.veh = v; h.nx = -dx; h.ny = -dy; h.nz = -dz; }
    }
    // characters (peds + cops)
    const checkChar = (ch) => {
      if (!ch.active || ch.rag.on) return;
      const p = ch.group.position;
      let t = this.raySphere(ox, oy, oz, dx, dy, dz, p.x, p.y + 1.0, p.z, 0.48);
      let head = false;
      const th = this.raySphere(ox, oy, oz, dx, dy, dz, p.x, p.y + 1.56, p.z, 0.26);
      if (th >= 0 && (t < 0 || th < t)) { t = th; head = true; }
      if (t >= 0 && t < h.d) { h.d = t; h.type = 'char'; h.ch = ch; h.head = head; h.nx = -dx; h.ny = 0; h.nz = -dz; }
    };
    if (fromPlayer) {
      for (const ch of Peds.list) checkChar(ch);
      for (const c of Police.footCops) checkChar(c.ch);
    }
    // helicopter
    if (fromPlayer && Police.heli.alive) {
      const hp = Police.heli.pos;
      const t = this.raySphere(ox, oy, oz, dx, dy, dz, hp.x, hp.y, hp.z, 3.6);
      if (t >= 0 && t < h.d) { h.d = t; h.type = 'heli'; }
    }
    h.x = ox + dx * h.d; h.y = oy + dy * h.d; h.z = oz + dz * h.d;
    return h;
  },
  bulletImpactFX(h, dmg, byPlayer) {
    if (h.type === 'static') Particles.impactSparks(h.x, h.y, h.z, h.nx, h.ny, h.nz, 5);
    else if (h.type === 'ground') { Particles.dust(h.x, h.y + 0.1, h.z); Particles.impactSparks(h.x, h.y, h.z, 0, 1, 0, 2); }
    else if (h.type === 'veh') { Particles.impactSparks(h.x, h.y, h.z, h.nx, h.ny, h.nz, 6); Vehicles.damage(h.veh, dmg, h.x, h.z, byPlayer); if (h.veh.state === 'parked') h.veh.state = 'free'; if (h.veh.state === 'traffic') h.veh.ai.panic = 5; }
    else if (h.type === 'char') Particles.hitPuff(h.x, h.y, h.z);
    else if (h.type === 'heli') { Particles.impactSparks(h.x, h.y, h.z, 0, -1, 0, 6); Police.damageHeli(dmg); }
  },
  // npc fire at the player
  npcShoot(fx, fy, fz, px, py, pz, hitChance, dmg) {
    AudioFX.shot('pistol', fx, fy, fz);
    Particles.muzzle(fx, fy, fz, (px - fx) * 0.05, 0, (pz - fz) * 0.05);
    if (Math.random() < hitChance) {
      Tracers.fire(fx, fy, fz, px + rnd(-0.2, 0.2), py + rnd(-0.2, 0.2), pz + rnd(-0.2, 0.2));
      Game.damagePlayer(dmg, fx, fz);
    } else {
      const mx = px + rnd(-3, 3), my = py + rnd(-1.5, 2), mz = pz + rnd(-3, 3);
      Tracers.fire(fx, fy, fz, mx, my, mz);
      const d = Math.hypot(mx - fx, my - fy, mz - fz) || 1;
      const h = this.cast(fx, fy, fz, (mx - fx) / d, (my - fy) / d, (mz - fz) / d, d + 20, false);
      if (h.type !== 'none') this.bulletImpactFX(h, 2, false);
    }
  },
};

// ---------- player weapons ----------
const Weapons = {
  defs: {
    pistol: { dmg: 17, rof: 0.22, spread: 0.012, kick: 0.35, clip: 12, reload: 1.0, auto: false, tracerEvery: 1 },
    smg: { dmg: 8, rof: 0.082, spread: 0.03, kick: 0.2, clip: 32, reload: 1.4, auto: true, tracerEvery: 1 },
    rocket: { dmg: 0, rof: 1.3, spread: 0.004, kick: 1.4, clip: 1, reload: 1.6, auto: false, tracerEvery: 0 },
  },
  cur: 'pistol',
  clip: { pistol: 12, smg: 32, rocket: 1 },
  reserve: { pistol: Infinity, smg: 60, rocket: 0 },
  has: { pistol: true, smg: true, rocket: false },
  cooldown: 0, reloadT: 0, spreadHeat: 0,
  order: ['pistol', 'smg', 'rocket'],
  select(name) {
    if (!this.has[name] || name === this.cur) return;
    this.cur = name;
    this.reloadT = 0;
    AudioFX.weaponSwitch();
    HUD.ammoDirty = true;
  },
  cycle(dir) {
    const avail = this.order.filter(n => this.has[n]);
    let i = avail.indexOf(this.cur);
    i = (i + dir + avail.length) % avail.length;
    this.select(avail[i]);
  },
  startReload() {
    const d = this.defs[this.cur];
    if (this.reloadT > 0) return;
    if (this.clip[this.cur] >= d.clip) return;
    if (this.reserve[this.cur] <= 0) return;
    this.reloadT = d.reload;
    AudioFX.click(820, 0.05);
  },
  update(dt) {
    this.cooldown -= dt;
    this.spreadHeat = Math.max(0, this.spreadHeat - dt * 2.4);
    if (this.reloadT > 0) {
      this.reloadT -= dt;
      if (this.reloadT <= 0) {
        // top up the clip, keeping leftover rounds
        const d = this.defs[this.cur];
        const want = d.clip - this.clip[this.cur];
        const take = this.reserve[this.cur] === Infinity ? want : Math.min(want, this.reserve[this.cur]);
        if (take > 0) {
          this.clip[this.cur] += take;
          if (this.reserve[this.cur] !== Infinity) this.reserve[this.cur] -= take;
          AudioFX.reload();
        }
        HUD.ammoDirty = true;
      }
    }
  },
  tryFire(muzzle, ox, oy, oz, dx, dy, dz, held) {
    const d = this.defs[this.cur];
    if (this.cooldown > 0 || this.reloadT > 0) return false;
    if (!d.auto && held) return false;
    if (this.clip[this.cur] <= 0) {
      if (this.reserve[this.cur] > 0) { this.reloadT = d.reload; }
      else if (!held) AudioFX.dryFire();
      this.cooldown = 0.18;
      return false;
    }
    this.cooldown = d.rof;
    this.clip[this.cur]--;
    if (this.clip[this.cur] <= 0 && this.reserve[this.cur] > 0) this.reloadT = d.reload;
    HUD.ammoDirty = true;
    const sp = d.spread + this.spreadHeat * 0.02;
    this.spreadHeat = Math.min(1.6, this.spreadHeat + 0.32);
    const sx = dx + rnd(-sp, sp), sy = dy + rnd(-sp, sp), sz = dz + rnd(-sp, sp);
    const sl = Math.hypot(sx, sy, sz);
    const ddx = sx / sl, ddy = sy / sl, ddz = sz / sl;
    if (this.cur === 'rocket') {
      Rockets.fire(muzzle.x, muzzle.y, muzzle.z, ddx, ddy, ddz);
      AudioFX.shot('rocket', muzzle.x, muzzle.y, muzzle.z);
      Shake.add(0.3);
    } else {
      const h = Combat.cast(ox, oy, oz, ddx, ddy, ddz, 140, true);
      AudioFX.shot(this.cur, muzzle.x, muzzle.y, muzzle.z);
      Particles.muzzle(muzzle.x, muzzle.y, muzzle.z, ddx, ddy, ddz);
      Tracers.fire(muzzle.x, muzzle.y, muzzle.z, h.x, h.y, h.z);
      Casings.eject(muzzle.x, muzzle.y, muzzle.z, -Math.cos(Game.yaw || 0), Math.sin(Game.yaw || 0));
      Combat.bulletImpactFX(h, d.dmg, true);
      if (h.type === 'char') {
        const ch = h.ch;
        const dmg = d.dmg * (h.head ? 2.5 : 1);
        if (ch.kind === 'civ') Peds.kill(ch, ddx * 4, 2.5, ddz * 4, 0.6, true);
        else Police.damageCop(ch, dmg, ddx, ddz);
      }
      Shake.add(d.kick * 0.18);
      Wanted.crime('shot', null);
      Peds.alarm(muzzle.x, muzzle.z, 32);
    }
    return true;
  },
};

// ---------- rockets ----------
const Rockets = {
  pool: [], N: 5,
  init() {
    const geo = BufferGeometryUtils.mergeGeometries([
      new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.07, 0.07, 0.5, 8).rotateX(Math.PI / 2),
      new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.07, 0.16, 8).rotateX(Math.PI / 2).translate(0, 0, 0.33),
    ]);
    const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x6a6a58, roughness: 0.5, metalness: 0.4 });
    for (let i = 0; i < this.N; i++) {
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
      m.visible = false;
      scene.add(m);
      this.pool.push({ m, on: false, x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, dx: 0, dy: 0, dz: 0, age: 0 });
    }
  },
  fire(x, y, z, dx, dy, dz) {
    for (const r of this.pool) {
      if (r.on) continue;
      r.on = true; r.age = 0;
      r.x = x; r.y = y; r.z = z;
      r.dx = dx; r.dy = dy; r.dz = dz;
      r.m.visible = true;
      r.m.position.set(x, y, z);
      r.m.lookAt(x + dx, y + dy, z + dz);
      return;
    }
  },
  detonate(r, x, y, z) {
    r.on = false; r.m.visible = false;
    FX.explode(x, y, z, 1.65);
    // count kills for slow-mo drama
    let kills = 0;
    for (const v of Vehicles.list) {
      if (v.active && v.state !== 'dead' && Math.hypot(v.x - x, v.z - z, v.y - y) < 9.5) kills++;
    }
    Vehicles.damageRadius(x, y, z, 9.5, 150, true);
    // launch nearby characters
    for (const ch of Peds.list) {
      if (ch.state !== 'walk' && ch.state !== 'flee') continue;
      const p = ch.group.position;
      const d = Math.hypot(p.x - x, p.z - z, p.y - y);
      if (d < 10) {
        const f = 1 - d / 10, inv = d > 0.5 ? 1 / d : 1;
