        Peds.kill(ch, (p.x - x) * inv * f * 18, f * 11, (p.z - z) * inv * f * 18, 1, true);
      }
    }
    Police.rocketBlast(x, y, z);
    Game.explosionNearPlayer(x, y, z, 9.5, 80);
    Wanted.crime('rocket', null);
    if (kills >= 2) SlowMo.trigger(0.32, 0.55);
  },
  update(dt) {
    for (const r of this.pool) {
      if (!r.on) continue;
      r.age += dt;
      if (r.age > 5) { this.detonate(r, r.x, r.y, r.z); continue; }
      const step = 62 * dt;
      const h = Combat.cast(r.x, r.y, r.z, r.dx, r.dy, r.dz, step + 0.6, true);
      if (h.type !== 'none' && h.d <= step + 0.5) {
        if (h.type === 'heli') Police.damageHeli(400);
        this.detonate(r, h.x, h.y, h.z);
        continue;
      }
      r.x += r.dx * step; r.y += r.dy * step; r.z += r.dz * step;
      r.dy -= 0.018 * dt;
      r.m.position.set(r.x, r.y, r.z);
      // exhaust glow + smoke trail
      Particles.add.emit(r.x - r.dx, r.y - r.dy, r.z - r.dz, rnd(-0.5, 0.5), rnd(-0.5, 0.5), rnd(-0.5, 0.5),
        0.14, 0.9, 0.2, 2.6, 1.4, 0.5, 0.9, 0, 4, 0.01);
      Particles.smoke.emit(r.x - r.dx * 0.6, r.y, r.z - r.dz * 0.6, rnd(-0.4, 0.4), rnd(0.2, 0.8), rnd(-0.4, 0.4),
        rnd(0.8, 1.4), 0.35, 1.5, 0.62, 0.6, 0.58, 0.5, -0.2, 1.2);
    }
  },
};

// ---------- pickups ----------
const Pickups = {
  rocket: null, beam: null, ring: null, respawnT: 0, drops: [],
  init() {
    const g = new THREE.Group();
    const launcher = GUN_PROTO.rocket.clone();
    launcher.scale.setScalar(2.2);
    launcher.visible = true;
    launcher.traverse(o => { if (o.isMesh) o.castShadow = true; });
    g.add(launcher);
    this.launcher = launcher;
    // golden beam
    const [bc, bctx] = makeCanvas(8, 64);
    const grad = bctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 64);
    grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
    grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)');
    bctx.fillStyle = grad; bctx.fillRect(0, 0, 8, 64);
    const beamTex = canvasTex(bc);
    const beamMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: beamTex, transparent: true, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide, fog: false });
    beamMat.color.setRGB(2.2, 1.55, 0.5);
    const beam = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.5, 0.8, 30, 12, 1, true), beamMat);
    beam.position.y = 14;
    g.add(beam);
    this.beam = beam;
    const ringMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffc566, transparent: true, opacity: 0.55, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false });
    ringMat.color.multiplyScalar(1.6);
    const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.RingGeometry(1.1, 1.5, 28).rotateX(-Math.PI / 2), ringMat);
    ring.position.y = 0.1;
    g.add(ring);
    this.ring = ring;
    const l = new THREE.PointLight(0xffb050, 60, 18, 1.9);
    l.position.y = 2;
    g.add(l);
    g.position.copy(ROCKET_PICKUP_POS);
    scene.add(g);
    this.rocket = g;
    // smg ammo drops
    const dropGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.4, 0.26, 0.3);
    const dropMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x3a4a32, roughness: 0.5, emissive: 0x9adf60, emissiveIntensity: 0.55 });
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(dropGeo, dropMat);
      m.visible = false;
      scene.add(m);
      this.drops.push({ m, on: false, x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, age: 0 });
    }
  },
  dropAmmo(x, y, z) {
    for (const d of this.drops) {
      if (d.on) continue;
      d.on = true; d.age = 0;
      d.x = x + rnd(-0.5, 0.5); d.z = z + rnd(-0.5, 0.5);
      d.y = groundHeightAt(d.x, d.z) + 0.15;
      d.m.visible = true;
      d.m.position.set(d.x, d.y, d.z);
      return;
    }
  },
  update(dt, px, py, pz) {
    if (this.rocket.visible) {
      this.launcher.position.y = 1.15 + Math.sin(Tick.t * 1.7) * 0.14;
      this.launcher.rotation.y = Tick.t * 0.9;
      this.ring.rotation.z = Tick.t * 0.5;
      this.beam.material.opacity = 0.5 + Math.sin(Tick.t * 2.2) * 0.14;
      const d = Math.hypot(px - ROCKET_PICKUP_POS.x, pz - ROCKET_PICKUP_POS.z);
      const dy = Math.abs(py - ROCKET_PICKUP_POS.y);
      if (d < 2.3 && dy < 2.5) {
        this.rocket.visible = false;
        this.respawnT = 50;
        Weapons.has.rocket = true;
        Weapons.reserve.rocket += 4;
        if (Weapons.clip.rocket <= 0) { Weapons.clip.rocket = 1; Weapons.reserve.rocket -= 1; }
        Weapons.select('rocket');
        AudioFX.pickup();
        HUD.ammoDirty = true;
      }
    } else {
      this.respawnT -= dt;
      if (this.respawnT <= 0) this.rocket.visible = true;
    }
    for (const d of this.drops) {
      if (!d.on) continue;
      d.age += dt;
      d.m.rotation.y = Tick.t * 2;
      if (d.age > 30) { d.on = false; d.m.visible = false; continue; }
      if (Math.hypot(px - d.x, pz - d.z) < 1.7 && Math.abs(py - d.y) < 2) {
        d.on = false; d.m.visible = false;
        Weapons.reserve.smg = Math.min(240, Weapons.reserve.smg + 36);
        if (Weapons.cur === 'smg' && Weapons.clip.smg <= 0 && Weapons.reloadT <= 0) Weapons.reloadT = 0.4;
        AudioFX.pickup();
        HUD.ammoDirty = true;
      }
    }
  },
};
// ============================================================ wanted system, police pursuit, helicopter
const Wanted = {
  heat: 0, stars: 0, unseenT: 0, destroyedRecent: 0, destroyedT: 0, lastShotCrime: -9,
  FLOORS: [0, 25, 80, 180, 350, 600],
  crime(type, pos) {
    let add = 0, floor = 0;
    switch (type) {
      case 'carTheft': add = 25; floor = 1; break;
      case 'carTheftPolice': add = 30; floor = 2; break;
      case 'shot': {
        if (Tick.t - this.lastShotCrime < 1.2) return;
        this.lastShotCrime = Tick.t;
        // needs witnesses
        let seen = Police.anyCopNear(Game.px, Game.pz, 70);
        if (!seen) {
          for (const ch of Peds.list) {
            if (ch.state === 'walk' || ch.state === 'flee') {
              if (Math.hypot(ch.group.position.x - Game.px, ch.group.position.z - Game.pz) < 38) { seen = true; break; }
            }
          }
        }
        if (!seen) return;
        add = 15; floor = 2;
        break;
      }
      case 'hitPed': add = 10; floor = 1; break;
      case 'killPed': add = 40; floor = 2; break;
      case 'killCop': add = 80; floor = 3; break;
      case 'rocket': add = 50; floor = 3; break;
      case 'destroyPolice':
        add = 70; floor = 4;
        this.destroyedRecent++; this.destroyedT = 40;
        if (this.destroyedRecent >= 2) floor = 5;
        break;
    }
    this.heat = Math.min(800, Math.max(this.heat + add, this.FLOORS[floor]));
    this.unseenT = 0;
  },
  clearAll() {
    this.heat = 0; this.unseenT = 0; this.destroyedRecent = 0;
  },
  starsFor(h) {
    if (h >= 600) return 5;
    if (h >= 350) return 4;
    if (h >= 180) return 3;
    if (h >= 80) return 2;
    if (h >= 25) return 1;
    return 0;
  },
  update(dt, px, pz) {
    this.destroyedT -= dt;
    if (this.destroyedT <= 0) this.destroyedRecent = 0;
    if (this.heat > 0) {
      const seen = Police.pursuitNear(px, pz);
      if (seen) {
        this.unseenT = 0;
        const fl = this.FLOORS[this.starsFor(this.heat)];
        if (this.heat < fl) this.heat = fl;
      } else {
        this.unseenT += dt;
        if (this.unseenT > 8) {
          const s = this.stars;
          const rate = s <= 2 ? 6 : s === 3 ? 4 : 3;
          this.heat = Math.max(0, this.heat - rate * dt);
        }
      }
    }
    const ns = this.starsFor(this.heat);
    if (ns !== this.stars) {
      this.stars = ns;
      HUD.setStars(ns);
      Music.setIntensity(ns);
    }
  },
};

const Police = {
  cars: [], footCops: [], copPool: [], swatPool: [], barriers: [],
  spawnT: 0, blockT: 8, unloadT: 0,
  BUDGET_CARS: [0, 2, 3, 5, 6, 8],
  BUDGET_FOOT: [0, 2, 4, 6, 7, 8],
  BUDGET_SWAT: [0, 0, 0, 1, 2, 3],
  BUDGET_BLOCK: [0, 0, 1, 1, 2, 3],
  heli: null,
  strobes: [],

  init() {
    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
      const ch = makeCharacter('cop');
      ch.group.visible = false;
      scene.add(ch.group);
      this.copPool.push(ch);
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      const ch = makeCharacter('swat');
      ch.group.visible = false;
      scene.add(ch.group);
      this.swatPool.push(ch);
    }
    // barrier props
    const [bc, bctx] = makeCanvas(64, 32);
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
      bctx.fillStyle = i % 2 ? '#e8e4da' : '#e07820';
      bctx.fillRect(i * 8, 0, 8, 32);
    }
    const barTex = canvasTex(bc);
    const barGeo = BufferGeometryUtils.mergeGeometries([
      new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.2, 0.32, 0.12).translate(0, 0.95, 0),
      new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.12, 1.0, 0.5).translate(-1, 0.5, 0),
      new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.12, 1.0, 0.5).translate(1, 0.5, 0),
    ]);
    const barMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: barTex, roughness: 0.6 });
    for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(barGeo, barMat);
      m.visible = false;
      m.castShadow = true;
      scene.add(m);
      this.barriers.push({ m, on: false, x: 0, z: 0, ry: 0 });
    }
    // strobe point lights for the nearest pursuing cruisers
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const l = new THREE.PointLight(0xff2222, 0, 42, 1.6);
      scene.add(l);
      this.strobes.push(l);
    }
    this.initHeli();
  },

  anyCopNear(px, pz, r) {
    for (const v of this.cars) {
      if (v.active && Math.hypot(v.x - px, v.z - pz) < r) return true;
    }
    for (const c of this.footCops) {
      if (c.ch.active && !c.dead && Math.hypot(c.ch.group.position.x - px, c.ch.group.position.z - pz) < r * 0.8) return true;
    }
    if (this.heli.alive && this.heli.state === 'orbit') return true;
    return false;
  },
  // dispatched units still closing in count as contact — evading means actually losing them
  pursuitNear(px, pz) {
    for (const v of this.cars) {
      if (v.active && v.state !== 'dead' && Math.hypot(v.x - px, v.z - pz) < 150) return true;
    }
    return this.anyCopNear(px, pz, 72);
  },

  // ---------- cruisers ----------
  spawnCruiser(px, pz, pvx, pvz, model) {
    const sp = Math.hypot(pvx, pvz);
    const ahead = Math.random() < 0.6 && sp > 6;
    let tx, tz;
    if (ahead) {
      const inv = 1 / (sp || 1);
      const d = rnd(95, 150);
      tx = px + pvx * inv * d; tz = pz + pvz * inv * d;
    } else {
      const a = rnd(Math.PI * 2);
      const d = rnd(80, 130);
      tx = px + Math.cos(a) * d; tz = pz + Math.sin(a) * d;
    }
    // snap to nearest graph node area
    const n = nearestNode(tx, tz);
    if (!n) return false;
    if (Math.hypot(n.x - px, n.z - pz) < 55) return false;
    const v = Vehicles.spawn(model || 'police', n.x + rnd(-4, 4), n.z + rnd(-4, 4), rnd(Math.PI * 2), 'police');
    if (!v) return false;
    v.cop = { mode: 'chase', pitT: 0, stuckT: 0, revT: 0, unloaded: false };
    v.sirenOn = true;
    return true;
  },
  driveCruiser(v, dt, px, pz, pvx, pvz, playerInCar, pSpeed) {
    const cop = v.cop;
    const speed = Math.hypot(v.vx, v.vz);
    const dx = px - v.x, dz = pz - v.z;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
    const fx = Math.sin(v.heading), fz = Math.cos(v.heading);
    // predicted target
    let tPred = clamp(dist / Math.max(speed, 10), 0.35, 1.0);
    let tx = px + pvx * tPred, tz = pz + pvz * tPred;
    // ram when right behind
    const fwdDot = (dx * fx + dz * fz) / (dist || 1);
    if (dist < 13 && fwdDot > 0.93) { tx = px; tz = pz; }
    // PIT: alongside the player's rear quarter
    if (playerInCar && cop.pitT <= 0 && speed > 14 && pSpeed > 14) {
      const lat = dx * -fz + dz * fx;
      const lon = dx * fx + dz * fz;
      if (Math.abs(lat) > 1.5 && Math.abs(lat) < 4 && lon > -1 && lon < 3) {
        cop.pitT = 4;
        cop.pitDir = Math.sign(lat);
        cop.pitExec = 0.7;
      }
    }
    if (cop.pitT > 0) cop.pitT -= dt;
    let steerOverride = null;
    if (cop.pitExec > 0) {
      cop.pitExec -= dt;
      steerOverride = cop.pitDir;
      const pv = Vehicles.playerVeh;
      if (pv && dist < 4.5) { pv.spin += cop.pitDir * dt * 4; }
    }
    const desired = Math.atan2(tx - v.x, tz - v.z);
    const err = wrapAngle(desired - v.heading);
    v.inSteer = steerOverride !== null ? steerOverride : clamp(err * 2.5, -1, 1);
    v.inHand = Math.abs(err) > 1.5 && speed > 13;
    v.inBrake = 0;
    if (playerInCar || dist > 17) {
      v.inThrottle = Math.abs(err) < 1.25 ? 1 : 0.35;
    } else {
      // player on foot: pull up, stop, unload officers
      if (dist > 13) v.inThrottle = 0.6;
      else { v.inThrottle = 0; v.inBrake = 1; }
      if (dist < 16 && speed < 3 && !cop.unloaded) {
        cop.unloaded = true;
        this.deployOfficers(v, 2, 'cop');
      }
    }
    if (dist > 15) cop.unloaded = false;
    // stuck recovery
    if (speed < 1.4 && v.inThrottle > 0.4) cop.stuckT += dt; else cop.stuckT = 0;
    if (cop.stuckT > 1.3) { cop.revT = 1.1; cop.stuckT = 0; }
    if (cop.revT > 0) {
      cop.revT -= dt;
      v.inThrottle = -0.8; v.inBrake = 0; v.inSteer = -v.inSteer;
    }
  },

  // ---------- officers on foot ----------
  deployOfficers(v, count, kind) {
    const pool = kind === 'swat' ? this.swatPool : this.copPool;
    let placed = 0;
    for (const ch of pool) {
      if (ch.active || placed >= count) continue;
      const budget = (kind === 'swat' ? this.BUDGET_SWAT : this.BUDGET_FOOT)[Wanted.stars];
      let cur = 0;
      for (const c of this.footCops) if (c.ch.active && !c.dead && c.kind === kind) cur++;
      if (cur >= budget) break;
      ch.active = true;
      ch.health = kind === 'swat' ? 80 : 40;
      ch.rag.on = false;
      ch.group.visible = true;
      const side = placed % 2 ? 1.6 : -1.6;
      const fx = Math.sin(v.heading), fz = Math.cos(v.heading);
      ch.group.position.set(v.x + (-fz) * side, v.y, v.z + fx * side);
      ch.group.quaternion.identity();
      charShowGun(ch, kind === 'swat' ? 'smg' : 'pistol');
      let rec = null;
      for (const c of this.footCops) if (c.ch === ch) { rec = c; break; }
      if (!rec) {
        rec = { ch, kind, fireT: rnd(0.5, 1.4), burst: 0, dead: false, deadT: 0 };
        this.footCops.push(rec);
      } else {
        rec.kind = kind; rec.fireT = rnd(0.5, 1.4); rec.burst = 0; rec.dead = false; rec.deadT = 0;
      }
      placed++;
      AudioFX.copShout(v.x, v.z);
    }
  },
  updateOfficer(c, dt, px, py, pz, pSpeed) {
    const ch = c.ch;
    const g = ch.group;
    if (c.dead) {
      c.deadT += dt;
      updateRagdoll(ch, dt);
      if (c.deadT > 9) { ch.active = false; g.visible = false; }
      return;
    }
    const dx = px - g.position.x, dz = pz - g.position.z;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
    if (dist > 130) { ch.active = false; g.visible = false; return; }
    const yaw = Math.atan2(dx, dz);
    g.rotation.y = yaw;
    const wantDist = c.kind === 'swat' ? 16 : 12;
    if (dist > wantDist + 6) {
      const sp = c.kind === 'swat' ? 4.2 : 4.8;
      g.position.x += (dx / dist) * sp * dt;
      g.position.z += (dz / dist) * sp * dt;
      const res = Colliders.resolveCircle(g.position.x, g.position.z, 0.4, g.position.y + 0.2, g.position.y + 1.6);
      g.position.x = res.x; g.position.z = res.z;
      g.position.y = groundHeightAt(g.position.x, g.position.z);
      ch.anim.mode = 'run';
      poseCharacter(ch, dt, sp);
      c.fireT = Math.max(c.fireT, 0.4);
    } else {
      ch.anim.mode = 'aim';
      ch.anim.aimPitch = Math.atan2(py + 0.8 - (g.position.y + 1.4), dist) * -1;
      poseCharacter(ch, dt, 0);
      // firing
      c.fireT -= dt;
      if (c.fireT <= 0) {
        if (c.burst <= 0) c.burst = c.kind === 'swat' ? rndi(4, 7) : rndi(2, 4);
        c.burst--;
        c.fireT = c.burst > 0 ? (c.kind === 'swat' ? 0.11 : 0.3) : rnd(1.2, 2.2);
        const mx = g.position.x + Math.sin(yaw) * 0.5, mz = g.position.z + Math.cos(yaw) * 0.5;
        const my = g.position.y + 1.35;
        if (!Colliders.losBlocked(mx, my, mz, px, py + 1.2, pz)) {
          const moveFactor = clamp(pSpeed * 0.02, 0, 0.18);
          const hc = clamp((c.kind === 'swat' ? 0.3 : 0.24) - dist * 0.004 - moveFactor, 0.05, 0.36);
          Combat.npcShoot(mx, my, mz, px, py + 1.2, pz, hc, c.kind === 'swat' ? 5 : 3.5);
        }
      }
    }
  },
  damageCop(ch, dmg, dx, dz) {
    for (const c of this.footCops) {
      if (c.ch !== ch || c.dead) continue;
      ch.health -= dmg;
      if (ch.health <= 0) {
        c.dead = true; c.deadT = 0;
        startRagdoll(ch, dx * 3.5, 2.8, dz * 3.5, 0.7);
        Wanted.crime('killCop', null);
        if (Math.random() < 0.65) Pickups.dropAmmo(ch.group.position.x, ch.group.position.y, ch.group.position.z);
      }
      return;
    }
  },
  rocketBlast(x, y, z) {
    for (const c of this.footCops) {
      if (!c.ch.active || c.dead) continue;
      const p = c.ch.group.position;
      const d = Math.hypot(p.x - x, p.z - z, p.y - y);
      if (d < 10) {
        const f = 1 - d / 10, inv = d > 0.5 ? 1 / d : 1;
        c.dead = true; c.deadT = 0;
        startRagdoll(c.ch, (p.x - x) * inv * f * 18, f * 12, (p.z - z) * inv * f * 18, 1);
        Wanted.crime('killCop', null);
      }
    }
    for (const b of this.barriers) {
      if (b.on && Math.hypot(b.x - x, b.z - z) < 10) {
        b.on = false; b.m.visible = false;
        Debris.burst(b.x, 1, b.z, 3, 0.7);
      }
    }
    if (this.heli.alive && this.heli.pos.distanceTo(_v1.set(x, y, z)) < 9) this.damageHeli(300);
  },

  // ---------- roadblocks ----------
  placeRoadblock(px, pz, pvx, pvz) {
    const sp = Math.hypot(pvx, pvz);
    if (sp < 12) return;
    const d = clamp(sp * 4.5, 110, 240);
    const tx = px + (pvx / sp) * d, tz = pz + (pvz / sp) * d;
    // find the nearest edge point
    let bestE = null, bestT = 0, bestD = 1e9;
    for (const e of Graph.edges) {
      if (e.bridge) continue;
      const t = clamp((tx - e.a.x) * e.fx + (tz - e.a.z) * e.fz, 8, e.len - 8);
      const x = e.a.x + e.fx * t, z = e.a.z + e.fz * t;
      const dd = Math.hypot(x - tx, z - tz);
      if (dd < bestD) { bestD = dd; bestE = e; bestT = t; }
    }
    if (!bestE || bestD > 40) return;
    const bx = bestE.a.x + bestE.fx * bestT, bz = bestE.a.z + bestE.fz * bestT;
    if (Math.hypot(bx - px, bz - pz) < 70) return;
    // cruisers angled across the road
    const cross = Wanted.stars >= 4 ? 3 : 2;
    for (let i = 0; i < cross; i++) {
      const off = (i - (cross - 1) / 2) * 5.2;
      const v = Vehicles.spawn('police', bx + (-bestE.fz) * off, bz + (bestE.fx) * off, Math.atan2(bestE.fx, bestE.fz) + (i % 2 ? 0.9 : -0.9), 'police');
      if (v) {
        v.cop = { mode: 'block', pitT: 0, stuckT: 0, revT: 0, unloaded: true, blockT: 12 };
        v.sirenOn = true;
        v.inBrake = 1;
      }
    }
    // barriers filling the gaps
    let bi = 0;
    for (const b of this.barriers) {
      if (b.on || bi >= 4) continue;
      const off = (bi - 1.5) * 3.1;
      b.on = true;
      b.x = bx + (-bestE.fz) * off + bestE.fx * 6;
      b.z = bz + (bestE.fx) * off + bestE.fz * 6;
      b.ry = Math.atan2(bestE.fz, -bestE.fx);
      b.m.visible = true;
      b.m.position.set(b.x, groundHeightAt(b.x, b.z), b.z);
      b.m.rotation.set(0, b.ry, 0);
      bi++;
    }
    this.deployOfficers({ x: bx + bestE.fx * 8, z: bz + bestE.fz * 8, y: 0, heading: Math.atan2(bestE.fx, bestE.fz) }, 2, Wanted.stars >= 3 ? 'swat' : 'cop');
  },
  updateBarriers(dt) {
    for (const b of this.barriers) {
      if (!b.on) continue;
      for (const v of Vehicles._active) {
        if (v.state === 'parked' || v.state === 'dead') continue;
        const sp = Math.hypot(v.vx, v.vz);
        if (Math.hypot(v.x - b.x, v.z - b.z) < 2.4) {
          if (sp > 5) {
            b.on = false; b.m.visible = false;
            Debris.burst(b.x, 1, b.z, 4, 0.55);
            Particles.impactSparks(b.x, 1, b.z, 0, 1, 0, 6);
            AudioFX.crash(b.x, b.z, 0.35);
            v.vx *= 0.92; v.vz *= 0.92;
            Vehicles.damage(v, 4, b.x, b.z);
            if (v === Vehicles.playerVeh) Shake.add(0.15);
          } else if (sp > 0.5) {
            v.vx *= 0.4; v.vz *= 0.4;
          }
          break;
        }
      }
    }
  },

  // ---------- helicopter ----------
  initHeli() {
    const g = new THREE.Group();
    const navy = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x1c2742, roughness: 0.45, metalness: 0.2, envMapIntensity: 0.9 });
    const white = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xd8dce4, roughness: 0.5 });
    const dark = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x14161c, roughness: 0.6 });
    const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.4, 1.9, 4.6), navy);
    body.position.y = 0.2;
    g.add(body);
    const nose = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.15, 10, 8), navy);
    nose.scale.set(1.02, 0.8, 1.2);
    nose.position.set(0, 0, 2.4);
    g.add(nose);
    const canopy = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.0, 0.9, 1.4), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x101522, roughness: 0.15, metalness: 0.4, envMapIntensity: 1.4 }));
    canopy.position.set(0, 0.65, 1.7);
    g.add(canopy);
    const stripe = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.45, 0.45, 4.0), white);
    stripe.position.set(0, -0.35, 0.2);
    g.add(stripe);
    const boom = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.3, 0.42, 4.4, 8).rotateX(Math.PI / 2), navy);
    boom.position.set(0, 0.45, -4.0);
    g.add(boom);
    const fin = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.16, 1.5, 0.9), navy);
    fin.position.set(0, 1.05, -6.0);
    g.add(fin);
    for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
      const skid = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.14, 0.14, 3.6), dark);
      skid.position.set(side * 1.05, -1.25, 0.6);
      g.add(skid);
      const strut = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.1, 0.65, 0.1), dark);
      strut.position.set(side * 1.05, -0.95, 1.3);
      g.add(strut);
      const strut2 = strut.clone();
      strut2.position.z = -0.4;
      g.add(strut2);
    }
    // rotors
    const rotor = new THREE.Group();
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      const blade = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.34, 0.05, 5.6), dark);
      blade.position.set(0, 0, 2.8);
      const holder = new THREE.Group();
      holder.rotation.y = (i / 4) * Math.PI * 2;
      holder.add(blade);
      rotor.add(holder);
    }
    const blur = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(5.7, 28).rotateX(-Math.PI / 2), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x666677, transparent: true, opacity: 0.13, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
    rotor.add(blur);
    rotor.position.y = 1.45;
    g.add(rotor);
    const tailRotor = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.05, 1.7, 0.22), dark);
    tailRotor.position.set(0.25, 1.0, -6.1);
    g.add(tailRotor);
    // searchlight housing + spotlight
    const housing = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.34, 8, 6), dark);
    housing.position.set(0, -1.1, 1.6);
    g.add(housing);
    const spot = new THREE.SpotLight(0xfff0d0, 950, 130, 0.22, 0.6, 1.4);
    spot.position.set(0, -1.1, 1.6);
    g.add(spot);
    const spotTarget = new THREE.Object3D();
    scene.add(spotTarget);
    spot.target = spotTarget;
    // volumetric cone
    const [cc, cctx] = makeCanvas(8, 64);
    const cg = cctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 64);
    cg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)');
    cg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)');
    cctx.fillStyle = cg; cctx.fillRect(0, 0, 8, 64);
    const coneTex = canvasTex(cc);
    const coneGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.5, 7.5, 1, 18, 1, true);
    coneGeo.translate(0, -0.5, 0);
    const coneMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: coneTex, transparent: true, opacity: 0.13, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide, fog: false });
    coneMat.color.setRGB(1.0, 0.9, 0.68);
    const cone = new THREE.Mesh(coneGeo, coneMat);
    cone.renderOrder = 9;
    scene.add(cone);
    const groundGlow = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(6, 24).rotateX(-Math.PI / 2), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: TEX.dot, transparent: true, opacity: 0.2, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, fog: false }));
    groundGlow.renderOrder = 9;
    scene.add(groundGlow);
    g.visible = false;
    cone.visible = false;
    groundGlow.visible = false;
    scene.add(g);
    this.heli = {
      alive: false, grp: g, rotor, tailRotor, spot, spotTarget, cone, groundGlow,
      pos: new THREE.Vector3(), vel: new THREE.Vector3(), state: 'in',
      hp: 110, orbitA: 0, yaw: 0, bank: 0, pitch: 0,
      fireT: 3, burst: 0, downSpin: 0, downT: 0, smokeT: 0,
    };
  },
  spawnHeli(px, pz) {
    const h = this.heli;
    h.alive = true; h.state = 'in'; h.hp = 110;
    h.pos.set(px + 420, 75, pz - 380);
    h.vel.set(0, 0, 0);
    h.grp.visible = true;
    h.cone.visible = true;
    h.groundGlow.visible = true;
    h.orbitA = rnd(Math.PI * 2);
    h.fireT = 4;
  },
  dismissHeli() {
    const h = this.heli;
    if (!h.alive || h.state === 'down') return;
    h.state = 'leave';
  },
  damageHeli(dmg) {
    const h = this.heli;
    if (!h.alive || h.state === 'down') return;
    h.hp -= dmg;
    if (h.hp <= 0) {
      h.state = 'down';
      h.downT = 0;
      h.downSpin = rnd(2, 3);
      h.vel.set(rnd(-6, 6), 2, rnd(-6, 6));
      FX.explode(h.pos.x, h.pos.y, h.pos.z, 1.9);
      SlowMo.trigger(0.22, 1.7);
      AudioFX.heliHit();
    } else if (h.hp < 50) {
      Particles.impactSparks(h.pos.x, h.pos.y, h.pos.z, 0, -1, 0, 8);
    }
  },
  updateHeli(dt, px, py, pz) {
    const h = this.heli;
    if (!h.alive) return;
    h.rotor.rotation.y += dt * 34;
    h.tailRotor.rotation.x += dt * 50;
    if (h.state === 'down') {
      h.downT += dt;
      h.downSpin += dt * 4;
      h.yaw += h.downSpin * dt;
      h.vel.y -= 7.5 * dt;
      h.pos.addScaledVector(h.vel, dt);
      h.pitch = damp(h.pitch, 0.35, 2, dt);
      h.bank = damp(h.bank, 0.5, 2, dt);
      if (Tick.frame % 2 === 0) {
        Particles.carFire(h.pos.x, h.pos.y, h.pos.z, 1.4);
      }
      const gy = groundHeightAt(h.pos.x, h.pos.z);
      const floor = isWaterAt(h.pos.x, h.pos.z) ? CONFIG.waterY : gy;
      if (h.pos.y < floor + 1.6) {
        FX.explode(h.pos.x, h.pos.y + 1, h.pos.z, 2.6);
        if (isWaterAt(h.pos.x, h.pos.z)) Particles.splash(h.pos.x, h.pos.z, true);
        h.alive = false;
        h.grp.visible = false;
        h.cone.visible = false;
        h.groundGlow.visible = false;
        Wanted.clearAll();
        Game.onHeliDown();
      }
    } else if (h.state === 'leave') {
      h.pos.y += 9 * dt;
      h.pos.x += 24 * dt;
      h.pos.z -= 18 * dt;
      h.yaw = damp(h.yaw, Math.atan2(24, -18), 2, dt);
      if (h.pos.y > 160) {
        h.alive = false;
        h.grp.visible = false; h.cone.visible = false; h.groundGlow.visible = false;
      }
    } else {
      // approach + orbit
      const orbitR = 36, orbitH = 34;
      h.orbitA += dt * 0.32;
      const tx = px + Math.cos(h.orbitA) * orbitR;
      const tz = pz + Math.sin(h.orbitA) * orbitR;
      const ty = orbitH + Math.sin(Tick.t * 0.6) * 2.5;
      _v1.set(tx - h.pos.x, ty - h.pos.y, tz - h.pos.z);
      const dist = _v1.length();
      if (h.state === 'in' && dist < 30) h.state = 'orbit';
      const sp = h.state === 'in' ? 42 : Math.min(26, dist * 1.4);
      _v1.normalize().multiplyScalar(sp);
      h.vel.x = damp(h.vel.x, _v1.x, 1.6, dt);
      h.vel.y = damp(h.vel.y, _v1.y, 1.6, dt);
      h.vel.z = damp(h.vel.z, _v1.z, 1.6, dt);
      h.pos.addScaledVector(h.vel, dt);
      const hsp = Math.hypot(h.vel.x, h.vel.z);
      if (hsp > 2) h.yaw = angleDamp(h.yaw, Math.atan2(h.vel.x, h.vel.z), 2.2, dt);
      h.bank = damp(h.bank, clamp(-wrapAngle(Math.atan2(h.vel.x, h.vel.z) - h.yaw) * 0.8 - 0.25, -0.45, 0.45), 2.5, dt);
      h.pitch = damp(h.pitch, clamp(hsp * 0.012 - 0.06, -0.2, 0.25), 2, dt);
      // door gunner
      if (h.state === 'orbit' && Wanted.stars >= 5) {
        h.fireT -= dt;
        if (h.fireT <= 0) {
          if (h.burst <= 0) h.burst = rndi(6, 10);
          h.burst--;
          h.fireT = h.burst > 0 ? 0.1 : rnd(1.5, 2.4);
          const mx = h.pos.x, my = h.pos.y - 0.8, mz = h.pos.z;
          if (!Colliders.losBlocked(mx, my, mz, px, py + 1.2, pz)) {
            Combat.npcShoot(mx, my, mz, px, py + 1.0, pz, 0.2, 4);
            AudioFX.heliGun();
          }
        }
      }
      if (h.hp < 50) {
        h.smokeT -= dt;
        if (h.smokeT <= 0) {
          h.smokeT = 0.08;
          Particles.smoke.emit(h.pos.x, h.pos.y - 0.5, h.pos.z, rnd(-1, 1), -2, rnd(-1, 1), 1.6, 0.8, 2.6, 0.15, 0.13, 0.13, 0.6, -1, 0.5);
        }
      }
    }
    // pose
    h.grp.position.copy(h.pos);
    h.grp.rotation.set(h.pitch, h.yaw, h.bank, 'YXZ');
    // spotlight + volumetric cone
    if (h.state !== 'down') {
      const wob1 = Math.sin(Tick.t * 2.4) * 2.4 + Math.sin(Tick.t * 0.9) * 1.6;
      const wob2 = Math.cos(Tick.t * 2.1) * 2.4 + Math.sin(Tick.t * 1.3) * 1.6;
      const tgx = px + wob1, tgz = pz + wob2;
      const tgy = groundHeightAt(tgx, tgz);
      h.spotTarget.position.set(tgx, tgy, tgz);
      _v2.setFromMatrixPosition(h.spot.matrixWorld);
      h.cone.position.copy(h.pos);
      h.cone.position.y -= 1.1;
      _v3.set(tgx - h.cone.position.x, tgy - h.cone.position.y, tgz - h.cone.position.z);
      const len = _v3.length();
      _v3.normalize();
      _v4.set(0, -1, 0);
      h.cone.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(_v4, _v3);
      h.cone.scale.set(1, len, 1);
      h.groundGlow.position.set(tgx, tgy + 0.25, tgz);
    }
  },

  // ---------- main update ----------
  update(dt, px, py, pz, pvx, pvz, playerInCar, pSpeed) {
    const stars = Wanted.stars;
    // collect active police cars
    this.cars.length = 0;
    for (const v of Vehicles.list) {
      if (v.active && v.state === 'police' && v.cop) this.cars.push(v);
    }
    // drive them
    for (const v of this.cars) {
      if (v.state === 'dead') continue;
      if (stars === 0) {
        // stand down: convert to traffic and drive off
        v.sirenOn = false;
        v.cop = null;
        v.state = 'traffic';
        Vehicles.assignRoute(v);
        continue;
      }
      if (v.cop.mode === 'block') {
        v.cop.blockT -= dt;
        v.inBrake = 1; v.inThrottle = 0;
        const d = Math.hypot(v.x - px, v.z - pz);
        if (v.cop.blockT <= 0) {
          if (d < 60) v.cop.mode = 'chase';
          else { v.sirenOn = false; v.cop = null; v.state = 'traffic'; Vehicles.assignRoute(v); }
        }
        continue;
      }
      this.driveCruiser(v, dt, px, pz, pvx, pvz, playerInCar, pSpeed);
    }
    // spawn cruisers up to budget
    if (stars > 0) {
      // nearby patrol cars join the pursuit immediately
      for (const v of Vehicles.list) {
        if (!v.active || v.model !== 'police' || v.cop || v.state !== 'traffic') continue;
        if (Math.hypot(v.x - px, v.z - pz) > 220) continue;
        let alive0 = 0;
        for (const c of this.cars) if (c.state !== 'dead') alive0++;
        if (alive0 >= this.BUDGET_CARS[stars]) break;
        v.state = 'police';
        v.cop = { mode: 'chase', pitT: 0, stuckT: 0, revT: 0, unloaded: false };
        v.sirenOn = true;
        this.cars.push(v);
      }
      this.spawnT -= dt;
      if (this.spawnT <= 0) {
        this.spawnT = 2.6;
        let alive = 0, swat = 0;
        for (const v of this.cars) {
          if (v.state !== 'dead') {
            if (v.model === 'swat') swat++; else alive++;
          }
        }
        if (alive < this.BUDGET_CARS[stars]) {
          for (let a = 0; a < 4; a++) if (this.spawnCruiser(px, pz, pvx, pvz, 'police')) break;
        }
        if (swat < this.BUDGET_SWAT[stars] && stars >= 3) this.spawnCruiser(px, pz, pvx, pvz, 'swat');
      }
      // roadblocks
      this.blockT -= dt;
      if (this.blockT <= 0 && this.BUDGET_BLOCK[stars] > 0 && playerInCar) {
        this.blockT = 13;
        this.placeRoadblock(px, pz, pvx, pvz);
      }
      // helicopter
      if (stars >= 5 && !this.heli.alive) this.spawnHeli(px, pz);
      else if (stars >= 5 && this.heli.alive && this.heli.state === 'leave') this.heli.state = 'in';
      if (stars < 5 && this.heli.alive) this.dismissHeli();
    } else {
      // wind down foot cops + clear leftover roadblock barriers
      for (const c of this.footCops) {
        if (c.ch.active && !c.dead) { c.ch.active = false; c.ch.group.visible = false; }
      }
      for (const b of this.barriers) {
        if (b.on) { b.on = false; b.m.visible = false; }
      }
      if (this.heli.alive && this.heli.state !== 'down') this.dismissHeli();
    }
    // officers
    for (const c of this.footCops) {
      if (!c.ch.active) continue;
      this.updateOfficer(c, dt, px, py, pz, pSpeed);
    }
    // swat van unloading
    for (const v of this.cars) {
      if (v.model !== 'swat' || v.state === 'dead' || !v.cop) continue;
      const d = Math.hypot(v.x - px, v.z - pz);
      const sp = Math.hypot(v.vx, v.vz);
      if (d < 24 && sp < 2 && !v.cop.unloaded) {
        v.cop.unloaded = true;
        this.deployOfficers(v, 3, 'swat');
      }
      if (d > 30) v.cop.unloaded = false;
    }
    // run over officers
    const pv = Vehicles.playerVeh;
    if (pv && pSpeed > 4) {
      const fx = Math.sin(pv.heading), fz = Math.cos(pv.heading);
      for (const c of this.footCops) {
        if (!c.ch.active || c.dead) continue;
        const p = c.ch.group.position;
        const dx = p.x - pv.x, dz = p.z - pv.z;
        if (Math.abs(dx) > 4 || Math.abs(dz) > 4) continue;
        const lon = dx * fx + dz * fz, lat = Math.abs(dx * -fz + dz * fx);
        if (Math.abs(lon) < pv.def.len * 0.5 + 0.3 && lat < pv.def.wid * 0.5 + 0.3) {
          c.dead = true; c.deadT = 0;
          startRagdoll(c.ch, pv.vx * 0.9, pSpeed * 0.34, pv.vz * 0.9, 1);
          Wanted.crime('killCop', null);
          AudioFX.thud(p, 0.8);
          Shake.add(0.12);
        }
      }
    }
    this.updateBarriers(dt);
    this.updateHeli(dt, px, py, pz);
    // strobe point lights follow the nearest sirening cars
    let si = 0;
    for (const v of this.cars) {
      if (si >= this.strobes.length) break;
      if (!v.sirenOn || v.state === 'dead') continue;
      const d = Math.hypot(v.x - px, v.z - pz);
      if (d > 70) continue;
      const l = this.strobes[si++];
      const phase = (Tick.t * 6 + v.poolI) % 2 < 1;
      l.color.setHex(phase ? 0xff2222 : 0x3344ff);
      l.intensity = 48;
      l.position.set(v.x, v.y + 2.6, v.z);
    }
    for (; si < this.strobes.length; si++) this.strobes[si].intensity = 0;
  },
};
// ============================================================ procedural audio (Web Audio)
const AudioFX = {
  ctx: null, master: null, sfx: null, amb: null, musicBus: null,
  nWhite: null, nBrown: null, nPink: null,
  engine: null, screechG: null, screechBP: null, screechLvl: 0,
  sirens: [], sirenTick: 0, aiEng: [], aiTick: 0,
  rotor: null, oceanG: null, cityG: null, waveT: 6,
  camRight: new THREE.Vector3(), camFwd: new THREE.Vector3(),

  start() {
    if (this.ctx) return;
    const ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    this.ctx = ctx;
    if (ctx.state === 'suspended') { const p = ctx.resume(); if (p && p.catch) p.catch(() => {}); }
    const comp = ctx.createDynamicsCompressor();
    comp.threshold.value = -18; comp.knee.value = 12; comp.ratio.value = 4;
    comp.attack.value = 0.005; comp.release.value = 0.25;
    comp.connect(ctx.destination);
    this.master = ctx.createGain(); this.master.gain.value = 0.8;
    this.master.connect(comp);
    this.sfx = ctx.createGain(); this.sfx.gain.value = 0.9; this.sfx.connect(this.master);
    this.amb = ctx.createGain(); this.amb.gain.value = 0.5; this.amb.connect(this.master);
    this.musicBus = ctx.createGain(); this.musicBus.gain.value = 0.5; this.musicBus.connect(this.master);
    // noise buffers
    const len = ctx.sampleRate * 2;
    const mk = (fill) => {
      const b = ctx.createBuffer(1, len, ctx.sampleRate);
      fill(b.getChannelData(0));
      return b;
    };
    this.nWhite = mk(d => { for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) d[i] = Math.random() * 2 - 1; });
    this.nBrown = mk(d => {
      let b = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) { b += (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.02; b *= 0.998; d[i] = b * 18; }
    });
    this.nPink = mk(d => {
      let b0 = 0, b1 = 0, b2 = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
        const w = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
        b0 = 0.997 * b0 + 0.029 * w; b1 = 0.985 * b1 + 0.032 * w; b2 = 0.95 * b2 + 0.048 * w;
        d[i] = (b0 + b1 + b2 + w * 0.05) * 2.2;
      }
    });
    this.buildEngine();
    this.buildScreech();
    this.buildSirens();
    this.buildRotor();
    this.buildBeds();
    Music.start();
  },
  noiseSrc(buf, loop) {
    const s = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
    s.buffer = buf; s.loop = !!loop;
    if (!loop) s.playbackRate.value = 1;
    return s;
  },
  spatial(x, z, ref) {
    const dx = x - camera.position.x, dz = z - camera.position.z;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
    const g = ref / (ref + d);
    let pan = 0;
    if (d > 0.5) {
      pan = clamp((dx * this.camRight.x + dz * this.camRight.z) / d * 0.8, -1, 1);
    }
    return { g, pan, d };
  },
  // one-shot helper: src -> filter? -> gain(env) -> pan -> sfx
  shotChain(pan, g) {
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    const p = ctx.createStereoPanner();
    p.pan.value = pan;
    gain.connect(p); p.connect(this.sfx);
    gain.gain.value = g;
    return { gain, pan: p };
  },

  // ---------- engine (player) ----------
  buildEngine() {
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    const shaper = ctx.createWaveShaper();
    const curve = new Float32Array(256);
    for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) { const x = (i / 128) - 1; curve[i] = Math.tanh(3 * x); }
    shaper.curve = curve;
    const lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); lp.type = 'lowpass'; lp.frequency.value = 700; lp.Q.value = 1;
    const out = ctx.createGain(); out.gain.value = 0;
    shaper.connect(lp); lp.connect(out); out.connect(this.sfx);
    const oscA = ctx.createOscillator(); oscA.type = 'sawtooth'; oscA.frequency.value = 30;
    const gA = ctx.createGain(); gA.gain.value = 0.25; oscA.connect(gA); gA.connect(shaper);
    const oscB = ctx.createOscillator(); oscB.type = 'sawtooth'; oscB.frequency.value = 60; oscB.detune.value = 8;
    const gB = ctx.createGain(); gB.gain.value = 0.12; oscB.connect(gB); gB.connect(shaper);
    const oscC = ctx.createOscillator(); oscC.type = 'square'; oscC.frequency.value = 15;
    const gC = ctx.createGain(); gC.gain.value = 0.18; oscC.connect(gC); gC.connect(shaper);
    const noise = this.noiseSrc(this.nWhite, true);
    const nbp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); nbp.type = 'bandpass'; nbp.frequency.value = 1200; nbp.Q.value = 0.8;
    const nG = ctx.createGain(); nG.gain.value = 0.02;
    noise.connect(nbp); nbp.connect(nG); nG.connect(lp);
    oscA.start(); oscB.start(); oscC.start(); noise.start();
    this.engine = { oscA, oscB, oscC, nG, lp, out, lastGear: 0, dipT: 0 };
  },
  engineUpdate(on, speed01, throttle) {
    if (!this.ctx || !this.engine) return;
    const e = this.engine, t = this.ctx.currentTime;
    if (!on) { e.out.gain.setTargetAtTime(0, t, 0.08); return; }
    const s = clamp(speed01, 0, 1);
    const gear = Math.min(3, Math.floor(s * 4));
    const frac = s * 4 - gear;
    const rpmN = 0.2 + 0.8 * clamp(frac, 0, 1);
    const f = 30 + 125 * rpmN;
    if (gear !== e.lastGear) { e.lastGear = gear; e.dipT = 0.1; }
    const dip = e.dipT > 0 ? 0.55 : 1;
    e.dipT -= Tick.rawDt;
    e.oscA.frequency.setTargetAtTime(f, t, 0.02);
    e.oscB.frequency.setTargetAtTime(f * 2, t, 0.02);
    e.oscC.frequency.setTargetAtTime(f * 0.5, t, 0.02);
    e.lp.frequency.setTargetAtTime((420 + 3000 * throttle) * dip, t, 0.05);
    e.nG.gain.setTargetAtTime(0.02 + 0.06 * throttle, t, 0.05);
    e.out.gain.setTargetAtTime((0.16 + 0.2 * throttle + 0.08 * s) * dip, t, 0.05);
  },
  // cheap AI engine pool
  buildAIEngine() {
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    const lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); lp.type = 'lowpass'; lp.frequency.value = 900; lp.Q.value = 0.7;
    const g = ctx.createGain(); g.gain.value = 0;
    const pan = ctx.createStereoPanner();
    lp.connect(g); g.connect(pan); pan.connect(this.sfx);
    const oscA = ctx.createOscillator(); oscA.type = 'sawtooth'; oscA.frequency.value = 40;
    const gA = ctx.createGain(); gA.gain.value = 0.2; oscA.connect(gA); gA.connect(lp);
    const oscC = ctx.createOscillator(); oscC.type = 'square'; oscC.frequency.value = 20;
    const gC = ctx.createGain(); gC.gain.value = 0.15; oscC.connect(gC); gC.connect(lp);
    oscA.start(); oscC.start();
    return { oscA, oscC, g, pan, veh: null };
  },
  updateAIEngines() {
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    this.aiTick -= Tick.rawDt;
    if (this.aiTick > 0) return;
    this.aiTick = 0.1;
    if (this.aiEng.length === 0) {
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) this.aiEng.push(this.buildAIEngine());
    }
    const t = this.ctx.currentTime;
    // pick the two loudest candidates (police first)
    let c0 = null, c1 = null, d0 = 1e9, d1 = 1e9;
    for (const v of Vehicles._active) {
      if (v === Vehicles.playerVeh || v.state === 'dead' || v.state === 'parked') continue;
      const sp = Math.hypot(v.vx, v.vz);
      if (sp < 3) continue;
      const s = this.spatial(v.x, v.z, 14);
      const score = s.d - (v.isPolice ? 25 : 0);
      if (score < d0) { d1 = d0; c1 = c0; d0 = score; c0 = v; }
      else if (score < d1) { d1 = score; c1 = v; }
    }
    const cands = [c0, c1];
    this.aiEng.forEach((e, i) => {
      const v = cands[i];
      if (!v) { e.g.gain.setTargetAtTime(0, t, 0.15); e.veh = null; return; }
      const sp = Math.hypot(v.vx, v.vz);
      const s = this.spatial(v.x, v.z, 14);
      if (s.d > 65) { e.g.gain.setTargetAtTime(0, t, 0.2); return; }
      const f = 35 + 110 * clamp(sp / v.def.top, 0, 1);
      e.oscA.frequency.setTargetAtTime(f, t, 0.06);
      e.oscC.frequency.setTargetAtTime(f * 0.5, t, 0.06);
      e.g.gain.setTargetAtTime(0.22 * s.g, t, 0.12);
      e.pan.pan.setTargetAtTime(s.pan, t, 0.1);
    });
  },

  // ---------- tire screech ----------
  buildScreech() {
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    const bp1 = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); bp1.type = 'bandpass'; bp1.frequency.value = 2400; bp1.Q.value = 12;
    const bp2 = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); bp2.type = 'bandpass'; bp2.frequency.value = 700; bp2.Q.value = 2;
    const g = ctx.createGain(); g.gain.value = 0;
    const n1 = this.noiseSrc(this.nWhite, true);
    const g2 = ctx.createGain(); g2.gain.value = 0.4;
    n1.connect(bp1); bp1.connect(g);
    n1.connect(bp2); bp2.connect(g2); g2.connect(g);
    g.connect(this.sfx);
    n1.start();
    this.screechG = g; this.screechBP = bp1;
  },
  screech(intensity) { this.screechLvl = Math.max(this.screechLvl, clamp(intensity, 0, 1)); },
  updateScreech() {
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    const t = this.ctx.currentTime;
    if (this.screechLvl > 0.01) {
      this.screechG.gain.setTargetAtTime(this.screechLvl * 0.34, t, 0.05);
      this.screechBP.frequency.setTargetAtTime(1900 + Math.sin(Tick.t * 8) * 250 + rnd(-150, 150), t, 0.04);
    } else {
      this.screechG.gain.setTargetAtTime(0, t, 0.12);
    }
    this.screechLvl *= Math.exp(-7 * Tick.rawDt);
  },

  // ---------- gunfire ----------
  shot(kind, x, y, z) {
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    const ctx = this.ctx, t = ctx.currentTime;
    const s = this.spatial(x, z, 26);
    if (s.g < 0.03) return;
    const ch = this.shotChain(s.pan, 1);
    const isP = kind === 'pistol', isS = kind === 'smg';
    if (kind === 'rocket') {
      const n = this.noiseSrc(this.nWhite, false);
      n.playbackRate.value = 0.9;
      const bp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); bp.type = 'bandpass'; bp.Q.value = 1.5; bp.frequency.setValueAtTime(300, t);
      bp.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(1400, t + 0.3);
      bp.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(500, t + 1.1);
      const g = ctx.createGain();
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
      g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.6 * s.g, t + 0.12);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 1.3);
      n.connect(bp); bp.connect(g); g.connect(ch.gain);
      n.start(t, rnd(0.4));
      n.stop(t + 1.4);
      const br = this.noiseSrc(this.nBrown, false);
      const lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); lp.type = 'lowpass'; lp.frequency.value = 250;
      const g2 = ctx.createGain();
      g2.gain.setValueAtTime(0.5 * s.g, t);
      g2.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 1.0);
      br.connect(lp); lp.connect(g2); g2.connect(ch.gain);
      br.start(t, rnd(0.5)); br.stop(t + 1.1);
      return;
    }
    const dec = isP ? 1 : 0.55;
    // crack
    const crack = this.noiseSrc(this.nWhite, false);
    const hp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); hp.type = 'highpass'; hp.frequency.value = 1000;
    const cg = ctx.createGain();
    cg.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
    cg.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.85 * s.g * rnd(0.85, 1.1), t + 0.002);
    cg.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.11 * dec);
    crack.connect(hp); hp.connect(cg); cg.connect(ch.gain);
    crack.start(t, rnd(1.5)); crack.stop(t + 0.2);
    // body
    const body = this.noiseSrc(this.nWhite, false);
    const lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); lp.type = 'lowpass'; lp.frequency.value = 420;
    const bg = ctx.createGain();
    bg.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
    bg.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.7 * s.g, t + 0.003);
    bg.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.16 * dec);
    body.connect(lp); lp.connect(bg); bg.connect(ch.gain);
    body.start(t, rnd(1.5)); body.stop(t + 0.25);
    // thump
    const o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = 'sine';
    o.frequency.setValueAtTime(isS ? 140 : 165, t);
    o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(isS ? 62 : 55, t + (isS ? 0.05 : 0.1));
    o.detune.value = rnd(-50, 50);
    const og = ctx.createGain();
    og.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
    og.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.9 * s.g, t + 0.003);
    og.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.14 * dec);
    o.connect(og); og.connect(ch.gain);
    o.start(t); o.stop(t + 0.2);
  },
  explosion(x, y, z, power) {
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    const ctx = this.ctx, t = ctx.currentTime;
    const s = this.spatial(x, z, 60);
    const ch = this.shotChain(s.pan, 1);
    // sub
    const o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = 'sine';
    o.frequency.setValueAtTime(110, t);
    o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(30, t + 1.2);
    const og = ctx.createGain();
    og.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
    og.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(1.0 * s.g * power, t + 0.006);
    og.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 1.4);
    o.connect(og); og.connect(ch.gain);
    o.start(t); o.stop(t + 1.5);
    // boom
    const br = this.noiseSrc(this.nBrown, false);
    const lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); lp.type = 'lowpass'; lp.Q.value = 0.7;
    lp.frequency.setValueAtTime(3000, t);
    lp.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(120, t + 1.8);
    const bg = ctx.createGain();
    bg.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
    bg.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(1.15 * s.g * power, t + 0.008);
    bg.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 2.0);
    br.connect(lp); lp.connect(bg); bg.connect(ch.gain);
    br.start(t, rnd(0.3)); br.stop(t + 2.1);
    // crackle grains
    const grains = Math.floor(18 * power);
    for (let i = 0; i < grains; i++) {
      const gt = t + 0.25 + Math.random() * Math.random() * 2.0;
      const gr = this.noiseSrc(this.nWhite, false);
      const ghp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); ghp.type = 'highpass'; ghp.frequency.value = 2500;
      const gg = ctx.createGain();
      gg.gain.setValueAtTime(0, gt);
      gg.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(rnd(0.1, 0.26) * s.g, gt + 0.004);
      gg.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, gt + rnd(0.015, 0.04));
      gr.connect(ghp); ghp.connect(gg); gg.connect(ch.gain);
      gr.start(gt, rnd(1.8)); gr.stop(gt + 0.08);
    }
    Music.duck();
  },
  crash(x, z, amt) {
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    const ctx = this.ctx, t = ctx.currentTime;
    const s = this.spatial(x, z, 22);
    if (s.g < 0.04) return;
    const ch = this.shotChain(s.pan, 1);
    const n = this.noiseSrc(this.nWhite, false);
    const bp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); bp.type = 'bandpass'; bp.frequency.value = rnd(700, 1300); bp.Q.value = 1.2;
    const g = ctx.createGain();
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.8 * amt * s.g, t + 0.004);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.35);
    n.connect(bp); bp.connect(g); g.connect(ch.gain);
    n.start(t, rnd(1.5)); n.stop(t + 0.4);
    const o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = 'sine';
    o.frequency.setValueAtTime(120, t);
    o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(45, t + 0.18);
    const og = ctx.createGain();
    og.gain.setValueAtTime(0.7 * amt * s.g, t);
    og.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.25);
    o.connect(og); og.connect(ch.gain);
    o.start(t); o.stop(t + 0.3);
  },
  thud(pos, amt) {
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    const ctx = this.ctx, t = ctx.currentTime;
    const s = this.spatial(pos.x, pos.z, 16);
    if (s.g < 0.05) return;
    const ch = this.shotChain(s.pan, 1);
    const o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = 'sine';
    o.frequency.setValueAtTime(95, t);
    o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(40, t + 0.12);
    const g = ctx.createGain();
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.55 * amt * s.g, t);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.18);
    o.connect(g); g.connect(ch.gain);
    o.start(t); o.stop(t + 0.2);
  },
  honk(x, z) {
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    const ctx = this.ctx, t = ctx.currentTime;
    const s = this.spatial(x, z, 20);
    if (s.g < 0.05) return;
    const ch = this.shotChain(s.pan, 1);
    const o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = 'square';
    o.frequency.value = pick([310, 350, 392, 415]);
    const g = ctx.createGain();
    const dur = rnd(0.18, 0.5);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.12 * s.g, t + 0.02);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12 * s.g, t + dur);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur + 0.04);
    const lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); lp.type = 'lowpass'; lp.frequency.value = 1500;
    o.connect(lp); lp.connect(g); g.connect(ch.gain);
    o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + 0.1);
  },
  scream(pos) {
    if (!this.ctx || Math.random() < 0.6) return;
    const ctx = this.ctx, t = ctx.currentTime;
    const s = this.spatial(pos.x, pos.z, 18);
    if (s.g < 0.05) return;
    const ch = this.shotChain(s.pan, 1);
    const o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = 'sawtooth';
    const f0 = rnd(600, 950);
    o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0, t);
    o.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(f0 * rnd(1.15, 1.4), t + 0.12);
    o.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(f0 * 0.8, t + 0.3);
    const lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); lp.type = 'lowpass'; lp.frequency.value = 1800;
    const g = ctx.createGain();
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.06 * s.g, t + 0.03);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.35);
    o.connect(lp); lp.connect(g); g.connect(ch.gain);
    o.start(t); o.stop(t + 0.4);
  },
  casing(x, z) {
    if (!this.ctx || Math.random() < 0.5) return;
    const ctx = this.ctx, t = ctx.currentTime;
    const s = this.spatial(x, z, 6);
    if (s.g < 0.1) return;
    const o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = 'triangle';
    o.frequency.value = rnd(3800, 5200);
    const g = ctx.createGain();
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05 * s.g, t);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.05);
    o.connect(g); g.connect(this.sfx);
    o.start(t); o.stop(t + 0.06);
  },
  weaponSwitch() { this.click(1400, 0.06); },
  dryFire() { this.click(900, 0.05); },
  reload() { this.click(700, 0.08); this.click(1100, 0.07, 0.09); },
  click(f, g0, delay) {
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    const ctx = this.ctx, t = ctx.currentTime + (delay || 0);
    const o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = 'square'; o.frequency.value = f;
    const g = ctx.createGain();
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(g0, t);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.04);
    o.connect(g); g.connect(this.sfx);
    o.start(t); o.stop(t + 0.05);
  },
  pickup() {
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    const ctx = this.ctx, t = ctx.currentTime;
    [523.25, 659.25, 783.99].forEach((f, i) => {
      const o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = 'sine'; o.frequency.value = f;
      const g = ctx.createGain();
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t + i * 0.07);
      g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.14, t + i * 0.07 + 0.02);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + i * 0.07 + 0.35);
      o.connect(g); g.connect(this.sfx);
      o.start(t + i * 0.07); o.stop(t + i * 0.07 + 0.4);
    });
  },
  copShout(x, z) {
    if (!this.ctx || Math.random() < 0.5) return;
    const ctx = this.ctx, t = ctx.currentTime;
    const s = this.spatial(x, z, 16);
    if (s.g < 0.06) return;
    // radio chirp
    const o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = 'square'; o.frequency.value = 1200;
    const g = ctx.createGain();
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04 * s.g, t);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.07);
    o.connect(g); g.connect(this.sfx);
    o.start(t); o.stop(t + 0.08);
  },
  heliGun() {},
  heliHit() { Music.duck(); },
  splashSfx(x, z, big) {
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    const ctx = this.ctx, t = ctx.currentTime;
    const s = this.spatial(x, z, 24);
    if (s.g < 0.05) return;
    const ch = this.shotChain(s.pan, 1);
    const n = this.noiseSrc(this.nWhite, false);
    const lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); lp.type = 'lowpass'; lp.frequency.value = 900;
    const g = ctx.createGain();
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime((big ? 0.5 : 0.2) * s.g, t + 0.03);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + (big ? 0.9 : 0.4));
    n.connect(lp); lp.connect(g); g.connect(ch.gain);
    n.start(t, rnd(1)); n.stop(t + 1);
  },

  // ---------- sirens (pooled, doppler) ----------
  buildSirens() {
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const lfo = ctx.createOscillator(); lfo.type = 'triangle'; lfo.frequency.value = 0.22 + i * 0.03;
      const lfoG = ctx.createGain(); lfoG.gain.value = 437;
      const car = ctx.createOscillator(); car.type = 'sawtooth'; car.frequency.value = 1162;
      const car2 = ctx.createOscillator(); car2.type = 'sawtooth'; car2.frequency.value = 2324;
      const lfoG2 = ctx.createGain(); lfoG2.gain.value = 874;
      lfo.connect(lfoG); lfoG.connect(car.frequency);
      lfo.connect(lfoG2); lfoG2.connect(car2.frequency);
      const lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); lp.type = 'lowpass'; lp.frequency.value = 4000; lp.Q.value = 0.7;
      const g = ctx.createGain(); g.gain.value = 0;
      const g2 = ctx.createGain(); g2.gain.value = 0.25;
      const pan = ctx.createStereoPanner();
      car.connect(lp); car2.connect(g2); g2.connect(lp);
      lp.connect(g); g.connect(pan); pan.connect(this.sfx);
      lfo.start(); car.start(); car2.start();
      this.sirens.push({ car, car2, g, pan, veh: null, lastD: 0, vRel: 0 });
    }
  },
  updateSirens() {
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    const t = this.ctx.currentTime;
    this.sirenTick -= Tick.rawDt;
    if (this.sirenTick <= 0) {
      this.sirenTick = 0.2;
      // rank siren cars by distance
      for (const sv of this.sirens) sv._keep = false;
      const used = [];
      for (let k = 0; k < this.sirens.length; k++) {
        let best = null, bd = 1e9;
        for (const v of Police.cars) {
          if (!v.sirenOn || v.state === 'dead' || used.includes(v)) continue;
          const d = Math.hypot(v.x - camera.position.x, v.z - camera.position.z);
          if (d < bd && d < 190) { bd = d; best = v; }
        }
        if (best) {
          used.push(best);
          const voice = this.sirens[k];
          if (voice.veh !== best) { voice.veh = best; voice.lastD = bd; voice.vRel = 0; }
          voice._keep = true;
        }
      }
      for (const sv of this.sirens) if (!sv._keep) sv.veh = null;
    }
    for (const sv of this.sirens) {
      if (!sv.veh || !sv.veh.active || !sv.veh.sirenOn) {
        sv.g.gain.setTargetAtTime(0, t, 0.2);
        continue;
      }
      const v = sv.veh;
      const s = this.spatial(v.x, v.z, 16);
      const vRelRaw = (sv.lastD - s.d) / Math.max(Tick.rawDt, 0.001);
      sv.lastD = s.d;
      sv.vRel = lerp(sv.vRel, clamp(vRelRaw, -60, 60), 0.08);
      const ratio = clamp(343 / (343 - sv.vRel), 0.8, 1.35);
      const cents = 1200 * Math.log2(ratio);
      sv.car.detune.setTargetAtTime(cents, t, 0.1);
      sv.car2.detune.setTargetAtTime(cents, t, 0.1);
      sv.g.gain.setTargetAtTime(0.42 * s.g, t, 0.15);
      sv.pan.pan.setTargetAtTime(s.pan, t, 0.1);
    }
  },

  // ---------- helicopter rotor ----------
  buildRotor() {
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    const slap = this.noiseSrc(this.nBrown, true);
    const lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); lp.type = 'lowpass'; lp.frequency.value = 600; lp.Q.value = 0.7;
    const am = ctx.createGain(); am.gain.value = 0.3;
    const lfo = ctx.createOscillator(); lfo.type = 'sine'; lfo.frequency.value = 15.5;
    const shaper = ctx.createWaveShaper();
    const curve = new Float32Array(64);
    for (let i = 0; i < 64; i++) { const x = (i / 32) - 1; curve[i] = Math.max(0, x) ** 3; }
    shaper.curve = curve;
    const lfoG = ctx.createGain(); lfoG.gain.value = 0.7;
    lfo.connect(shaper); shaper.connect(lfoG); lfoG.connect(am.gain);
    const distLP = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); distLP.type = 'lowpass'; distLP.frequency.value = 300;
    const out = ctx.createGain(); out.gain.value = 0;
    const pan = ctx.createStereoPanner();
    slap.connect(lp); lp.connect(am); am.connect(distLP);
    const rumble = this.noiseSrc(this.nBrown, true);
    const rlp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); rlp.type = 'lowpass'; rlp.frequency.value = 150;
    const rg = ctx.createGain(); rg.gain.value = 0.3;
    rumble.connect(rlp); rlp.connect(rg); rg.connect(distLP);
    distLP.connect(out); out.connect(pan); pan.connect(this.sfx);
    slap.start(); rumble.start(); lfo.start();
    this.rotor = { out, distLP, pan };
  },
  updateRotor() {
    if (!this.ctx || !this.rotor) return;
    const t = this.ctx.currentTime;
    const h = Police.heli;
    if (!h || !h.alive) { this.rotor.out.gain.setTargetAtTime(0, t, 0.4); return; }
    const dx = h.pos.x - camera.position.x, dz = h.pos.z - camera.position.z, dy = h.pos.y - camera.position.y;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy, dz);
    const g = 0.55 * 30 / (30 + d);
    this.rotor.out.gain.setTargetAtTime(g, t, 0.2);
    this.rotor.distLP.frequency.setTargetAtTime(Math.max(250, 3600 * (1 - d / 420)), t, 0.2);
    let pan = 0;
    if (d > 1) pan = clamp((dx * this.camRight.x + dz * this.camRight.z) / d, -1, 1) * 0.7;
    this.rotor.pan.pan.setTargetAtTime(pan, t, 0.2);
  },

  // ---------- ambient beds ----------
  buildBeds() {
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    // ocean
    const ocean = this.noiseSrc(this.nPink, true);
    const olp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); olp.type = 'lowpass'; olp.frequency.value = 700; olp.Q.value = 0.5;
    const og = ctx.createGain(); og.gain.value = 0;
    ocean.connect(olp); olp.connect(og); og.connect(this.amb);
    const lfo = ctx.createOscillator(); lfo.frequency.value = 0.07;
    const lfoG = ctx.createGain(); lfoG.gain.value = 280;
    lfo.connect(lfoG); lfoG.connect(olp.frequency);
    ocean.start(); lfo.start();
    this.oceanG = og;
    // city hum
    const city = this.noiseSrc(this.nBrown, true);
    const clp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); clp.type = 'lowpass'; clp.frequency.value = 220;
    const cg = ctx.createGain(); cg.gain.value = 0;
    city.connect(clp); clp.connect(cg); cg.connect(this.amb);
    city.start();
    this.cityG = cg;
  },
  updateBeds() {
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    const t = this.ctx.currentTime;
    const z = camera.position.z;
    const beach = clamp((z - 140) / 110, 0, 1);
    const swell = 0.5 + Math.sin(Tick.t * 0.44) * 0.25 + Math.sin(Tick.t * 0.69) * 0.15;
    this.oceanG.gain.setTargetAtTime(0.30 * beach * (0.5 + swell * 0.5) + 0.02, t, 0.5);
    this.cityG.gain.setTargetAtTime(0.16 * (1 - beach * 0.7), t, 0.5);
    // occasional wave break
    this.waveT -= Tick.rawDt;
    if (this.waveT <= 0 && beach > 0.4) {
      this.waveT = rnd(7, 13);
      const n = this.noiseSrc(this.nWhite, false);
      const bp = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter(); bp.type = 'bandpass'; bp.frequency.value = 1100; bp.Q.value = 0.7;
      const g = this.ctx.createGain();
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
      g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.16 * beach, t + 1.4);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 4.2);
      n.connect(bp); bp.connect(g); g.connect(this.amb);
      n.start(t, rnd(0.5)); n.stop(t + 4.4);
    }
  },

  update() {
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    _v1.set(1, 0, 0).applyQuaternion(camera.quaternion);
    this.camRight.copy(_v1);
    this.updateScreech();
    this.updateSirens();
    this.updateAIEngines();
    this.updateRotor();
    this.updateBeds();
  },
};

// ============================================================ synthwave music
const Music = {
  BPM: 104, step: 0, nextT: 0, timer: null, tier: 0, barTier: 0,
  g: {}, padLP: null, leadDelay: null, leadWet: null, duckT: 0,
  mtof(m) { return 440 * Math.pow(2, (m - 69) / 12); },
  start() {
    const ctx = AudioFX.ctx;
    this.padLP = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); this.padLP.type = 'lowpass'; this.padLP.frequency.value = 800; this.padLP.Q.value = 0.5;
    for (const k of ['kick', 'snare', 'hat', 'bass', 'pad', 'lead', 'ohat']) {
      this.g[k] = ctx.createGain();
      this.g[k].gain.value = 0;
      this.g[k].connect(AudioFX.musicBus);
    }
    this.padLP.connect(this.g.pad);
    // lead delay (dotted 8th)
    this.leadDelay = ctx.createDelay(1.0);
    this.leadDelay.delayTime.value = 0.4327;
    const fb = ctx.createGain(); fb.gain.value = 0.35;
    this.leadWet = ctx.createGain(); this.leadWet.gain.value = 0.3;
    this.leadDelay.connect(fb); fb.connect(this.leadDelay);
    this.leadDelay.connect(this.leadWet); this.leadWet.connect(this.g.lead);
    this.g.pad.gain.value = 0.9;
    this.g.bass.gain.value = 0.9;
    this.nextT = ctx.currentTime + 0.15;
    this.step = 0;
    this.timer = setInterval(() => this.tick(), 25);
    this.setIntensity(0);
  },
  duck() {
    if (!AudioFX.ctx) return;
    const t = AudioFX.ctx.currentTime;
    AudioFX.musicBus.gain.cancelScheduledValues(t);
    AudioFX.musicBus.gain.setValueAtTime(AudioFX.musicBus.gain.value, t);
    AudioFX.musicBus.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.2, t + 0.05);
    AudioFX.musicBus.gain.setTargetAtTime(0.5 + this.tier * 0.03, t + 0.7, 0.4);
  },
  setIntensity(stars) {
    this.tier = stars;
    if (!AudioFX.ctx) return;
    const t = AudioFX.ctx.currentTime;
    const set = (k, v) => this.g[k].gain.setTargetAtTime(v, t, 0.5);
    set('pad', 0.9);
    set('bass', 0.95);
    set('kick', stars >= 1 ? 1 : 0);
    set('hat', stars >= 1 ? 0.8 : 0);
    set('snare', stars >= 2 ? 0.85 : 0);
    set('lead', stars >= 4 ? 0.8 : 0);
    set('ohat', stars >= 5 ? 0.6 : 0);
    const cuts = [800, 1000, 1400, 2000, 3500, 6000];
    this.padLP.frequency.setTargetAtTime(cuts[stars], t, 0.8);
    AudioFX.musicBus.gain.setTargetAtTime(0.5 + stars * 0.03, t, 0.8);
    if (this.leadWet) this.leadWet.gain.setTargetAtTime(stars >= 5 ? 0.45 : 0.3, t, 0.5);
  },
  tick() {
    const ctx = AudioFX.ctx;
    if (!ctx) return;
    const SIX = 60 / this.BPM / 4;
    while (this.nextT < ctx.currentTime + 0.13) {
      this.schedule(this.step, this.nextT);
      this.nextT += SIX;
      this.step = (this.step + 1) % 64;
    }
  },
  schedule(step, t) {
    const ctx = AudioFX.ctx;
    const bar = (step / 16) | 0, s16 = step % 16;
    if (s16 === 0) this.barTier = this.tier;
    const tier = this.barTier;
    // kick: four on the floor
    if (s16 % 4 === 0) this.kick(t, tier >= 3);
    // snare on 2 & 4
    if (s16 === 4 || s16 === 12) this.snare(t);
    // hats
    if (tier >= 3) { this.hat(t, s16 % 2 === 1 ? 0.22 : 0.1); }
    else if (s16 % 2 === 0) this.hat(t, 0.2);
    if (s16 === 2 || s16 === 6 || s16 === 10 || s16 === 14) this.openHat(t);
    // bass
    const roots = [33, 29, 36, 31];
    const pat = [0, 0, 12, 0, 0, 12, 0, 12, 0, 0, 12, 0, 7, 12, 0, 12];
    if (tier >= 2 || s16 % 2 === 0) this.bass(t, roots[bar] + pat[s16]);
    // pads at bar start
    if (s16 === 0) {
      const chords = [[57, 60, 64], [53, 57, 60], [55, 60, 64], [55, 59, 62]];
      this.pad(t, chords[bar]);
    }
    // lead arp
    const leads = [[69, 72, 76, 81], [69, 72, 77, 81], [67, 72, 76, 79], [67, 71, 74, 79]];
    if (tier >= 5) this.lead(t, leads[bar][s16 % 4]);
    else if (tier >= 4 && s16 % 2 === 0) this.lead(t, leads[bar][(s16 / 2) % 4]);
  },
  kick(t, click) {
    const ctx = AudioFX.ctx;
    const o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = 'sine';
    o.frequency.setValueAtTime(160, t);
    o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(55, t + 0.1);
    const g = ctx.createGain();
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(1.0, t + 0.003);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.25);
    o.connect(g); g.connect(this.g.kick);
    o.start(t); o.stop(t + 0.3);
    if (click) {
      const n = AudioFX.noiseSrc(AudioFX.nWhite, false);
      const hp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); hp.type = 'highpass'; hp.frequency.value = 2000;
      const cg = ctx.createGain();
      cg.gain.setValueAtTime(0.3, t);
      cg.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.012);
      n.connect(hp); hp.connect(cg); cg.connect(this.g.kick);
      n.start(t, rnd(1)); n.stop(t + 0.02);
    }
  },
  snare(t) {
    const ctx = AudioFX.ctx;
    for (const off of [-0.024, -0.012, 0]) {
      const n = AudioFX.noiseSrc(AudioFX.nWhite, false);
      const bp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); bp.type = 'bandpass'; bp.frequency.value = 1800; bp.Q.value = 0.9;
      const g = ctx.createGain();
      const peak = off === 0 ? 0.8 : 0.28;
      const tt = t + Math.max(0, off + 0.024);
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(0, tt);
      g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(peak, tt + 0.003);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, tt + 0.18);
      n.connect(bp); bp.connect(g); g.connect(this.g.snare);
      n.start(tt, rnd(1.5)); n.stop(tt + 0.2);
    }
    const o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = 'triangle'; o.frequency.value = 190;
    const og = ctx.createGain();
    og.gain.setValueAtTime(0.3, t + 0.024);
    og.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.1);
    o.connect(og); og.connect(this.g.snare);
    o.start(t + 0.024); o.stop(t + 0.12);
  },
  hat(t, peak) {
    const ctx = AudioFX.ctx;
    const n = AudioFX.noiseSrc(AudioFX.nWhite, false);
    const hp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); hp.type = 'highpass'; hp.frequency.value = 7000;
    const g = ctx.createGain();
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(peak, t);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.04);
    n.connect(hp); hp.connect(g); g.connect(this.g.hat);
    n.start(t, rnd(1.8)); n.stop(t + 0.05);
  },
  openHat(t) {
    const ctx = AudioFX.ctx;
    const n = AudioFX.noiseSrc(AudioFX.nWhite, false);
    const hp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); hp.type = 'highpass'; hp.frequency.value = 6000;
    const g = ctx.createGain();
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.16, t);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.22);
    n.connect(hp); hp.connect(g); g.connect(this.g.ohat);
    n.start(t, rnd(1.6)); n.stop(t + 0.25);
  },
  bass(t, midi) {
    const ctx = AudioFX.ctx;
    const SIX = 60 / this.BPM / 4;
    const o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = 'sawtooth';
    o.frequency.value = this.mtof(midi);
    const lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); lp.type = 'lowpass'; lp.frequency.value = 900; lp.Q.value = 1.5;
    const g = ctx.createGain();
    const dur = SIX * 0.7;
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.34, t + 0.004);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.34, t + dur - 0.012);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
    o.connect(lp); lp.connect(g); g.connect(this.g.bass);
    o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
  },
  pad(t, notes) {
    const ctx = AudioFX.ctx;
    const barDur = (60 / this.BPM) * 4;
    for (const m of notes) {
      for (const det of [-7, 7]) {
        const o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = 'sawtooth';
        o.frequency.value = this.mtof(m);
        o.detune.value = det;
        const g = ctx.createGain();
        g.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
        g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.09, t + 0.4);
        g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.09, t + barDur);
        g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + barDur + 0.6);
        o.connect(g); g.connect(this.padLP);
        o.start(t); o.stop(t + barDur + 0.7);
      }
    }
  },
  lead(t, midi) {
    const ctx = AudioFX.ctx;
    const o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = 'square';
    o.frequency.value = this.mtof(midi);
    const lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); lp.type = 'lowpass'; lp.frequency.value = 2500;
    const g = ctx.createGain();
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.13, t + 0.004);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.13);
    o.connect(lp); lp.connect(g);
    g.connect(this.g.lead);
    g.connect(this.leadDelay);
    o.start(t); o.stop(t + 0.16);
  },
};
// ============================================================ HUD: stars, minimap, ammo, crosshair
const HUD = {
  starEls: [], mapCv: null, mapCtx: null, roads: null, ammoDirty: true,
  W: 424, scale: 0, cx: 0, titleCardT: 0,
  ICONS: {
    pistol: 'M6 9 h26 v6 h-7 l-1.5 7 h-7 l1.5 -7 h-6 a4 4 0 0 1 -4 -4 z M32 9 h8 v4 h-8 z',
    smg: 'M2 10 h32 v5 h-9 l-1 6 h-6 l1 -6 h-5 l-1.5 6 h-5 l1.5 -6 h-7 z M34 8 h10 v3 h-10 z M14 6 h12 v4 h-12 z',
    rocket: 'M2 10 h30 v7 h-30 z M32 8 a6 6 0 0 1 0 11 z M2 8 l-2 -3 h6 l2 3 z M16 17 l-2 6 h6 l1 -6 z',
  },
  init() {
    this.starEls = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#stars svg'));
    this.mapCv = elGet('map');
    this.mapCtx = this.mapCv.getContext('2d');
    // pre-render the road map
    const R = document.createElement('canvas');
    R.width = 1024; R.height = 1024;
    const ctx = R.getContext('2d');
    // world [-520..480] x, [-420..580] z  -> 1024px
    const s = 1024 / 1000;
    const wx = (x) => (x + 520) * s;
    const wz = (z) => (z + 420) * s;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#0b1626';                       // ocean
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 1024, 1024);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#221d2e';                       // main island
    ctx.fillRect(wx(-136), wz(-256), (376 + 136) * s, (250 + 256) * s);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#574a36';                       // beach
    ctx.fillRect(wx(-136), wz(246), (376 + 136) * s, 44 * s);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#221d2e';                       // port island
    ctx.fillRect(wx(-470), wz(-190), (470 - 264) * s, 380 * s);
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#cfc4cf';
    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    for (const r of ROADS) {
      ctx.lineWidth = 11 * s;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(wx(r.x1), wz(r.z1));
      ctx.lineTo(wx(r.x2), wz(r.z2));
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    // pier
    ctx.lineWidth = 7 * s;
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#8a7a5e';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(wx(150), wz(250)); ctx.lineTo(wx(150), wz(346)); ctx.stroke();
    this.roads = R;
    this.updateAmmo();
  },
  setStars(n) {
    this.starEls.forEach((el, i) => {
      const was = el.classList.contains('lit');
      const is = i < n;
      el.classList.toggle('lit', is);
      if (is && !was) {
        el.classList.remove('pop');
        void el.offsetWidth;
        el.classList.add('pop');
      }
    });
  },
  updateAmmo() {
    this.ammoDirty = false;
    const w = Weapons.cur;
    const icon = elGet('ammoIcon');
    icon.innerHTML = `<path d="${this.ICONS[w]}" />`;
    const clip = Weapons.clip[w], res = Weapons.reserve[w];
    elGet('ammoClip').textContent = String(clip);
    elGet('ammoRes').textContent = res === Infinity ? '∞' : '/ ' + res;
  },
  crosshair(on, spread) {
    const c = elGet('cross');
    c.classList.toggle('on', on);
    if (on) c.style.setProperty('--g', (3 + spread * 16).toFixed(1) + 'px');
  },
  drawMap(px, pz, heading, health01) {
    const ctx = this.mapCtx, W = this.W, c = W / 2;
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, W);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(c, c, c - 4, 0, 7);
    ctx.clip();
    const zoom = 0.95;     // canvas px per meter
    const s = 1024 / 1000;
    ctx.translate(c, c);
    ctx.rotate(heading + Math.PI);
    ctx.scale(zoom, zoom);
    ctx.translate(-(px + 520) * s, -(pz + 420) * s);
    ctx.drawImage(this.roads, 0, 0);
    // police blips
    const blip = (x, z, color, r) => {
      ctx.fillStyle = color;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc((x + 520) * s, (z + 420) * s, r, 0, 7);
      ctx.fill();
    };
    const strobe = (Tick.t * 5 | 0) % 2;
    for (const v of Vehicles.list) {
      if (!v.active || !v.isPolice || v.state === 'dead') continue;
      blip(v.x, v.z, v.sirenOn ? (strobe ? '#ff4444' : '#4466ff') : '#9fb6ff', 6);
    }
    for (const fc of Police.footCops) {
      if (!fc.ch.active || fc.dead) continue;
      blip(fc.ch.group.position.x, fc.ch.group.position.z, strobe ? '#ff6666' : '#7788ff', 3.5);
    }
    if (Police.heli.alive) {
      const h = Police.heli;
      blip(h.pos.x, h.pos.z, strobe ? '#ffb030' : '#ff5030', 8);
    }
    ctx.restore();
    // player arrow
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(c, c);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffeede';
    ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(255,200,140,0.9)';
    ctx.shadowBlur = 10;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(0, -11);
    ctx.lineTo(7.5, 9);
    ctx.lineTo(0, 4.5);
    ctx.lineTo(-7.5, 9);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
    // health arc around the rim
    const a0 = -Math.PI / 2;
    ctx.lineWidth = 7;
    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,235,215,0.13)';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(c, c, c - 9, a0, a0 + Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.stroke();
    const low = health01 < 0.3;
    const flash = low ? (0.55 + 0.45 * Math.sin(Tick.t * 7)) : 1;
    ctx.strokeStyle = low ? `rgba(255,70,70,${flash})` : 'rgba(255,235,215,0.9)';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(c, c, c - 9, a0, a0 + Math.PI * 2 * clamp(health01, 0.001, 1));
    ctx.stroke();
  },
  showTitleCard() {
    const el = elGet('titleCard');
    el.classList.add('show');
    this.titleCardT = 5;
  },
  update(dt) {
    if (this.ammoDirty) this.updateAmmo();
    if (this.titleCardT > 0) {
      this.titleCardT -= dt;
      if (this.titleCardT <= 0) elGet('titleCard').classList.remove('show');
    }
  },
};
// ============================================================ game orchestration
const Game = {
  state: 'title',           // title | flyin | play
  mode: 'foot',             // foot | drive | ragdoll | dead
  px: 152, py: 0, pz: 250.5, vy: 0,
  velX: 0, velZ: 0,
  yaw: -1.95, pitch: 0.1, heading: -1.95,
  health: 100, regenT: 0, damageFlash: 0, flash: 0, expProtT: 0,
  veh: null, player: null,
  aimBlend: 0, fireHoldT: 0, deathT: 0, flyT: 0,
  camPos: new THREE.Vector3(), camTgt: new THREE.Vector3(),
  fov: 62, headlights: [], heliDownT: -1, splashT: 0,
  driveYaw: 0, driveOrbit: 0,

  init() {
    this.player = makeCharacter('player');
    scene.add(this.player.group);
    this.player.group.position.set(this.px, 0, this.pz);
    charShowGun(this.player, 'pistol');
    // headlight rig (attached to whichever car the player drives)
    for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
      const l = new THREE.SpotLight(0xffe2b0, 0, 55, 0.45, 0.5, 1.3);
      const tgt = new THREE.Object3D();
      scene.add(l); scene.add(tgt);
      l.target = tgt;
      this.headlights.push({ l, tgt });
    }
    this.camPos.set(this.px - 60, 12, this.pz + 70);
    // start click
    window.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {
      if (this.state !== 'title') return;
      this.state = 'flyin';
      this.flyT = 0;
      AudioFX.start();
      Input.enabled = true;
      Input.lock();
      elGet('titleScreen').classList.add('gone');
    });
  },

  // ---------- damage ----------
  damagePlayer(amount, fx, fz) {
    if (this.mode === 'dead' || this.state !== 'play') return;
    if (this.mode === 'drive' && amount < 100) amount *= 0.5;   // the car soaks small-arms fire
    this.health -= amount;
    this.damageFlash = Math.min(1, this.damageFlash + 0.55);
    this.regenT = 4.5;
    Shake.add(0.18);
    if (this.health <= 0) this.die();
  },
  explosionNearPlayer(x, y, z, r, maxDmg) {
    const d = Math.hypot(this.px - x, this.pz - z, (this.py + 1) - y);
    if (d > r) return;
    const f = 1 - d / r;
    // a single blast can hurt badly but never one-shots from full health,
    // and back-to-back blasts (chain explosions) hit at quarter strength
    let dmg = Math.min(maxDmg * f, 68);
    if (this.expProtT > 0) dmg *= 0.25;
    else this.expProtT = 1.2;
    this.damagePlayer(dmg, x, z);
    if (this.mode === 'foot' && f > 0.2 && this.health > 0) {
      const inv = d > 0.5 ? 1 / d : 1;
      this.startRagdoll((this.px - x) * inv * f * 14, f * 9, (this.pz - z) * inv * f * 14);
    }
  },
  startRagdoll(vx, vyy, vz) {
    if (this.mode !== 'foot') return;
    this.mode = 'ragdoll';
    this.ragT = 0;
    startRagdoll(this.player, vx, vyy, vz, 1);
  },
  die() {
    if (this.mode === 'dead') return;
    if (this.veh) this.exitVehicle(true);
    this.health = 0;
    this.mode = 'dead';
    this.deathT = 0;
    SlowMo.trigger(0.35, 1.6);
    if (!this.player.rag.on) startRagdoll(this.player, rnd(-2, 2), 3, rnd(-2, 2), 1);
  },
  respawn() {
    this.px = HOSPITAL_SPAWN.x; this.py = 0; this.pz = HOSPITAL_SPAWN.z;
    this.vy = 0; this.velX = 0; this.velZ = 0;
    this.health = 100;
    this.mode = 'foot';
    this.heading = this.yaw = Math.PI;
    this.pitch = 0.1;
    this.player.rag.on = false;
    this.player.group.visible = true;
    this.player.group.quaternion.identity();
    this.player.group.position.set(this.px, 0, this.pz);
    Wanted.clearAll();
    Wanted.update(0, this.px, this.pz);
    this.camPos.set(this.px - Math.sin(this.yaw) * 5, 2.5, this.pz - Math.cos(this.yaw) * 5);
  },
  onVehicleExploded(v) {
    const wasMine = this.veh === v;
    if (wasMine) {
      // detach BEFORE lethal damage so die() never routes through exitVehicle
      // (which would resurrect the dead hulk to 'free' and leak its pool slot)
      this.veh = null;
      Vehicles.playerVeh = null;
      this.mode = 'foot';
      this.px = v.x; this.pz = v.z; this.py = v.y + 0.5;
      this.player.group.visible = true;
      AudioFX.engineUpdate(false, 0, 0);
    }
    // brutal but survivable from decent health — blown clear of the wreck instead of vaporized
    this.damagePlayer(wasMine ? 72 : 50, v.x, v.z);
    this.expProtT = Math.max(this.expProtT, 1.2);
    if (wasMine && this.health > 0) this.startRagdoll(rnd(-6, 6), 7, rnd(-6, 6));
  },
  onHeliDown() {
    this.heliDownT = 2.6;
  },

  // ---------- vehicles ----------
  enterVehicle(v) {
    if (v.state === 'dead') return;
    const wasState = v.state;
    v.state = 'player';
    v.cop = null;
    v.sirenOn = false;
    v.inThrottle = 0; v.inBrake = 0; v.inHand = false;
    Vehicles.playerVeh = v;
    this.veh = v;
    this.mode = 'drive';
    this.player.group.visible = false;
    this.driveYaw = v.heading;
    this.driveOrbit = 0;
    if (!v.wasStolen && wasState !== 'free') {
      v.wasStolen = true;
      Wanted.crime(v.isPolice ? 'carTheftPolice' : 'carTheft', null);
      Peds.alarm(v.x, v.z, 18);
    }
    AudioFX.click(500, 0.07);
  },
  exitVehicle(forced) {
    const v = this.veh;
    if (!v) return;
    const sp = Math.hypot(v.vx, v.vz);
    const fx = Math.sin(v.heading), fz = Math.cos(v.heading);
    // try left door, then right, then behind
    let ex = 0, ez = 0, placed = false;
    for (const side of [1.9, -1.9]) {
      ex = v.x + (-fz) * -side; ez = v.z + (fx) * -side;
      const res = Colliders.resolveCircle(ex, ez, 0.4, v.y + 0.3, v.y + 1.8);
      if (!res.hit) { placed = true; break; }
    }
    if (!placed) { ex = v.x - fx * (v.def.len / 2 + 1); ez = v.z - fz * (v.def.len / 2 + 1); }
    v.state = 'free';
    Vehicles.playerVeh = null;
    this.veh = null;
    this.px = ex; this.pz = ez;
    this.py = groundHeightAt(ex, ez);
