    this.velX = v.vx * 0.4; this.velZ = v.vz * 0.4;
    this.player.group.visible = true;
    this.player.group.position.set(this.px, this.py, this.pz);
    this.heading = v.heading;
    if (this.mode !== 'dead') this.mode = 'foot';
    AudioFX.engineUpdate(false, 0, 0);
    if (!forced && sp > 11) {
      // bailed out at speed
      this.startRagdoll(v.vx * 0.75, 3.5, v.vz * 0.75);
      this.damagePlayer(sp * 0.8, v.x, v.z);
    }
    AudioFX.click(420, 0.06);
  },

  // ---------- per-frame ----------
  update(dt) {
    if (this.state === 'title') { this.titleCam(dt); return; }
    if (this.state === 'flyin') {
      this.flyT += Tick.rawDt;
      if (this.flyT >= 1.7) {
        this.state = 'play';
        elGet('hud').classList.add('on');
      }
    }
    // ---- player control ----
    const k = Input.keys;
    if (this.mode === 'drive' && this.veh && this.veh.active && this.veh.state === 'player') {
      const v = this.veh;
      this.yaw -= Input.lookX * 0.0024;
      this.driveOrbit -= Input.lookX * 0.0024;
      this.driveOrbit *= Math.exp(-1.6 * dt);
      this.pitch = clamp(this.pitch + Input.lookY * 0.0019, -0.9, 1.2);
      const fwd = (k.KeyW ? 1 : 0), back = (k.KeyS ? 1 : 0);
      const sp = Math.hypot(v.vx, v.vz);
      const fx = Math.sin(v.heading), fz = Math.cos(v.heading);
      const movingFwd = (v.vx * fx + v.vz * fz) > 0.5;
      v.inThrottle = fwd ? 1 : (back ? (sp > 1 && movingFwd ? 0 : -1) : 0);
      v.inBrake = back && movingFwd && sp > 1 ? 1 : 0;
      v.inSteer = (k.KeyA ? 1 : 0) - (k.KeyD ? 1 : 0);
      v.inHand = !!k.Space;
      this.px = v.x; this.py = v.y + 0.6; this.pz = v.z;
      if (Input.pressed.KeyF || Input.pressed.KeyE) this.exitVehicle(false);
      AudioFX.engineUpdate(true, sp / v.def.top, v.inThrottle > 0 ? 1 : 0.12);
      if (v.inThrottle > 0 && sp < 12 && Tick.frame % 4 === 0) {
        Particles.exhaust(v.x - fx * (v.def.len / 2), v.y + 0.3, v.z - fz * (v.def.len / 2));
      }
      // headlights follow the car
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
        const h = this.headlights[i];
        const side = i ? 0.7 : -0.7;
        h.l.intensity = 160;
        h.l.position.set(v.x + fx * (v.def.len / 2) + (-fz) * side, v.y + 0.7, v.z + fz * (v.def.len / 2) + fx * side);
        h.tgt.position.set(v.x + fx * 26 + (-fz) * side, v.y - 1.5, v.z + fz * 26 + fx * side);
      }
      // splash spray in the shallows
      if (groundHeightAt(v.x, v.z) < -0.6 && sp > 6 && Tick.frame % 3 === 0) {
        Particles.splash(v.x, v.z, false);
        if (this.splashT <= 0) { AudioFX.splashSfx(v.x, v.z, false); this.splashT = 0.7; }
      }
    } else if (this.mode === 'foot') {
      AudioFX.engineUpdate(false, 0, 0);
      for (const h of this.headlights) h.l.intensity = 0;
      this.yaw -= Input.lookX * 0.0024;
      this.pitch = clamp(this.pitch + Input.lookY * 0.0019, -1.05, 1.3);
      const aiming = Input.rmb;
      this.aimBlend = damp(this.aimBlend, aiming ? 1 : 0, 14, Tick.rawDt);
      if (Input.lmb || aiming) this.fireHoldT = 0.5; else this.fireHoldT -= dt;
      // movement
      let mx = (k.KeyD ? 1 : 0) - (k.KeyA ? 1 : 0);
      let mz = (k.KeyW ? 1 : 0) - (k.KeyS ? 1 : 0);
      const ml = Math.hypot(mx, mz);
      const sprint = k.ShiftLeft || k.ShiftRight;
      const speed = aiming ? 2.6 : (sprint ? 7.0 : 4.4);
      let tx = 0, tz = 0;
      if (ml > 0) {
        mx /= ml; mz /= ml;
        const sy = Math.sin(this.yaw), cy = Math.cos(this.yaw);
        tx = (sy * mz - cy * mx) * speed;
        tz = (cy * mz + sy * mx) * speed;
      }
      this.velX = damp(this.velX, tx, 12, dt);
      this.velZ = damp(this.velZ, tz, 12, dt);
      this.px += this.velX * dt;
      this.pz += this.velZ * dt;
      // collide with world
      const res = Colliders.resolveCircle(this.px, this.pz, 0.38, this.py + 0.25, this.py + 1.65);
      this.px = res.x; this.pz = res.z;
      // collide with cars
      for (const v of Vehicles.list) {
        if (!v.active) continue;
        const dx = this.px - v.x, dz = this.pz - v.z;
        if (Math.abs(dx) > 5 || Math.abs(dz) > 5) continue;
        if (Math.abs(this.py - v.y) > 2) continue;
        const fx2 = Math.sin(v.heading), fz2 = Math.cos(v.heading);
        const lon = dx * fx2 + dz * fz2, lat = dx * -fz2 + dz * fx2;
        const hw = v.def.wid / 2 + 0.36, hl = v.def.len / 2 + 0.36;
        if (Math.abs(lon) < hl && Math.abs(lat) < hw) {
          const sp = Math.hypot(v.vx, v.vz);
          if (sp > 7) {
            this.damagePlayer(sp * 1.0, v.x, v.z);
            if (this.health > 0) this.startRagdoll(v.vx * 1.05, sp * 0.36, v.vz * 1.05);
            else { startRagdoll(this.player, v.vx * 1.05, sp * 0.36, v.vz * 1.05, 1); }
            break;
          }
          // push out along smaller axis
          if (hl - Math.abs(lon) < hw - Math.abs(lat)) {
            const push = (hl - Math.abs(lon)) * Math.sign(lon || 1);
            this.px += fx2 * push; this.pz += fz2 * push;
          } else {
            const push = (hw - Math.abs(lat)) * Math.sign(lat || 1);
            this.px += -fz2 * push; this.pz += fx2 * push;
          }
        }
      }
      // gravity / jumping
      const gy = groundHeightAt(this.px, this.pz);
      const grounded = this.py <= gy + 0.05 && this.vy <= 0;
      if (grounded) {
        this.py = gy; this.vy = 0;
        if (Input.pressed.Space) { this.vy = 5.6; AudioFX.click(300, 0.03); }
      } else {
        this.vy -= 16 * dt;
        this.py += this.vy * dt;
        if (this.py < gy) { this.py = gy; this.vy = 0; }
      }
      // wading splash
      if (gy < -0.5 && ml > 0 && Tick.frame % 9 === 0) {
        Particles.splash(this.px, this.pz, false);
        if (this.splashT <= 0) { AudioFX.splashSfx(this.px, this.pz, false); this.splashT = 0.9; }
      }
      // facing + pose
      const moveSpeed = Math.hypot(this.velX, this.velZ);
      if (this.aimBlend > 0.4 || this.fireHoldT > 0) {
        this.heading = angleDamp(this.heading, this.yaw, 18, dt);
      } else if (moveSpeed > 0.6) {
        this.heading = angleDamp(this.heading, Math.atan2(this.velX, this.velZ), 12, dt);
      }
      const ch = this.player;
      ch.group.position.set(this.px, this.py, this.pz);
      ch.group.rotation.y = this.heading;
      const aimMode = this.aimBlend > 0.4 || this.fireHoldT > 0;
      ch.anim.mode = aimMode ? (moveSpeed > 0.6 ? 'aimwalk' : 'aim')
        : moveSpeed > 5.2 ? 'run' : moveSpeed > 0.6 ? 'walk' : 'idle';
      ch.anim.aimPitch = this.pitch;
      ch.anim.speed = moveSpeed;
      poseCharacter(ch, dt, moveSpeed);
      // weapons
      if (Input.pressed.Digit1) Weapons.select('pistol');
      if (Input.pressed.Digit2) Weapons.select('smg');
      if (Input.pressed.Digit3) Weapons.select('rocket');
      if (Input.pressed.KeyR) Weapons.startReload();
      if (Input.wheel !== 0) Weapons.cycle(Input.wheel > 0 ? 1 : -1);
      if (Weapons.cur !== this.player.gunKind) charShowGun(this.player, Weapons.cur);
      if (Input.lmb && this.state === 'play') {
        ch.group.updateMatrixWorld(true);
        _v5.setFromMatrixPosition(ch.mount.matrixWorld);
        camera.getWorldDirection(_v6);
        const camToP = Math.hypot(this.px - camera.position.x, this.pz - camera.position.z);
        const ox = camera.position.x + _v6.x * camToP;
        const oy = camera.position.y + _v6.y * camToP;
        const oz = camera.position.z + _v6.z * camToP;
        Weapons.tryFire(_v5, ox, oy, oz, _v6.x, _v6.y, _v6.z, !Input.lmbPressed);
      }
      // enter vehicle
      if (Input.pressed.KeyF || Input.pressed.KeyE) {
        const v = Vehicles.nearestEnterable(this.px, this.pz, 4.0);
        if (v && Math.abs(v.y - this.py) < 3) this.enterVehicle(v);
      }
    } else if (this.mode === 'ragdoll') {
      this.ragT += dt;
      updateRagdoll(this.player, dt);
      this.px = this.player.group.position.x;
      this.py = this.player.group.position.y;
      this.pz = this.player.group.position.z;
      if (this.ragT > 2.1 && this.player.rag.settled) {
        this.mode = 'foot';
        this.player.rag.on = false;
        this.player.group.quaternion.identity();
        this.py = groundHeightAt(this.px, this.pz);
        this.player.group.position.y = this.py;
      }
    } else if (this.mode === 'dead') {
      this.deathT += Tick.rawDt;
      if (this.player.rag.on) updateRagdoll(this.player, dt);
      if (this.deathT < 1.4) this.flash = Math.min(1, this.flash + Tick.rawDt * 0.9);
      else if (this.deathT < 1.6) { /* hold white */ }
      else if (this.deathT < 1.7) { this.respawn(); }
      void 0;
    }
    if (this.mode !== 'dead' && this.flash > 0) this.flash = Math.max(0, this.flash - Tick.rawDt * 0.7);
    this.splashT -= dt;
    // health regen
    if (this.health > 0 && this.health < 100) {
      this.regenT -= dt;
      if (this.regenT <= 0) this.health = Math.min(100, this.health + 14 * dt);
    }
    this.damageFlash = Math.max(0, this.damageFlash - dt * 1.8);
    this.expProtT = Math.max(0, this.expProtT - dt);
    // climax title card
    if (this.heliDownT > 0) {
      this.heliDownT -= dt;
      if (this.heliDownT <= 0) { HUD.showTitleCard(); this.heliDownT = -1; }
    }
    // hero car idle shimmer
    const hero = Vehicles.heroSports;
    if (hero && hero.state === 'parked' && Tick.frame % 7 === 0) {
      const hfx = Math.sin(hero.heading), hfz = Math.cos(hero.heading);
      Particles.exhaust(hero.x - hfx * 2.2, hero.y + 0.3, hero.z - hfz * 2.2);
    }
  },

  // ---------- cameras ----------
  titleCam(dt) {
    const t = Tick.t * 0.018;
    const sx = 60 + Math.sin(t) * 130;
    this.camPos.set(sx, 11 + Math.sin(t * 2.1) * 2, 322);
    camera.position.copy(this.camPos);
    camera.lookAt(120 + Math.sin(t * 0.7) * 60, 26, 0);
    camera.fov = 58;
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  },
  updateCamera(dt) {
    if (this.state === 'title') return;
    let desX, desY, desZ, lookX, lookY, lookZ, wantFov;
    if (this.mode === 'drive' && this.veh) {
      const v = this.veh;
      const sp = Math.hypot(v.vx, v.vz);
      // follow velocity direction (reads drifts), fall back to heading at low speed
      let followYaw = v.heading;
      if (sp > 4) {
        const velYaw = Math.atan2(v.vx, v.vz);
        const fwdness = Math.cos(wrapAngle(velYaw - v.heading));
        if (fwdness > 0) followYaw = velYaw;
      }
      this.driveYaw = angleDamp(this.driveYaw, followYaw + this.driveOrbit, 3.6, Tick.rawDt);
      const dist = 6.6 + sp * 0.055;
      const h = 2.7 + sp * 0.012;
      desX = v.x - Math.sin(this.driveYaw) * dist;
      desZ = v.z - Math.cos(this.driveYaw) * dist;
      desY = v.y + h;
      lookX = v.x + Math.sin(this.driveYaw) * 6;
      lookY = v.y + 1.25;
      lookZ = v.z + Math.cos(this.driveYaw) * 6;
      wantFov = 60 + 13 * sstep(0, 1, clamp((sp - 13) / 30, 0, 1));
    } else {
      const aim = this.aimBlend;
      const dist = lerp(4.3, 2.15, aim);
      const shoulder = lerp(0.5, 0.78, aim);
      const pivotY = this.py + 1.62;
      const sy = Math.sin(this.yaw), cy = Math.cos(this.yaw);
      const fx = sy * Math.cos(this.pitch), fz = cy * Math.cos(this.pitch);
      const fy = -Math.sin(this.pitch);
      const rx = -cy, rz = sy;
      const pivX = this.px + rx * shoulder, pivZ = this.pz + rz * shoulder;
      desX = pivX - fx * dist;
      desY = pivotY - fy * dist + 0.18;
      desZ = pivZ - fz * dist;
      lookX = pivX + fx * 8;
      lookY = pivotY + fy * 8;
      lookZ = pivZ + fz * 8;
      wantFov = lerp(62, 50, aim);
    }
    // camera collision: ray from look pivot to desired position
    {
      const ax = lookX !== undefined ? (this.mode === 'drive' ? this.px : this.px) : this.px;
      const ay = this.py + 1.6, az = this.pz;
      let dx = desX - ax, dy = desY - ay, dz = desZ - az;
      const dl = Math.hypot(dx, dy, dz) || 1;
      dx /= dl; dy /= dl; dz /= dl;
      const hit = Colliders.raycast(ax, ay, az, dx, dy, dz, dl + 0.3);
      if (hit >= 0 && hit < dl) {
        const hd = Math.max(0.5, hit - 0.32);
        desX = ax + dx * hd; desY = ay + dy * hd; desZ = az + dz * hd;
      }
      const gy = groundHeightAt(desX, desZ);
      if (desY < gy + 0.4) desY = gy + 0.4;
    }
    if (this.state === 'flyin') {
      const t = sstep(0, 1, clamp(this.flyT / 1.7, 0, 1));
      this.camPos.x = lerp(this.camPos.x, desX, t * t);
      this.camPos.y = lerp(this.camPos.y, desY, t * t);
      this.camPos.z = lerp(this.camPos.z, desZ, t * t);
    } else {
      const rate = this.mode === 'drive' ? 7 : 26;
      this.camPos.x = damp(this.camPos.x, desX, rate, Tick.rawDt);
      this.camPos.y = damp(this.camPos.y, desY, rate, Tick.rawDt);
      this.camPos.z = damp(this.camPos.z, desZ, rate, Tick.rawDt);
    }
    // speed shake while driving
    if (this.mode === 'drive' && this.veh) {
      const sp = Math.hypot(this.veh.vx, this.veh.vz);
      Shake.add(clamp((sp - 30) * 0.0012, 0, 0.03) * 60 * Tick.rawDt);
    }
    Shake.sample(_v3, _v4);
    camera.position.set(this.camPos.x + _v3.x, this.camPos.y + _v3.y, this.camPos.z + _v3.z);
    this.camTgt.set(lookX, lookY, lookZ);
    camera.lookAt(this.camTgt);
    camera.rotation.x += _v4.x;
    camera.rotation.y += _v4.y;
    camera.rotation.z += _v4.z;
    this.fov = damp(this.fov, wantFov, 5, Tick.rawDt);
    camera.fov = this.fov;
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  },
};

// ============================================================ boot + main loop
Sky.init();
Ocean.init();
buildCity();
Particles.init();
Tracers.init();
Casings.init();
Debris.init();
Rockets.init();
FX.init();
Vehicles.init();
Vehicles.spawnParked();
Peds.init();
Police.init();
Pickups.init();
HUD.init();
Game.init();

// hidden test hooks (not visible anywhere in the UI)
window.NT = {
  Game, Vehicles, Police, Wanted, Tick, renderer, Weapons, Quality, Input, Pickups, Rockets, Combat,
  start() {
    if (Game.state === 'title') {
      Game.state = 'flyin'; Game.flyT = 0;
      AudioFX.start(); Input.enabled = true;
      elGet('titleScreen').classList.add('gone');
    }
  },
  teleport(x, z) { Game.px = x; Game.pz = z; Game.py = groundHeightAt(x, z); Game.player.group.position.set(x, Game.py, z); },
  setHeat(h) { Wanted.heat = h; },
  key(code, down) { Input.keys[code] = down; if (down) Input.pressed[code] = true; },
  look(dx, dy) { Input.lookX += dx; Input.lookY += dy; },
  info() {
    return {
      state: Game.state, mode: Game.mode, hp: Game.health,
      pos: [Game.px.toFixed(1), Game.py.toFixed(1), Game.pz.toFixed(1)],
      stars: Wanted.stars, heat: Wanted.heat,
      calls: renderer.info.render.calls, tris: renderer.info.render.triangles,
      ms: Quality.ema.toFixed(2), scale: Quality.scale,
      traffic: Vehicles.trafficCount, heli: Police.heli.alive,
    };
  },
};

let _lastNow = performance.now();
function frame(now) {
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  const rawMs = now - _lastNow;
  _lastNow = now;
  const rawDt = clamp(rawMs / 1000, 0.0005, 0.05);
  SlowMo.update(rawDt);
  const dt = rawDt * Tick.scale;
  Tick.rawDt = rawDt;
  Tick.dt = dt;
  Tick.t += dt;
  Tick.frame++;

  Game.update(dt);
  if (Game.state !== 'title') {
    Police.update(dt, Game.px, Game.py, Game.pz,
      Game.veh ? Game.veh.vx : Game.velX, Game.veh ? Game.veh.vz : Game.velZ,
      Game.mode === 'drive',
      Game.veh ? Math.hypot(Game.veh.vx, Game.veh.vz) : Math.hypot(Game.velX, Game.velZ));
    Wanted.update(dt, Game.px, Game.pz);
  }
  Vehicles.update(dt, Game.px, Game.pz);
  Peds.update(dt, Game.px, Game.pz);
  Weapons.update(dt);
  Rockets.update(dt);
  Tracers.update(dt);
  Casings.update(dt);
  Debris.update(dt);
  Particles.update(dt);
  FX.update(dt);
  Pickups.update(dt, Game.px, Game.py, Game.pz);
  Sky.update(dt);
  Ocean.update();
  CityFX.update();
  if (CityFX.frondMat && CityFX.frondMat.userData.shader) CityFX.frondMat.userData.shader.uniforms.uTime.value = Tick.t;
  Sky.updateSun(Game.px, Game.pz);
  Shake.update(rawDt);
  Game.updateCamera(dt);
  AudioFX.update();

  // HUD
  if (Game.state === 'play') {
    if (Tick.frame % 2 === 0) HUD.drawMap(Game.px, Game.pz, Game.mode === 'drive' && Game.veh ? Game.veh.heading : Game.yaw, Game.health / 100);
    HUD.update(rawDt);
    HUD.crosshair(Game.mode === 'foot', Weapons.spreadHeat + (Game.aimBlend > 0.5 ? 0 : 0.3));
  }
  gradePass.uniforms.uTime.value = Tick.t;
  gradePass.uniforms.uFlash.value = Game.flash;
  gradePass.uniforms.uDamage.value = Game.damageFlash * (0.6 + 0.4 * Math.sin(Tick.t * 9));
  composer.render();
  Quality.update(rawMs, performance.now() - now, rawDt);
  Input.endFrame();
}
requestAnimationFrame(frame);
requestAnimationFrame(() => { elGet('fade').classList.add('clear'); });
</script>
</body>
</html>
